#101 Xu Shilun's Adventures in Ultra Civilization.

Chapter 101 Xu Shilun’s Adventures in Ultra Civilization.

So, at present, Xu Shilun was not far from the Ultraman Civilization.

Looking at the planet in the distance, Xu Shilun began to make his plans.

His own strength was only at the peak of Tier 2, not even reaching perfection.

But with Da Boluo's Tier 3 strength, striking a blow against the Ultraman Civilization shouldn't be a big problem.

Moreover, his Dark energy originally came from the Ultraman Civilization, and he could even use this Dark energy to access the Ultraman Civilization's mental network and directly corrupt them.

What if they had already reached Tier 3?

Although he hadn't reached Tier 3, with Da Boluo's power, he could still unleash Tier 3 combat strength.

Then, relying on Dark energy to corrupt the Ultraman Civilization, he would fight to grow stronger, becoming more powerful with each battle.

After that, he would completely dominate the Ultraman Civilization.

Crippling the Ultraman, suppressing their technological development, and unifying the Ashura—his mission to unify the three races would then be complete.

At that time, he would know what the reward for completing the mission was.

If he could go back, that would be for the best, but even if he couldn't, he would continue to suppress them, not allowing their strength to break through.

Only then could he possibly live the longest.

Xu Shilun's ultimate goal was never the so-called unification of the three races, but to survive.

Only by surviving could one talk about the future; the dead had no right to discuss anything.

Everyone probably thinks I'm dead now, so when I suddenly appear, it will definitely surprise everyone, right?

The plan was simple and perfect.

After finalizing his plan, Xu Shilun immediately set off towards the Ultraman Planet.

When he stealthily arrived next to the moon, he hid himself.

Then, he dispersed three hundred Dark Ultraman, who began to approach the Ultraman Civilization's planet.

They remained invisible, cautiously and continuously approaching the edge of the Ultraman Civilization's planet.

As long as these Dark Ultraman entered the Ultraman Civilization and merged with the bodies of those Ultraman, then through their consciousness links, they would spread the darkness.

This darkness would spread rapidly like a virus.

At that time, with the Ultraman Civilization in chaos, he would step in, reap a harvest, and instantly break through to Tier 3!

Just as Xu Shilun was thinking this, his perfect plan encountered its first obstacle.

The three hundred Dark Ultraman were blocked by the Mental Web outside the Ultraman Civilization's atmosphere!

Seeing this, Xu Shilun immediately knew something was wrong, and before he could think further, a sharp, urgent alarm sounded instantly.

This scene completely bewildered Xu Shilun.

No!

By now, in everyone's perception, I should be dead, and the Soul Clan has already been wiped out!

Why are you still setting up this Mental Web?!

Who the hell are you guarding against?!

I was blown up by Dark Tiga; everyone saw it.

You were the ones who led the extermination of the Soul Clan; the planet was even blown up!

Now you're still wearing a turtle shell Mental Web!!!

You guys clearly don't consider me dead, and you don't consider me human at all!

Xu Shilun, seeing that things were not right, was about to make a quick escape when, the next moment, a terrifying white light illuminated everything around the Ultraman Civilization.

Xu Shilun and the three hundred Dark Ultraman were clearly exposed in front of everyone.

Subsequently, over six hundred Ultraman warriors flew out, rapidly heading towards Xu Shilun's direction.

Before the figures even arrived, six hundred terrifying energy light pillars had already shot out first.

The three hundred Dark Ultraman instantly turned to dust at the first encounter.

The light pillars, after eliminating the Dark Ultraman, continued their momentum, directly striking towards Xu Shilun's direction.

They had no qualms about the moon where Xu Shilun was hiding.

With their current technology, the existence or non-existence of the moon had almost no impact on them; at most, it was just aesthetically pleasing.

If they truly wanted to, they could now create a moon with their bare hands.

Therefore, the terrifying energy light pillars directly intended to destroy Xu Shilun and the moon where he was hiding together.

Seeing this, Xu Shilun did not hesitate in the slightest, immediately using Da Boluo's power, first dodging, then creating a protective shield in front of himself, and finally, also firing a dark energy ray.

The classic 'energy clash' scene appeared.

But unfortunately, Xu Shilun's position was wrong.

And the opponent clearly had more numbers.

In just one encounter, the Dark energy formed by Da Boluo's power was destroyed by the six hundred light pillars.

Xu Shilun's expression changed drastically, and he turned to run without hesitation.

"Xu Shilun, you can't escape!"

Just as Xu Shilun made his move, over a hundred more Giants of Light appeared in the distance.

In the starry sky, a huge screen emerged, with Liu Dajun's figure prominently displayed on it.

The purpose of doing this was nothing else but to distract Xu Shilun.

Xu Shilun might play dirty, but Liu Dajun had the computational power of the entire Ultraman Civilization backing him; if he couldn't scheme Xu Shilun to death, then everyone in the entire Ultraman Civilization might as well die.

【Boom! Boom! Boom!】

Xu Shilun desperately dodged a round of attacks, but he was still hit by dozens of terrifying abilities, relying entirely on the protective shield formed by Da Boluo's power; otherwise, with his current strength, he probably wouldn't have been able to withstand even one hit.

He cursed inwardly, calling Liu Dajun an old schemer.

At the same time, he was also amazed at how fast the Ultraman Civilization's technology had developed.

If they were truly allowed to continue developing like this, the Evil God would probably truly descend before long; no matter what, he had to try every possible way to stop him.

Thinking of this, a clean smile appeared on Xu Shilun's gaunt face as he spoke to the giant screen in front of him:

"General Jun, what are you doing?!"

"What am I doing? Isn't it obvious? I'm going to deal with you!"

Xu Shilun: "No, we're Old fellows, there's no need for this, General Jun. Is there some misunderstanding between us? I've always respected you and always called you General Jun."

"Heh heh, yes, 'General Jun' with every word, and a city with every punch, right?"

【Boom! Boom! Boom!~】

Terrifying energy rays continued to attack Xu Shilun's direction relentlessly.

Xu Shilun tried to escape several times, but each time he was forced back.

As time passed, the Giants of Light began to spread out and encircle him, firmly trapping him in the center, making escape impossible.

"General Jun, can't we just talk things over? There's no need for all this fighting and killing."

【Boom!】

Another ability struck him, and then dozens more attacks nearly hit him.

He was almost riddled with holes.

After narrowly dodging an attack once more, Xu Shilun spoke again:

"General Jun, I came to find you this time because I truly have something to say. Can't you just make them stop attacking first?"

"It's fine, you say what you need to say, they'll keep fighting; it won't interfere."

"I'll f***ing kill you, Liu Dajun! Don't you f***ing force me!"

"Oh, and then what? Are you done talking? If you're done, I'll send you on your way. It's just a pity that Old fellow Wang Jianzhi isn't here, otherwise, he really wanted to flay you alive and pull out your tendons."

"No, wait, who is Wang Jianzhi?"

"Heh, you're acting very well. You just went to Ashura and massacred them, and now you have the nerve to ask me who Wang Jianzhi is. But it doesn't matter if you don't admit it, because I never expected you to."

【Boom! Boom! Boom!】

The encirclement grew smaller and smaller, and Xu Shilun's dodging space also became smaller and smaller. Several times, he was hit by dozens of energy light pillars, bouncing around like a rubber ball.

Despite this, Xu Shilun's consciousness remained very clear.

It must be said that Da Boluo was truly strong; one or two energy light pillars couldn't break his defense.

Even if they did break through, he could instantly recover the next moment.

"General Jun, killing me will do you no good and will only accelerate the demise of the Ultraman Civilization!"

"Oh, go on, I'm listening."

"I'll f***ing kill you, Liu Dajun!"

"Still have the strength to curse? Pass it on, tell them to increase the firepower."

【Boom! Boom! Boom!~】

"I'll f*** you, Liu Dajun, you f***ing..."

【Boom! Boom!~】

Xu Shilun was sent flying everywhere like a rubber ball, finally being knocked back to his original position.

"General Jun, can you at least let me die knowing why!"

"Oh, you don't understand? If you don't understand, then forget it. Continue the attack."

"Wait, General Jun, I haven't streamed in a long time. Although I know I'm not a good person, can you let me start a live stream to say goodbye to my family?"

"I remember them saying you were an orphan."

"I'll f***ing... General Jun, look at my current situation, I can't escape anyway. Can't you just let me say a few words? As the saying goes, 'when a man is about to die, his words are kind.' I... I'll f***ing..."

【Boom! Boom! Boom!~】

Xu Shilun, who was halfway through his sentence, was sent flying again.

"You're not kind at all. If you had done anything decent, I would have let you finish your words, at least leave a last will. But unfortunately, you don't do anything decent.

The Soul Clan was ruined by you, and then you went to ruin the Ashura. You, no, you don't even deserve to be called a human!"

【Boom! Boom!~】

While being knocked around, Xu Shilun glanced towards the Ultraman Planet and the six hundred Giants of Light that were closing in.

He constantly pondered his next moves while also considering what Liu Dajun had said.

He could guarantee that he had truly been diligently collecting Dark energy in the asteroid field all this time.

In fact, since coming to this world, he had never even visited the Ashura's planet.

Although he had wanted to go there several times, he was afraid that Da Boluo would cause trouble, so he never went.

After figuring this out, he realized that someone was causing trouble under his name.

It seemed that going offline wasn't such a good choice after all.

No, he had to ask for clarification.

However, given Liu Dajun's current demeanor, it was impossible for him to tell him.

His goal was to distract him, so instead of asking him, it would be better to ask his friends in the live stream.

Thinking of this, he opened his live stream, which he hadn't done in a long time.

As soon as the live stream lit up, a bullet comment immediately popped up.

【Xiao Pang Shu: Lun Ge, I'm Wolong! You're finally online! The people who said they were bringing warmth to my doorstep are now smashing the door down! Lun Ge, save me!!!】

【Yu Jiemo: Lun Ge, I'm Phoenix Fledgling! Someone's smashing my door down too! What should I do!!!】

Xu Shilun: Emmm...

~

Ending: Xu Shilun has something to say:

【Xu Shilun: Pain, so much pain! Today I was tragically surrounded, and I never thought my two great strategists would also be in such a state. Is it truly Heaven's will to destroy me!!!】


#102 The Dark Ages begin!

Chapter 102 The Dark Age Begins!

Yu Jiemo: It’s quite normal for people to die on your side, isn’t it?

Xiao Pang Shu: Yes, wait, no, Lun Ge, what exactly are you so conflicted about? Why would you go to the Ultraman Civilization to die?!

Six, six, six, what a perfect trap.

Did he do too many bad things and finally couldn't bear the condemnation of his conscience?

Lun Ge, don't panic. In this situation, in this situation, you just need to stand still and shout three times, 'I am the King of the Soul Clan! Who wants to kill me! Who dares to kill me! Who can kill me!'

Remember to strike a domineering pose when you shout, then you can restart.

What the heck is 'restart'? Do you think this is a game?!

Xiao Pang Shu: Lun Ge, it's not that we don't want to help you, but in your situation, you're surrounded. What can we do? And I'm in trouble myself right now!

Yu Jiemo: Lun Ge, so why did you turn off your live stream back then? You turned it off, and then you went to the Ashura side to cause trouble. Everyone has heard about it. Now both sides are looking for you, and this makes things very difficult for us.

A strategist isn't omnipotent!

Xu Shilun was hit by dozens of attacks again, but at that instant, he grasped the key point and quickly asked, "You said I went to Ashura! But I've been cultivating in the starry sky, I didn't go at all! Damn it... crap... crap."

Before he could finish speaking, Xu Shilun was sent flying again.

But at this moment, he shouted to Liu Dajun:

"General Jun, there's a problem! It wasn't me who went to the Ashura Civilization, it really wasn't me! Someone is impersonating me!"

Liu Dajun: "Oh, keep making excuses, I'm listening."

"Damn it, don't you even think about it? If it wasn't me who did this, then who else could it be?!"

Liu Dajun: "Yes, who else could it be if it wasn't you?"

The same words, different tones, spoken by two people with different stances, completely changed their meaning.

"Someone is impersonating me!!!"

"Oh, then that person is truly despicable, to even impersonate you."

"Liu Dajun, you old bastard! Are you even listening to what I'm saying?!"

"You speak, I'm listening. By the way, tell them to increase their firepower. They've been fighting for so long, and this guy is still full of life. It's a bit uncomfortable to watch."

Liu Dajun said this to deliberately provoke Xu Shilun.

Not to mention that all their minds were linked, the giant screen in the starry sky was also visible to everyone.

"Liu Dajun, don't push me!"

"I'm not pushing you, you came here yourself."

Xu Shilun glanced at the Giant of Light that had completely surrounded him, and immediately roared:

"Damn it, I'm going to blow up with you!"

After speaking, black energy surged around Xu Shilun's body, and in the next moment, it exploded violently.

Terrifying black energy spread in all directions, and instantly, everyone lost their vision.

Upon seeing this, Liu Dajun did not hesitate and immediately ordered: "Activate defensive mode, direct fire coverage!"

As soon as the black mist touched the Giant of Light, it was blocked by a protective shield.

Subsequently, a dense round of firepower covered the area.

"Damn you, Liu Dajun, I'll remember you!"

Within the black mist, a phantom figure suddenly darted out.

But his direction was not to escape, but to directly crash into the Ultraman Planet.

"Activate planet defense, activate all firepower, eliminate him!"

Xu Shilun flew rapidly towards the Ultraman Planet, ignoring the terrifying energy light beams attacking him. He simply condensed a protective shield behind himself using Da Boluo energy.

Terrifying attacks struck the protective shield one after another, shattering it in an instant. Xu Shilun's figure also rapidly approached the Ultraman Planet under the impact of this terrifying energy.

Another light beam passed through his body, and his soul body instantly became even more ethereal, even appearing somewhat transparent.

But even in this situation, he was still charging towards the Ultraman Civilization.

Given the current situation, escape was simply impossible.

Since there was no retreat, then there was only one option: to fight to the death!

Xu Shilun was ruthless to others, and even more ruthless to himself!

Today, he would either die here, or he would usher in the Ultraman Dark Era!

Boom boom boom!

Attacks passed through Xu Shilun's body one after another. It was only because he currently possessed the strength of a second-order peak and had the big pineapple fragments as support; otherwise, he would have died long ago.

After being hit seven or eight times consecutively, the already short distance, accelerated by those energy attacks, meant he reached the outer defenses of the Ultraman Civilization in a blink of an eye.

At this moment, Xu Shilun was so weak that he couldn't even control the power of Da Boluo.

He was merely flying forward, carried by the force of the attacks.

But he still had his last trump card, and that was Da Boluo!

"Da Boluo, break this turtle shell for me!"

A figure with four arms suddenly appeared behind Xu Shilun. Two arms went backward, blocking all attacks.

The other two arms were raised high and then slammed downwards.

Boom!

The Ultraman Civilization's psychic defense system shattered for the second time under the power of Da Boluo.

But after doing all this, Da Boluo returned to Xu Shilun's body.

Because if he didn't go back, Xu Shilun would truly die.

Before, he had barely survived by relying on Da Boluo's power. When he tried to control the power himself, he really almost kicked the bucket.

Da Boluo here actually approved of Xu Shilun.

Although he had never actively observed Xu Shilun's thoughts, according to his promise.

But he could also see from Xu Shilun's actions that although he committed all sorts of evil and killed without batting an eye, his goal from beginning to end was to keep himself alive and prevent the evil god from appearing.

Therefore, even if Xu Shilun destroyed his fragments, he said nothing, even lending him power.

The most important point was that Xu Shilun had never harmed the Ashura Civilization from beginning to end!

This was the main reason he was willing to help him.

Although he knew Xu Shilun had his own ulterior motives and that he was unwilling to take him to the Ashura Civilization.

But as long as the Ashura Civilization still existed, that was fine.

He believed that as long as this guy truly achieved his goal, truly unified the three races, and truly prevented the evil god's descent, he would eventually return to Ashura.

Therefore, he was never anxious and never urged.

Although to be honest, sometimes Da Boluo truly looked down on Xu Shilun's actions from the bottom of his heart.

Because he was despicable, broke promises, deceived, and even massacred innocents.

Whether as Da Boluo or as the Ashura King, he looked down on Xu Shilun.

However, if one were to put themselves in Xu Shilun's shoes, then one would feel that he had already done his best.

Because, like him, Xu Shilun had no choice from beginning to end.

Xu Shilun gave a weak laugh and said, "Thanks."

Then his body was pulled by gravity and plummeted downwards.

At this moment, on the ground, two extreme beams of light erupted, aiming directly at Xu Shilun.

"Everyone, follow me and usher in the Dark Era! Explode!"

Boom!

The instant the light was about to touch Xu Shilun, his body violently exploded, transforming into countless black mists.

At this moment, he completely abandoned his physical body.

The power of darkness furiously attacked the citizen consciousness network; this was his last retreat.

At this moment, the big pineapple fragments that had accompanied him for over twenty years were also completely abandoned by him.

This time, it's either a King's Return!

Or, the return of the deceased!

Terrifying white light, carrying immense energy, blasted a large amount of black mist out of the Ultraman Planet.

But the remaining mist directly connected into the consciousness network space.

His live stream went black once again.

An Ultraman citizen, who was watching the battle, originally had only fairness, justice, love, and peace in his heart.

In the next moment, a surge of darkness poured in, and endless dark thoughts, evil consciousness, and despairing consciousness enveloped him.

One, two, three, a hundred, a thousand, ten thousand...

Darkness spread rapidly.

At this moment, Xu Shilun's consciousness was like a ghost walking within the network, and like a virus, it began to rapidly spread darkness.

Liu Dajun looked at the big pineapple fragments that had been blown out of Xu Shilun's body and remained silent for a long time: "Is he dead?"

"Scanning...

Xu Shilun has no soul body organization.

Xu Shilun has no soul body abilities.

Xu Shilun has no dark energy.

Tentatively, deceased."

Although from the results, Xu Shilun seemed to have really died.

But he still felt a little uneasy.

Especially what Xu Shilun said earlier, that he had never been to Ashura.

Although he had been mocking him at the time, he had not completely dismissed it.

If that incident wasn't done by Xu Shilun, then who was it?

This was a very serious problem.

But if it really was Xu Shilun who did it, then why would Da Boluo help him just now?

Although there were still some doubts in his mind, he still said, "Conduct a thorough check, don't miss a single detail."

After Liu Dajun finished speaking, he was about to turn and leave when he heard an exclamation from over there: "Leader, there's a problem with the psychic space!"

Upon hearing this, Liu Dajun's face suddenly turned pale.

The psychic space was the cornerstone of their Ultraman Civilization's rise, and it connected the brain consciousness of all Ultraman.

If there was a problem with the psychic space, then everyone would have a problem!

"Immediately, disconnect the psychic network!"

His order had just been given when the psychic network space immediately shut down.

But his order was still a step too late.

A Dark Tiga, thousands of meters tall, appeared above the Ultraman Planet.

His body was still expanding, and his energy was still growing stronger.

"How regrettable, you still discovered me. If you were a little later, I could have infected two-thirds of the entire Ultraman Civilization.

General Jun, I'm so sad!"

"Xu Shilun!!!"

"General Jun, look at you, getting excited again, aren't you? You weren't like this when you talked to me just now.

You didn't give me a choice before, but I'm different, I will give you a choice.

First, I stand here, and you kill me.

Second, surrender to me.

Third, die!

Make your choice, the lives of half of the Ultraman Civilization are in your hands!"


#103 Xu Shilun’s roar and mission rewards!

Chapter 103 Xu Shilun’s roar and mission rewards!

Chapter 103 (I'm posting early today, praise me!!!)

Liu Dajun remained silent.

At this moment, his heart was filled with mixed emotions: anger, unwillingness, fear, resentment, various feelings constantly intertwined.

But in the end, only a bitter taste lingered in his mouth, unable to dissipate for a long time.

He knew he had lost.

From the moment Xu Shilun appeared before him in the form of Dark Tiga, he had already lost.

He wanted to roar in anger, to denounce Xu Shilun's inhumane actions.

But he couldn't bring himself to say it.

Hadn't this guy done enough despicable things already?

What good would cursing him do?

Haha, it truly seems that Heaven wants to destroy the Ultraman Clan.

Seeing Liu Dajun remain silent for a long time, Xu Shilun spoke again: "Your time is limited. I hope you can make a choice quickly, and make a choice that satisfies everyone.

Currently, I hold the lives of half of the Ultraman people, and so do you.

Either you choose to kill half of my people and be buried with me.

Of course, I will also choose to resist. As for the outcome, whether you win or I win, or if we both suffer great losses and perish together, that will depend on fate.

Actually, there is no such thing as fate, and you have no chance of winning at all.

We can still use mental connection now, but can you?"

Xu Shilun's words once again struck Liu Dajun like a heavy blow.

Yes, the other party could use mental connection recklessly.

But if they dared to activate mental connection, there was no guarantee that darkness wouldn't infiltrate it, and then not only would there be no benefit, but the losses would be even greater.

So he didn't dare to gamble.

Liu Dajun continued to be silent, and Xu Shilun still spoke indifferently: "Actually, there is only one path you can choose, and that is to surrender."

"Heh, I never thought I would ultimately lose to a treacherous villain like you. Perhaps this is fate."

At this point, Xu Shilun, who had initially looked calm, suddenly became enraged:

"Fate, what a load of crap! There is no such thing as fate in this world.

It's just an excuse for you losers.

Do you think it was easy for me to get to where I am today?

Do you really think anyone can get to this point and stand before you just by luck?

You, such an arrogant person, who has been high and mighty from the start, simply don't understand my hardships.

At the beginning of your journey as a Traveler, you were far ahead from the start, and relying on memories from your previous journey, you easily led the entire Ultraman Civilization to new heights.

Even Ashura already knew his future extraordinary path.

And me? At that time, I hadn't even found where my clansmen were."

Liu Dajun sneered: "Because of this? Because of this, you massacred your own clansmen, using their suffering to enhance your strength.

Because of this, you came to our Ultraman Civilization and slaughtered wantonly.

You are simply a lunatic."

"Shut up! You don't understand at all! You understand nothing! You have always been an arrogant person, doing arrogant things with your arrogant mindset.

Do you really think leading the Ultraman Civilization to develop is a good path? No, this is a dead end!

Why did I go through so much trouble to enhance my strength with the blood and flesh of the Soul Clan? Do you really think I specifically came here to beat you up because I was bored, or because you were bored?

Having both experienced nine years of education, both growing up in the sunlight under the red flag, you can be so bright and great, while I am a rat in the gutter!

Am I really that despicable, unable to stand even a tiny bit of good for others!

Seeing you prosper, I desperately rushed over, wanting to beat you up and perish with you!

Let me tell you, I, Xu Shilun, am not that despicable!"

Liu Dajun scoffed: "Yes, you've won now. Whatever you say goes."

However, Xu Shilun, who usually enjoyed being sarcastic and mocking, was surprisingly enraged this time.

He roared towards the direction of the Ultraman Civilization's highest laboratory:

"To this day, you still haven't understood the purpose of everything I've done. You're simply a fool!

The Ultraman Civilization in your hands, even without me, would perish sooner or later.

If you had even a speck of a brain, you should have wondered why Da Boluo would help me!

From beginning to end, all you saw was my arrogant, bloodthirsty, cruel, and evil side.

But have you ever considered that my rise was ten thousand times more arduous than you can imagine?"

"At the beginning of the Soul Clan, due to one wrong thought of mine, they all transformed into my image. Subsequently, my friend used the big pineapple fragments to control them and lead them down another wrong path. The Rebellion of the Five Kings, the Yellow Turban Rebellion, I was put on the gallows, and I almost died.

To prevent you, this fool, from leading everyone to a dead end, I traveled thousands of miles to destroy the Plasma Spark Tower, but you rebuilt it.

Because the Soul Clan transformed into my image and possessed my memories, and were controlled by the big pineapple fragments, their future, their potential, was locked at that moment.

Whether for my future or to allow me to live, I would not allow them to have power surpassing mine.

And I, in order to gain even greater power, once again interrupted your foolish behavior, risking my life and almost dying at the hands of Dark Tiga.

Even now, as I stand before you again, do you think I haven't paid anything?

I abandoned the power I painstakingly acquired, I abandoned Da Boluo, and I abandoned my soul body. Even my life, at that moment, was abandoned by me.

Now, I have only a final wisp of mental consciousness left. I personally severed the path back to the real world.

After completing the mission, you might still have a chance to return to the real world, but I can only live in the spiritual world of all the dark Three-Eyed People of the Ultraman Civilization.

Only they can make me appear in this world.

Now, I am truly integrated with the Ultraman Civilization, truly achieving a state where we prosper together and suffer together.

And with me having reached this point, what right do you have to mock me!!!"

Xu Shilun's words were deafening, leaving Liu Dajun momentarily speechless.

This scene was also broadcast from Liu Dajun's live stream to the real world.

At this moment, countless viewers in Liu Dajun's live stream were also instantly speechless.

Yes, from beginning to end, all they had seen was a mad Xu Shilun.

He was mad, bloodthirsty, cruel, insidious, cunning, and could even be described as utterly evil; all the negative adjectives applicable in the current real world were displayed in him.

But from beginning to end, had they truly understood Xu Shilun?

However, at this moment, Liu Dajun sneered again: "Are you trying to whitewash yourself? There's no need at all. Even if you don't say all this, I have no choice but to submit to you now.

Because I have no other option, and I will not take the lives of half of the Ultraman Civilization's people to fight this meaningless battle with you.

So, you win."

Xu Shilun returned with a cold laugh: "Whitewashing? What a ridiculous term. I, Xu Shilun, have never thought of whitewashing myself from beginning to end.

I am insidious and cunning, I kill without mercy, I am bloodthirsty and cruel—I admit all of this.

I have never denied that I am a bad person, whether from a moral standpoint or any other, so I don't need to be whitewashed!

Because that's not my dark history; that's the path I've walked!"

Xu Shilun's words, spoken through Dark Tiga, resonated throughout the entire Ultraman Civilization, and for a moment, everything was silent.

The entire universe seemed to fall silent at this moment.

On this day, Ultraman Civilization New Era Year 54 was officially renamed Dark Era Year 54.

From then on, the Ultraman, who had once lived under the light, no longer dared to activate their mental connection.

However, the Three-Eyed People, who had already been infected by Xu Shilun, recklessly vented everything and released darkness on the mental network.

The technology of the Light faction, controlled by Liu Dajun, halted its progress.

Xu Shilun continued to firmly suppress the development of the Dark Otto faction's technology, using their entire mental network to reconstruct his own body.

Although he would perish from this world if all the Three-Eyed People died out, it did not prevent him from using dark power to build his new body.

On this day, Liu Dajun, the supreme leader of the Light Empire Ultraman Civilization, officially chose to submit to Xu Shilun.

And on this day, Xu Shilun began his dark era.

On the newly built throne in the Ultraman Civilization's highest laboratory, Xu Shilun, controlling his Dark Tiga, sat quietly above, turning his head to glance at Liu Dajun beside him:

"Connect with Wang Jianzhi. It's time to unify the three clans and complete my mission."

Liu Dajun also turned his head to look at Xu Shilun: "You've done all this just to unify the three clans?"

"Heh, you still understand nothing, not even as much as Da Boluo, who has been dead for countless years."

Da Boluo, with his broken, ethereal body and four arms, stood quietly to the side, not speaking.

In the past few days, Xu Shilun had spent some time specifically gathering Da Boluo's fragments.

Although there were only 12 pieces, this was equivalent to more than half of Da Boluo's power.

This time, Xu Shilun specifically gathered Da Boluo for no other reason than to prove in his upcoming conversation with Wang Jianzhi that he had never been to Ashura and had never harmed Ashura.

Then came his final goal: to have Da Boluo help him persuade Wang Jianzhi to complete his mission of unifying the three clans.

Over the next three days, with Da Boluo's help, Xu Shilun finally completely convinced Wang Jianzhi.

Half a month later, Wang Jianzhi arrived on Ultraman Planet.

On this day, the four Travelers finally gathered.

And on this day, Wang Jianzhi and Liu Dajun would officially declare their submission to Xu Shilun.

At this moment, Xu Shilun excitedly opened his live stream and stared intently at his mission.

Today, he was going to complete this mission of unifying the three clans.

At this moment, Liu Dajun, Da Boluo, Wang Jianzhi, and Xu Shilun were all filled with anticipation.

Along with countless viewers in the live stream, they too were curious.

They were extremely curious about what kind of rewards they would receive after completing the mission!


#104 Fight against the evil plan!

#105 The dragon of the God of Creation!

This means that Liu Dajun and his group created a local area network outside the Ultraman Civilization's mental network.

The effect is roughly similar to a high-tech mobile phone; this thing can connect to the network and transmit power, which is actually similar to the effect of a smart girl.

With this thing, they can avoid connecting to the main network and can isolate dark infection. Although they lose the previous rapid communication effect, it has both advantages and disadvantages.

From then on, the Ultraman Clan, led by Liu Dajun, completely disconnected from the mental link and entered the transformer era.

After the transformation is complete, they can actively enable defense mode, and then cut off darkness through the transformer, which is essentially adding a filter to the network.

After isolating darkness, they can connect mentally again. Although the network speed will be slowed down due to the transformer.

And it's more troublesome to operate.

But it's better than directly sinking into it like this.

The most important thing is that once they can connect mentally, their technology can develop once again.

Everyone will have to live together in the future; there is still plenty of time.

Otto and Ashura Clan developed peacefully.

Multi-Plasma Spark Tower plan initiated.

Two next to each sun, so they could have a continuous supply of energy.

#106 The kind God of Creation decided to give you a chance.

Chapter 106 The kind God of Creation decides to give you a chance.

Because they didn't exist in the real world, he had never truly seen them.

But at this moment, Su Mo looked at the Four-legged snake suspended in mid-air and felt that it would be a huge waste if this creature wasn't thrown into his Little Universe to become a dragon!

A snake's head and a snake's legs—what else could this be but a dragon-snake?!

The only flaw seemed to be the lack of horns.

Although Su Mo could modify it to have them in his Little Universe.

But after thinking about it, he felt it was unnecessary.

Because, without horns, it's called a Flood Dragon!

Dragons are righteous and should belong to the God of Creation.

And Flood Dragons are evil, so they belong to the Evil God!

No problem!

Flood Dragons need evil power, so it's perfectly normal for those who create evil power to be targeted!

Moreover, a Four-legged snake transforming into a Flood Dragon is incredibly simple.

Just make it a bit bigger, lengthen its tail, add a row of manes behind it, and some whiskers by the corners of its mouth.

A true Flood Dragon, right?!

This is a Flood Dragon. If I want to create a true Dragon later, I can just follow suit and sculpt one by hand. It would be perfect!

Or, should I still make a Dragon?

Thinking of this, Su Mo's heart began to stir.

But in the end, he gave up.

Mainly because he found it troublesome.

If he wasn't bothered by the trouble, he could even sculpt one by hand.

A Flood Dragon is also a dragon, so who cares.

Without the slightest hesitation, Su Mo directly threw the Four-legged snake into a small space specially made in his Little Universe and locked it up.

He decided to first look for other things. If there was truly nothing else, he planned to get a large cabbage to take back for lunch.

After eating, he would go to the farmer's market.

Then it could be the Monster Era.

Hmm, this time there's also a dragon, so let's call it 'The God of Creation provides a one-stop service.'

He circled twice and indeed found no other good things.

Ultimately, Su Mo got a cabbage, and then, using his mental power, he grabbed the previously locked-on alternative candidates: the Star Pusher and the Dung Beetle.

Now he had two monsters.

Su Mo pondered, wondering whether to start now.

Although these two monsters were very strong this time.

The Four-legged snake—bah, the Flood Dragon—could achieve planetary destruction just by its size.

But even ants can bite an elephant to death!

Those third-tier beings inside were also capable of exploding planets.

It was really hard to say who would win or lose in a fight.

Then there was the Star Pusher. Su Mo planned to enlarge it and strengthen its power, and that would be that.

Of course, the ability to walk in the void and survive in a vacuum was essential.

The Dung Beetle—an adult stepping on it might not even kill it, which showed how hard its shell was.

Placed in the Little Universe, with that defense, after being proportionally enlarged, it would basically be an invincible existence!!!

But thinking of this, Su Mo fell silent again.

Looking at it this way, it still didn't seem strong enough!

The Dung Beetle, pushing a planet around, didn't seem to have much destructive power; its defense was just incredibly high.

Speaking of which, the dragon also didn't seem quite right.

What kind of dragon fights with its bare body? It's not a Tyrannosaurus Rex.

At the very least, it should be able to summon wind and rain.

But how should he go about this?

Directly load a rule onto it?

But wouldn't that be a bit too overpowered?

And his main goal was for them to develop civilization.

If he loaded a cosmic rule onto this Four-legged snake, wouldn't it just be a crushing victory?

Weaken the rules?

Su Mo walked towards his kitchen with the cabbage, deep in thought.

How could he make them feel despair while also having the pressure to break through?

This was a problem.

And it was a serious one.

Indeed, even the God of Creation had worries.

Because Da Boluo directly crushed him with his strength.

Da Boluo wanted to kill him.

All it can do is push their planets to collide with other planets.

Or find a way to directly destroy their planets.

That is, peak third-tier.

That's impossible, unless he breaks through to the fourth tier within the Dung Beetle's body, otherwise, he won't be allowed to win.

The goal this time is for a fourth-tier to definitely appear.

That is, the Evil God descends!

Oh my, I am truly too evil.

No, as a God of Creation, how can I have such evil thoughts?

No, I'll change my perspective.

A Soul Clan member whose heart has suffered countless injustices, facing the world's harshness again and again, persisting again and again, but ending in failure every time.

This time, he is about to truly die, and the great God of Creation decides to bestow upon him powerful strength.

To let him fulfill his dream!

This God of Creation is truly too great!!!

Change the direction, change the perspective, and the same thing completely transforms.

Moreover, Da Boluo was indeed an ancestor of the Soul Clan.

As a result, this evil Da Boluo actually wanted to seize the body of his own descendant. This was truly too evil.

He first paused Zhang Jiao's time and also threw him into a small space.

So he decided to give them twenty more minutes to continue developing.

Uh, mainly because Su Mo decided to eat first.

If their wings didn't have problems, they could even directly use the acceleration from their wings for a charge.

The Star Pusher, the Dung Beetle.

So, the second step: the carapace defense ability was also increased by a hundredfold!

The third step: strength increased by a hundredfold, and then loaded with a low-level gravity rule, which would allow him to easily push planets around.

The final step: appearance modification.

His original six crawling limbs were all thickened and enlarged; the two lowest legs were feet, and the four upper hands, two for pushing planets, and two for fighting!

The original lines on his abdomen were directly changed into blocks of black armored abs! (Image should be here)

#109 I am back again!

Chapter 109 I, Zhang Jue, am back!

That pressure made it impossible for him to even think of resisting.

On his four arms, veins bulged, and black-red patterns continuously erupted with light!

However, the planet’s speed towards the sun remained undiminished.

His strength was still not enough!

“King, we are here to help you!”

“King, take my power, you must succeed!”

“King, you are the strongest!”

“Ashura!”

“Ashura!!!”

“…”

Yet another Ashura sacrificed their life.

Balls of flame once again merged into Wang Jianzhi’s body.

In the starry sky, Dark Tiga, controlled by Xu Shilun, watched this scene, his pupils contracting slightly.

“This guy is so incredibly fierce!

However, this time it was those Ashura who sacrificed their own lives, so it shouldn’t be counted against me, right?

After this is over, he surely won’t really try to beat me to death, right?!”

Xu Shilun kept pondering.

Finally, after calculation, he came to a conclusion: the possibility of him being beaten to death reached ninety-nine percent…

After reaching the conclusion, Xu Shilun cursed!

He even began to struggle internally, wondering if he should make a move when Ashura Planet reached the edge of the sun.

After all, he had built dozens of Plasma Spark Towers next to each sun.

And near each Plasma Spark Tower, he had placed at least three thousand Dark Tiga clones.

Coupled with Wang Jianzhi’s power, if they wanted to, there really was a chance to stop this planet.

Ultimately, Xu Shilun shook his head. If he helped Wang Jianzhi, his chance of being beaten to death was ninety-nine percent.

If he didn’t help, it would be a true one hundred percent.

By then, not to mention Wang Jianzhi, even Da Boluo would probably want to kill him.

At this moment, not only Xu Shilun was paying attention to everything here.

Even the great God of Creation was no exception.

Ever since he created Black Armor Great Emperor, he had been watching from inside.

It was just that this time he was invisible.

Ever since he was caught by Da Boluo watching the show without restraint last time, he now liked to turn invisible when watching shows.

If he didn’t want to be found, no one could find him.

The reason he kept watching was because his goal was not destruction, but to make them grow faster.

If Zhang Jiao did well, it was pressure; if he didn’t do well and truly destroyed civilization, Su Mo himself would probably feel heartache for a while.

It must be said, Zhang Jiao did really well.

Because in just this short amount of time, Su Mo’s idiotic system had already prompted three times.

【Discovered Transcendent Path】

【Name: Path of Rage (Incomplete)】

【Name: Path of Resentful Souls (Incomplete)】

【Name: Path of Body Cultivation (Incomplete)】

【Rank: 3.14】

【Level: 5】

【Description: A rule of the universe’s Grand Dao, complete it can directly reach Six-Tier.】

The introduction was very simple, but the two words “Six-Tier” at the end excited Su Mo for a long time.

Ashura truly lived up to its name as his lucky star!

In just this short time, three Grand Daos had emerged.

Although they were incomplete, they were still the highest transcendent attributes so far.

If it were before, it would be hard to say if Su Mo would have intervened if Ashura were destroyed.

But now, if Wang Jianzhi couldn’t withstand this wave of attacks and still chose to live and die with Ashura.

Su Mo would absolutely intervene.

After all, the Six-Tier path was on Wang Jianzhi.

Before he created the Six-Tier Grand Dao, or rather, before he was completely exploited by Su Mo, the great God of Creation would not allow him to die so quickly.

This is the greatness of God!

This is the grace of God!

To prevent him from dying so quickly, the great God of Creation would even protect Ashura as well.

This is called loving the house and its crow!

After all, he is the God of Creation full of love and justice!

Wang Jianzhi’s four arms seized the sky, and infinite flames surged behind him, creating counter-force, wanting to stop the planet with his own strength.

If it were before, he would only think that since there was no chance anyway, rather than surviving alone, it was better to die with Ashura.

But now, he couldn’t do that.

The Ashura believed in him so much, they would rather sacrifice themselves to let him break through!

They would rather die themselves than let him live!

If he still died, how could he face those Ashura who sacrificed for this!

He must live!

Carrying those Ashura who had already died, carrying the remaining ones who were still alive, they would all live on together!

“So, stop for me!!!!!”

The next chapter will be a bit late, I’m going out to eat.


#116 The king breaks through the realm, and all parties react.

Perhaps, he should also start that plan.

Da Boluo was silent for a moment: “This is the only option.”

“There’s absolutely no need! We must have other ways!”

Xu Shilun shook his head: “Stubborn. Bolo, I can’t beat them, so I’ll leave it to you.”

Da Boluo nodded, and with a wave of his hand, a terrifying power caused the entire Land of Light to tremble.

Cracks in space even threatened to collapse the entire Land of Light.

Da Boluo, who had already stepped halfway into the fourth stage, could shake space with every move of his hands and feet.

Countless energy beams shot towards Da Boluo, but he suppressed them with a single hand, then unleashed a powerful punch. The next moment, a giant mushroom cloud appeared over the Land of Light.

Da Boluo’s power shocked them.

At this moment, they truly understood how powerful the first Ashura King was.

Liu Dajun watched this scene and let out a furious roar. He was unwilling, he was unresigned!

Why, why, why?!

Why was it always him?!

Why did Da Boluo always help Xu Shilun?!

Why did his plans always fail?!

Liu Dajun roared, and at the peak of the third stage, he stepped into the air. His terrifying power had just begun to gather when, the next moment, his body was sent flying backward.

He was simply no match for Da Boluo!

“Xu Shilun, I’m going to kill you~”

Liu Dajun, still flying backward, continued to roar.

The energy in his hand finally condensed at this moment.

At the same time, within the Land of Light, all high-energy weapons had locked onto Xu Shilun at this moment.

Da Boluo frowned, pulled Xu Shilun, and stepped in front of Liu Dajun. He slapped away the energy ball in Liu Dajun’s hand again, then directly stomped him into the ground.

Beams of energy continued to shoot towards Da Boluo and Xu Shilun.

Da Boluo’s figure blurred, dodging all of them; those he couldn't dodge were blocked by the transparent protective shield around him.

He retaliated with a hand chop, instantly annihilating a city and shattering countless high-energy weapons.

This was because Da Boluo had targeted a city with fewer people.

The terrifying explosion caused the Land of Light, which had been established less than a month ago, to fall into turmoil.

At this moment, the first Ashura King, Da Boluo, suppressed an entire civilization with his own power!

In just a few dozen breaths, thirty million of the two hundred million Ultraman had died!

At this point, Xu Shilun shook his head at Da Boluo, indicating that these were all materials for his advancement, and he couldn't kill so many right now.

Moreover, Liu Dajun would still be useful later.

So, the Ultraman couldn't die yet, at least not so quickly.

Da Boluo saw Xu Shilun’s signal and, being amenable, immediately stopped his attack.

The next moment, Xu Shilun spoke to Liu Dajun, who had stood up again:

“General Jun, I told you, no matter what you do, you have to follow the crowd, but you just won’t listen, and I’m very helpless.

Now I’ll give you another chance. If you’re still stubborn, then I really can’t do anything. To sneak through, the premise is that everyone dies, so whether you agree or not, you will eventually die.

But I still gave you this opportunity for the sake of our Old fellow. I hope you don’t take it for granted!”

After Xu Shilun finished speaking, Liu Dajun, who had stood up, glanced at the slightly broken Land of Light.

He weighed the outcome of a death match with Da Boluo in his mind, and finally lowered his head.

He had, once again, failed.

He had failed again at the hands of Xu Shilun and Da Boluo!

And he would, once again, lead the Ultraman down a path of death.

But this time was different from before; this time, it was their initiative, just hoping…

As Liu Dajun’s thoughts reached the hearts of all citizens of the Ultraman Empire, they finally agreed to Xu Shilun’s idea.

The attack stopped, and Xu Shilun and Da Boluo still stood in mid-air.

Liu Dajun, with a gloomy face, glanced at the two and said, “We agree to your plan. Give us half an hour to record the data, and then, after that, their, their physical bodies, you can…”

By the end, Liu Dajun still couldn’t bring himself to say those words.

Xu Shilun, seeing this, nodded: “I’m glad to hear you say that. Originally, there wasn't enough time, but since you asked, we’ll wait an extra half hour.”

In fact, Liu Dajun’s agreement was already within Xu Shilun’s calculations.

Because Liu Dajun’s inner thoughts and the Ultraman’s way of thinking had already been grasped by him.

Coupled with his dark psychic network, it wasn't difficult to calculate this step.

For the next half hour, the three of them stared at each other, none speaking.

One hundred eighty million Ultraman recorded all their memories into the Land of Light.

They merged their final will into Liu Dajun’s soul.

At this moment, Liu Dajun also reached the peak of the third stage.

A rule also appeared before him!

Guard!

As long as he integrated his will into it, he would become a true level four powerhouse!

At this moment, although his aura was still not as profound as Da Boluo’s, he had already far surpassed Xu Shilun.

At this moment, his eyes were filled with killing intent as he looked at Xu Shilun. Just now, just now, this guy had killed nearly thirty million more Ultraman!

Then he glanced at Da Boluo beside him, constantly calculating in his mind.

He was indeed no match for Da Boluo, but what about Xu Shilun…

Now that they all knew the plan, what did Xu Shilun’s life or death have to do with it?

He was a disaster alive; instead of letting him continue to live, it was better to kill him directly!

Moreover, even if he revived in the future, it would do him no good. Killing him now was actually the best option.

At this moment, Liu Dajun’s killing intent surged.

“You want to kill me? Don’t forget, this is just a clone of mine. I’m just like you; as long as the Dark Tiga aren’t completely wiped out, I won’t truly die.

Your Land of Light plan was even based on my situation.

Moreover, why do you think I dared to reveal the plan when I was at a disadvantage?

Because I had already calculated that you would want to kill me, so, as soon as my will perishes, eight million Dark Tiga across the entire universe will broadcast.

Of course, you can also pray that the wicked god just happens to be busy or went home for dinner, or some other absurd excuse.

So, do you want to give it a try?!”

Xu Shilun’s words brought Liu Dajun’s killing intent to a halt.

This guy was absolutely ruthless!

His main point was: if I can’t do it, all of you have to die with me!

I came up with the plan. Either you cooperate with me honestly, let me break through, and we sneak through together.

If you want to know the plan and then not include me, then sorry, if you don’t include me, then no one gets to play!

This was his confidence!

Seeing that neither of them spoke, Xu Shilun spoke again: “Alright, you should be ready to break through to the fourth stage at any time now, right?

And all their memory data has been collected, right?”

Liu Dajun still said nothing.

Seeing this, Xu Shilun continued: “Since it’s done, then you two can leave. It’s my turn to upgrade now.

And the next scene won’t be pretty; I don’t think you’d want to see it.”

Da Boluo turned his head away, still silent.

Xu Shilun looked at Liu Dajun: “Alright, General Jun, don’t be angry. Too much anger isn’t good for your health. You two should still leave.”

Liu Dajun snorted coldly: “I’ll just watch from here!”

“You, alright, Bolo, keep an eye on him, don’t let him go crazy. Oh, and General Jun, remember not to let your people resist, otherwise I’ll look bad. If I accidentally die here, it won’t be good if the broadcast outside goes off.

General Jun, Bolo, wouldn’t you agree?”


#125 The wrath of the Creator God!

Chapter 125 The Wrath of the God of Creation!

Over the next half-day, Xu Shilun used the flesh and blood of billions of living beings as a foundation, raising his strength to Three-Tier Peak.

At this moment, even if Dark Otto were completely destroyed by Zhang Jiao, Xu Shilun would already possess Three-Tier Peak strength.

Although it consumed the flesh and blood of nearly two hundred million people to finally elevate his strength from Three-Order Middle Stage to Three-Tier Peak.

But at this moment, he finally no longer needed to depend on the consciousness of the Ultraman to survive.

Although his strength also reached Three-Tier Peak, compared to Da Boluo or Liu Dajun, he was still somewhat lacking—no, not somewhat, but a significant amount!

Because the path to the Fourth-Tier had not yet appeared, and even the Three-Tier Peak had not yet reached perfection.

But he didn't feel any surprise about these things.

Because he had calculated long ago that for him to reach the Fourth-Tier, he would need to be with the black and white Otto and Ashura.

Otherwise, he could only rely on time.

As for relying on time, he had already given up on that after his last live stream ended.

At that time, he already knew how terrible his cultivation talent truly was.

On this day, Liu Dajun and the Ultraman were completely dead.

But at this time, the evil Evil God—no, it should be said, the great God of Creation—was still smoking.

The next day, they headed to Ashura.

Under Xu Shilun's deliberate control, Ashura was already not far from Ultraman Planet.

With their strength, it took them only a day to arrive.

Afterward, Da Boluo, following Xu Shilun's instructions, copied all of Ashura's memories onto his soul body.

To ensure that these memories would not become chaotic after his death, he cleared the memories from his other two arms.

Then, he used them entirely to store Ashura's memories.

Every particle of his soul was equivalent to a massive USB drive, capable of storing a large amount of memory items.

However, even so, it was thanks to Xu Shilun's previous actions.

Otherwise, a single strike from a million Ashura would have been quite difficult for him with just two arms.

Regarding this matter, Da Boluo really didn't know whether to thank Xu Shilun or to kill him.

In any case, upon learning that only two hundred thousand Ashura remained, Da Boluo's expression was very grim.

Even Xu Shilun felt that Da Boluo wanted to slap him to death.

But in the end, for some unknown reason, he chose to give up.

It could be because he suddenly had a change of heart, or it could be because of the eight hundred thousand Dark Tiga in the universe.

Xu Shilun wasn't very clear about why this happened, mainly because he didn't dare to ask.

He felt that it was better for people to maintain a certain distance from each other.

Therefore, he didn't plan to ask about this matter.

Next, two Three-Tier Perfected beings, with one foot already in the Fourth-Tier, and one Three-Tier Peak being, along with a being possessing eight hundred thousand clones, set off towards Zhang Jiao.

Now, their mission was to get Wang Jianzhi back.

However, unfortunately, their imagination was beautiful, but the outcome had some discrepancies.

For example, Wang Jianzhi couldn't be woken up no matter how much they called him.

For example, when Wang Jianzhi was chasing Zhang Jiao, he kept attacking Xu Shilun.

And for example, as soon as Da Boluo appeared, he was once again sent flying by Zhang Jiao.

Finally, Xu Shilun and the others reformulated their plan.

...

【Boom~】

...

Su Mo, who was smoking outside, suddenly stiffened, and most of his cigarette ash fell off.

Then he abruptly turned his gaze into the Little Universe, and his entire being was dumbfounded.

The universe was empty; there were no stars, no civilizations.

At this moment, the only living beings in the entire Little Universe were a Mosquito and a Da Spiders who had fled to a remote area long ago.

And his status began to rapidly increase at that very instant.

Name: Su Mo

Gender: Male

Status: 7.0 (Current World Status: 3.14)

Creation Level: 2

Looking at the status on his idiotic system, the sense of repulsion from this world, and the pulling sensation from other worlds, he knew this wasn't fake.

Directly, this wave, damn it, was a pure status upgrade!

And it instantly rose from a medium status to a high status.

Although he wanted status, he didn't say he didn't want to improve his strength!

Now that the gap between his strength and status was so large, he should inherently exist in a high-dimensional universe.

But now he was still in a low-dimensional world of only 3.14, and his strength hadn't improved; he would be pulled up to a high-dimensional universe!

To put it nicely, it's ascension; to put it badly, this is a small world not allowing such a powerful existence.

Su Mo secretly calculated that if he continued to stay in the real world, he would be pulled into a high-dimensional world in no more than five hours.

In a seven-dimensional world, not to mention the top-tier Primordial Era, it was truly a place where Da Luo Immortals were everywhere!

With his Second-Tier strength, if he really appeared in such a place, even someone farting could kill him!

Strength is mainly divided into dimension and level.

Different dimensions compare dimensions; the same dimension compares levels.

If he were really pulled into a seven-dimensional world, he wouldn't even know how he died.

No, he absolutely couldn't go up.

Therefore, he must raise his God of Creation level to the Third-Tier within the shortest possible time.

In the real world, he could stay for a maximum of five hours; exceeding five hours, the world would automatically warn him, and then high-dimensional beings would come down to apprehend him.

In fact, Su Mo felt that let alone five hours, even three hours might be enough for him to be discovered by those above.

After all, in his current situation, a seven-dimensional status in this 3.14 world was as dazzling as a large light bulb in the dark night; anyone who glanced at the low-dimensional world could see his existence.

Therefore, Su Mo no longer planned to stay in the real world; he wanted to hide in the Little Universe.

His Little Universe was actually similar to the so-called 'Cave Heavens and Blessed Lands' in the cultivation world.

Hiding inside during a great tribulation of heaven and earth, although he might still die, the probability of death would definitely be smaller than outside.

He could stay outside for a maximum of five hours, which would theoretically be a little over two hundred years.

But if he remained hidden in the Little Universe without showing his face, he could actually stay for a thousand years.

That is, one day in the outside world.

But that's the absolute maximum of one day; exceeding one day, those above would definitely discover him.

Even if he hid in the Little Universe, it would be the same.

To avoid being discovered, he felt that even the Third-Tier wasn't safe enough; he had to break through to the Fourth-Tier!

The Fourth-Tier controls rules.

He already possessed the rules of creation; as long as he met the requirements, he could break through to the Fourth-Tier.

Unlike others, who had to find the path step by step, develop rules, and finally break through.

When the Little Universe civilization breaks through to the Fourth-Tier, he can break through to the Third-Tier God of Creation.

When two civilizations in the Little Universe break through to the Fifth-Tier, he can directly reach the Fourth-Tier God of Creation.

Therefore, this time, he must cultivate two Fifth-Tier civilizations within a thousand years—no, eight hundred, no, six hundred years.

After thinking all of this through, Su Mo finally looked at his Little Universe.

Now, he wanted to see what caused him to suddenly become so passive!

He just went out for a cigarette; what else happened!

How could all civilizations be annihilated in such a short moment!

Then, the evil Evil God Su Mo suppressed the anger in his heart and began to replay the events.

He wanted to see what exactly happened!

If he found out who was behind this, he would skin them alive and pull out their tendons!

A moment later, the scene began to play.

And Su Mo's face grew darker and darker!

His fists couldn't help but clench, making cracking sounds!

If it weren't for the fact that this Little Universe was still useful, if it weren't for the fact that he needed to hide inside now, he would have wanted to destroy this entire universe!

Stowaway!

I'll let you stow away, damn it!

You're good at stowing away, aren't you, damn it!

Next time, I'll just drain all the seawater; let's see how you stow away!!!

Su Mo watched as Da Boluo and Liu Dajun restrained Wang Jianzhi, who was in a rage, and then Xu Shilun killed all the Ashura in front of him.

Instantly, Wang Jianzhi broke through to the Fourth-Tier.

And at the moment his status increased, or rather, during that brief time difference when he entered the Little Universe.

With a single thought, he also killed all the Dark Ultraman, absorbing their computing power, allowing him to also break through to the Fourth-Tier simultaneously.

Then, he self-destructed!

And Da Boluo and Liu Dajun together!

They dragged Wang Jianzhi and Zhang Jiao into the self-destruction!

The self-destruction of three Fourth-Tier beings almost flattened half a galaxy!

Seeing this scene, Su Mo almost exploded at that moment!

He wanted to improve his strength, which required civilization to reach the Fourth-Tier for his God of Creation level to advance to the Third-Tier.

But, damn it, none of these breakthroughs were from those civilizations!

All four of them were Travelers!

Excluding them, the strength of those civilizations before their demise was at most the Third-Tier.

Ultraman, Third-Tier; the only Zhang Jiao of the Soul Clan, Third-Tier.

The worst Ashura was only Second-Tier!

All combined, the status he gained was only 1.1, damn it!

At this moment, Su Mo truly wanted to collect all of Xu Shilun's fragments, revive him, and then tear him apart piece by piece!

You're so good at coming up with ideas, aren't you!

You're so good at finding solutions, aren't you!

You're the smartest, aren't you, damn it!

Good, you're smart!

Everyone in the world except you is stupid!

Since you're so smart, perfect, I'm not going out now.

I'll just watch you from inside the universe!

Let's see how smart you truly are!

You want to resurrect, don't you!

You want to stow away, don't you!

Good, you've successfully stowed away now, and not only did you succeed, but you also brought others along.

But do you think it's over just because you stowed away!

Do you think I can't do anything to you just because you successfully stowed away!

Perfect, you've broken through and also possess rules.

Then, let your Grand Dao rules be the path of transcendence this time!

I wonder, when you resurrect and find all your power gone, will you still be so happy!


#126 New civilization!!!

Chapter 126 New Civilization!!!

They are Wrath, Soul Body, Physique, Darkness, and Guardian.

Then he grabbed all the surrounding planets and gathered them into one super continent.

The area of this continent was more than thirty times larger than the previous planets.

Then three suns rotated in a cycle, creating night and light.

After that, he reinforced the continent, adding extraordinary rules and restrictions.

Previously, his civilizations were basically slash-and-burn.

After the world's destruction, a one-click, user-friendly creation system would suffice.

This time, he wanted to do it himself!

The continent's rules were set: those within and including Two-Tier could not fly.

Three-Tier could not leave the continent.

Four-Tier, leaving the continent for more than half a month would result in death!

Only Five-Tier could truly roam the starry sky!

This was a rule Su Mo specifically set to prevent people in this world from running rampant across the world again after breaking through.

Then, he used the five extraordinary rules as cornerstones, as nourishment for the new civilization.

He collected all the fragments of those goods and placed them in various parts of the continent, creating numerous secret realms.

The first rule for all secret realms: the person himself is not allowed to enter!

The second rule: fragments can be taken out, but power cannot!

The third rule: indiscriminate attack!

After that, each secret realm created by a fragment had other different aspects.

For example, Wang Jianzhi's Wrath Secret Realm would definitely be filled with raging flames, and only by defeating all the Ashura transformed by wrath could his fragment be taken.

Moreover, each secret realm was connected; when more than five fragments were taken from a secret realm, the difficulty of subsequent dungeons would double.

By the time of the last fragment, they would face two Wang Jianzhi at their peak, who had already broken through to Four-Tier!

And this was not the most important thing; the most important thing was that these secret realms had to be cleaned regularly.

If not cleaned, the monsters inside would rush out.

For example, the Soul Clan from Da Boluo's domain.

Dark Tiga from Xu Shilun's secret realm.

Ashura from Wang Jianzhi's dungeon.

And finally, the robots from Liu Dajun's dungeon.

The purpose of doing this was to incentivize the new civilization to quickly grow stronger.

After all, his time was limited, and he couldn't keep playing around with them here.

If it was before, it wouldn't have mattered, but now it was only one day.

As a result, this time he was tricked by Xu Shilun.

This made the God of Creation very unhappy!

And when the God of Creation is unhappy, no one can have an easy time!

After sorting out the current map and dungeon plans, he pondered a bit more.

He also pulled in text.

Then he replicated the Three-Order Middle Stage Ant Queen from history.

The replicated Ant Queen could not upgrade, and even if killed, no status could be obtained.

However, she possessed the Ant Queen's original wisdom and abilities.

Su Mo didn't strengthen the Mosquitoes; after all, they had been food for so many epochs, they should be used to it by now.

He just moved all the Mosquitoes to this continent.

He also grabbed the Spider Sky Emperor and strengthened him, making his power directly reach Four-Tier Peak.

Then he controlled him to seal off the entire continent with spider webs.

To prevent those people from escaping the continent, those who had just broken through to Four-Tier and originally thought they could go see the universe, the moment they left the universe, they would either be swallowed by the Spider Sky Emperor.

Let them know that ascension is a scam!

Then he would speed up the flow of time and begin the evolution of new life.

He roughly calculated that it would take about three hundred years for new life to evolve, even using a time flow rate of one to ten thousand years.

If he stayed inside, he would have to stay for three hundred years.

So he still decided to stay outside for a while.

As for why he didn't use a time flow rate of one to ten thousand years after the civilization began, but always chose one to one thousand years, it's actually very simple.

Because the Little Universe cannot bear it, and the life within it cannot bear it either.

Anyway, in short, the best time ratio is one to one thousand years. At least for now, it is.

Just in that brief moment, he felt the repulsion strengthen once more.

So, from now on, until he breaks through to Four-Tier God of Creation every day, he won't go anywhere else; he'll just stay in the Little Universe!

New life began to be released.

It's unclear if it was because the great God of Creation was watching from nearby.

Or because his status had improved, or because this map was exceptionally large, the number of lives released this time was exceptionally high.

This 'many' doesn't refer to types, but to quantity.

Of course, in terms of types, there were actually many as well.

For example, various animals, plants, and other co-existing ecologies were also released.

But there was only one species with cosmic-standard intelligence.

Because this was not normal flesh-and-blood life, nor was it a Soul Body like Zhang Jiao.

Instead, it was a group of silicon-based life forms!

Their bodies were entirely made of stone and various minerals.

They fed on dirt.

Their collective consciousness was very strong and highly concentrated.

Most importantly, they had no emotions!

When he saw this, Su Mo was dumbfounded.

Damn it, he had painstakingly set up a world himself and created so many dungeons, but these guys were completely immune!

No matter how much the Ant Queen reproduced or how numerous they were, they wouldn't conflict with a bunch of stones.

Moreover, these stones ate dirt.

The only possible conflict would be with the Mosquitoes, as Mosquitoes also eat dirt.

But on such a large continent, even if they were truly let loose, they might not be able to eat even one-tenth of it in a thousand years.

This wasn't even the most difficult part; the most difficult part was that these guys had no emotions!

Damn it, no emotions meant no motivation.

Without emotions, they wouldn't be attracted to those dungeons.

Plus, they wouldn't conflict with the Ant Queen or Mosquitoes, so no wars would break out.

No war meant no desire to become stronger!

And they also had an almost endless supply of food.

This, this, what the hell can be done now!

Anyone would just lie flat.

After all, he had created and destroyed worlds so many times, but this was his first time creating non-flesh-and-blood life.

Moreover, these guys didn't even have souls.

They only had a cluster of will, similar to Xu Shilun's situation where he abandoned his Soul Body and only retained a cluster of will.

Every day they'd find a spot to lie down, flip over when hungry, take a couple bites of dirt, and once full, flip back over to sunbathe.

A species like this, let alone a thousand years, even if you gave them ten thousand or a hundred thousand years, it's questionable if they could even get a rice bowl!

How the hell is this supposed to work!

So, what the hell is going on!

Thinking of this, Su Mo for the first time began to check the simulated creation scenario.

Simulated creation means replicating the current map, then continuously incubating life, and finally seeing which life can survive on this planet.

Although only three hundred years had passed for him, in the simulation, three million years had actually passed.

All kinds of life forms constantly appeared and constantly perished.

They were either killed by Mosquitoes, or fought a great battle with Ants and were eventually eaten by them.

Or monsters appeared from the major secret realms, killing all life.

The closest a race came to success had already begun to develop intelligence, but in the end, because they were too close to Xu Shilun's secret realm, they were infected by his Darkness and became insane.

Then a new race appeared.

To prevent being infected by various dark emotions, they directly abandoned emotions.

To avoid fighting with Mosquitoes and Ants, their bodies evolved into stone.

To avoid insufficient food, they chose to eat dirt.

And then this thing appeared!

After seeing all this, Su Mo fell silent.

At this moment, he felt like destroying this race directly and recreating it.

But there wasn't enough time.

And re-evolving civilization would take a day's time.

If he had time, he wouldn't care about a day like this.

Looking at this result, and then at the Stone Race who were completely lying flat, so much so that they didn't even bother to move when Ants crawled over them, as if they were real stones, Su Mo was truly going a bit crazy.

Damn it, Xu Shilun, you dog!

If your Darkness hadn't driven that race insane, how could such a thing have appeared!!!

Now, what do you want me to do!

Am I really going to be captured and taken to the higher dimensional world!

No, this is absolutely not allowed.

I must find a way.

Should I create another Traveler?

But now, Travelers already know what this world is like.

Even if they came, they would surely choose to lie flat just like these guys.

Or give some rewards?

But what should I give?

Lead the tribe to break through Five-Tier and then let them go back?

This, would anyone believe it?

Thinking of this, Su Mo fell into deep thought.

With the current credibility of the God of Creation, ah, no, the Evil God, would anyone really believe him?

Moreover, this civilization is so good; they can just eat dirt every day, don't have to work, and have food and drink just by lying down.

This is already the dream of countless working people.

What to do?

...

Su Mo constantly thought about how to give this civilization ambition.

How to make them develop quickly.

He didn't care before, but this time he was genuinely anxious and in a great hurry.

This was the promise of the God of Creation, and also the promise of the Evil God; Su Mo stated that he would deliver on his word!!!

In the real world, Xu Shilun, Liu Dajun, and the others' live streams had completely cut off.

This cutoff wasn't a black screen, but a direct disappearance.

Just like when Da Boluo died.

At this moment, they knew that Xu Shilun and the others were truly dead.

Initially, some people hadn't quite understood why Xu Shilun and the others would do this.

But after some analysis by interested parties, Wolong, Phoenix Fledgling, Wenhe, and some other more outspoken people, their faces turned extremely grim.


#127 Worship God! Worship the God of Creation!

Chapter 127: Worship God! Worship the God of Creation!

No, it should be said that Xu Shilun can live, but if they were to travel to a world where Xu Shilun is still alive, that would be absolutely impossible.

Who is Xu Shilun? Others might not know, but how could they not be clear?

Look at those Travelers, those from his era, which one of them had a good end?

Before, they truly didn't know why Xu Shilun and the others suddenly closed their live broadcasts.

It wasn't until their live broadcast rooms completely disappeared that they learned from the people in Wang Jianzhi's live broadcast room that this guy had run off with Wang Jianzhi, Liu Dajun, and Da Boluo.

You know, at that time, although Xu Shilun and Liu Dajun had closed their live broadcast rooms, Wang Jianzhi, that madman, had never closed his.

It was precisely because he kept his live broadcast open until his death that others knew what had happened inside.

Otherwise, they still wouldn't have reacted to what was going on inside.

Why did all these people's live broadcast rooms suddenly disappear?

You know, this only happens when someone dies.

Before, they had no experience and were fooled by Xu Shilun closing his live broadcast room, making everyone think he was dead.

Although this plan was terrible, basically no one believed it, and later Zhang Jiao even came out and caused trouble under his name.

But now they know.

Closing a live broadcast room is just a black screen; only when the live broadcast room disappears does it mean the Traveler has died.

But what's the use of knowing this now?

Before, they didn't know what happened.

But those few people mentioned earlier, which one wasn't smart? In an instant, they realized that this guy had stowed away!

And not by conventional means.
