As soon as the live broadcast began, he immediately invested five million to attract traffic.

Today, he was going to tell the world that he had established contact with a God and that he would invest one billion to build the most magnificent temple for the God!

He would be the first true Divine Envoy in this world!

It was because Su Mo sensed the other party's thoughts that he decided to wait a bit.

Wouldn't it be interesting if, when this guy announced that he had become a Divine Envoy and that he was going to build a temple for himself, an evil god suddenly reached out from the screen and grabbed his soul?

You ask why the evil god would do this.

And why only grab the soul, not the body as well.

Of course, it's because the evil god has a grudge against the God of Creation.

So, is there any problem with the evil god grabbing the God of Creation's Divine Envoy?

As for why only the soul is grabbed, that's even easier to explain.

Of course, it's for the sake of the show's effect.

After all, he had set up such a stage.

If the evil god were to instantly grab this person, who claimed to have communicated with the God of Creation and called himself a Divine Envoy, wouldn't the God of Creation lose a lot of face?

At that time, the evil god's hand would extend from the camera, and black mist would fill the sky.

Just as it was about to grab this kid, a ball of white light would emerge from his body, blocking it.

The show's effect would be maxed out.

But in the end, the evil god would become enraged, extend another hand, and give up on grabbing his body, instead absorbing his soul.

Wouldn't all of this make sense?

As for his own thoughts, Su Mo believed he was simply a logical genius.

No matter what he did, it was so flawless.

Even this civilization, which only knew how to rot, had been thoroughly subdued by him, let alone an ordinary person.

That would be even easier.

Su Mo waited quietly here.

On the other side, Yu Jiemo's live broadcast finally began.

“Hello everyone, I am Yu Jiemo, the sole designated heir of Qiangsheng Group, a Fortune Global 500 company. You might not be very familiar with me.

Then I’ll introduce myself in another way, and perhaps you’ll understand: you know Phoenix Fledgling from Xu Shilun’s live stream, right?

That’s right, Phoenix Fledgling is me!”

【Holy cow, I didn't expect to be watching a live stream with such a big shot!】

【Holy cow, I even cursed him in the live stream before, will I be okay?!】

【My goodness, Phoenix Fledgling is that awesome!!!】

【I just knew who in the live stream could possess such wisdom; it turns out to be the young master of Qiangsheng Group. That makes sense.】

【Handsome, smart, and rich—such a person is simply a perfect match!】

Yu Jiemo looked at the barrage of comments below and nodded with great satisfaction. Although some people were still cursing him.

But after he used his superpower and summoned a wave of paid commenters, the comment section instantly became much more pleasant.

Most of them were praising him for being handsome and smart.

When Bolo first gained his superpower and wanted to show off, the best outcome would actually have been something like this.

But unfortunately, he failed to show off.

Actually, he felt that the main reason was that Bolo didn't use his superpower. Of course, at that time, he had already become a Traveler, so superpowers were useless anyway.

“Ahem, thank you all for your support. Today, I’m starting this live broadcast mainly to announce something.

I believe everyone knows about the issue of leaving too many messages in the Traveler world’s live stream, which could potentially attract the attention of evil gods.

And as one of the people who spoke the most, I was naturally worried. But after discussing with a few other friends, we came up with a solution: praying to a deity.

I don’t know how they did it, nor do I know if they received a response, but!

I, just ten minutes ago, prayed to a deity and received a response!

Everyone, don’t be afraid. I prayed not to an evil god, but to that one, the existence who can suppress an evil god with a single hand!

That is, the one who blew up everyone’s phones back then.

Regarding this matter, I, Yu Jiemo, promise that as long as the phone was indeed damaged by the God, Qiangsheng Group will reimburse it within three days. Of course, it must be a domestic phone.”

【Qiangsheng Group is too awesome!】

【Yu Gongzi, I love you!】

【Husband, you're amazing!!!】

【Qiangsheng Group is awesome!】

【Support Qiangsheng Group!】

【Why aren't foreign ones covered???】

At first, some people were still concerned about his worship of the deity, but after hearing that mobile phones could be reimbursed, everyone went crazy, constantly flooding the screen.

“Ahem, the reason I dare to be responsible for the phones is, first, because Qiangsheng Group does not lack this amount of funds, and second, because I have received the recognition of that God!

It is precisely because of this that I dare to say such words!

Next, I will announce that Qiangsheng Group will invest one billion to build the most magnificent temple in the world for the great God!”

People all over the world were stunned watching the live broadcast.

They weren't sure if what the person from Qiangsheng Group said was true.

After all, what he said was indeed a bit outrageous.

It's only now that the Gods have intervened several times.

If this had been before, if he dared to say such a thing, the mental hospital's car would have been at his door the next moment.

After all, it was indeed too outrageous.

They used to crack down on supernatural phenomena, but now, who dares to say there are no Gods? They are genuinely afraid that a guy covered in black mist will pop out of their phones.

If you say he's not a God, it seems he really is.

But if you say he is a God, he's not a good God...

It was precisely because of this that everyone was half-believing Yu Jiemo's words.

Those who believed were frantically trying to contact Yu Jiemo, hoping to also contribute a portion of the funds.

Those who didn't believe simply didn't believe.

“And I, too, will become the first Divine Envoy!”

As Yu Jiemo's words fell, the clear sky suddenly darkened.

Then, strong winds swept through, and trash, leaves, mineral water bottles, and so on flew everywhere.

Just as everyone was still bewildered, a large Black Hand stretched out from a nearby camera.

At first, it was only the size of a baby’s arm, but in just a moment, it had expanded to tens of meters in size.

Then, the large hand covered the sky, and darkness spread across the land.

The last bit of sunlight in the sky also disappeared under the obscuring shadow of this arm.

Upon seeing this scene, those present were first stunned, then began to scream frantically.

Finally, they ran around in chaos, only to find that all surrounding roads had been blocked.

In the live broadcast room, people who saw this scene were directly dumbfounded.

What the hell were you saying before?

Didn't you say you were worshipping the one who could suppress an evil god with a single hand?

Why did this one appear just as you announced becoming a Divine Envoy?

Did you worship the wrong God!!!!

Yu Jiemo's face was filled with extreme terror, and he screamed frantically.

His body curled into a ball, constantly crawling backward, hoping that this Black Hand would not harm him.

Then his thoughts were so ridiculous in front of the great evil god.

The giant hand crossed everything and directly grabbed towards him.

“God of Creation, save me!”

【Boom!】

A ray of light shone, and the Black Hand was instantly repelled.

The staff, who had been filled with fear just moments before, were once again stunned on the spot.

Even the audience in the live broadcast room were all dumbfounded once again.

Before, they had all thought this guy was certainly dead.

But who would have thought there would be a twist!

Moreover, this guy actually received the protection of that God!!!

At this moment, no one doubted whether what Yu Jiemo had said earlier was true.

Because the facts were already laid bare before their eyes!

At this moment, countless people were filled with envy.

This guy was already so rich.

Why did such good fortune still fall upon him?

It is estimated that Qiangsheng Group will reach another level in the future.

And Yu Jiemo, the young master of Qiangsheng Group, will also become one of the most respected people in this world.

After all, he was the first person to communicate with a God!

And he was also going to invest one billion to build a temple for the God.

Many of those high-ranking officials weren't short of one billion, but what they lacked was the courage to communicate with a God. Now, this foolish boy had gotten the advantage, so who wouldn't be envious?

The other guys in the group who had talked about worshipping a God before were now green with regret.

It's one thing not to be as rich as him, but they weren't as daring either. If they had communicated with the God before, then they would be standing in that position now!

No money, so what if they had no money? As long as they truly communicated with a God and stood in that position, if a situation like today's occurred, half of the world's billionaires would have to grovel before them!

Yu Jiemo watched the Black Hand recede, then glanced at the light on his body, and immediately stood up, laughing loudly:

“Hahaha, I told you the God of Creation would protect me!”


#131 See you through time again!

Chapter 131 Another time travel!

However, the black mist that had just been repelled suddenly surged once more!

Then, the terrifying black mist once again reached towards Yu Jiemo.

Two pitch-Black Hands separated from within the black mist, and the light on Yu Jiemo's body erupted once more.

His laughter suddenly came to an abrupt halt!

Everyone only saw a translucent phantom, identical to Yu Jiemo, being snatched out by a large hand from within the Black Hand.

Yu Jiemo, who had been standing upright, instantly collapsed to the ground.

Subsequently, a terrifying white light burst forth from his body, forcing the black mist back.

At the same time, the Black Hand vanished, and with it, Yu Jiemo's soul.

The entire network instantly fell silent.

Looking at Yu Jiemo in the live stream, whose darkness had disappeared, and whose light was also gradually fading.

At this moment, the entire network was terrifyingly silent.

One could even hear a pin drop!

Yu Jiemo's laughter still echoed in their ears.

One could vaguely hear the mocking of that Evil God.

I can't defeat that existence, but my inability to defeat him is not a reason for you to be arrogant in front of me!

Although I can't defeat him, handling you is still easy!

How dare you be arrogant in front of me!

Xu Shilun fell, and the live stream changed at this moment.

It became a transmigration live stream, just like before.

On a vast plain with three suns, a Stone Men nine meters tall saw the bullet comments floating past his eyes, and he suddenly stood up.

【Yu Jiemo, what is this?!】

【Fengchu Brother!】

【This, this, this is transmigration!】

【Damn it, he was forcibly pulled down by the Evil God!】

【The Evil God personally took action to snatch someone away. One has to say, this is the style of my Fengchu Brother!】

【Too fierce, too fierce, simply too fierce! As expected of Fengchu Brother, he dared to directly confront the Evil God and start a fight! He's truly too fierce!】

【Whose general is this, to be so brave? I originally thought Lu Bu was invincible, but I didn't expect there to be someone braver than him in this world!】

【You, a rich second-generation heir, why couldn't you just honestly stay at home and live off your parents? You just had to be a Divine Emissary. Now look, you've transmigrated. Are you satisfied?】

【I know this question. Rich second-generation heirs, they've already experienced a life we can never experience, so it's quite normal for them to seek some excitement.

【Now that you mention it, I even suspect that transmigration was something he specifically planned.

Otherwise, how could an ordinary person dare to mock an Evil God? Isn't that just asking to die faster!】

【Damn, now that you mention it, I really suspect it's true. Fengchu Brother, you've successfully transmigrated, and the money in the real world is useless to you now. Why don't you help us poor people out?

We can offer you advice in the live stream.】

【Yes, brother, you've transmigrated, can I inherit your legacy?】

【Brother, although you've transmigrated, I'm your half-brother. Can you let me inherit the Qiangsheng Group?】

The Stone Men's expression was unusually blank. He first stared at the bullet comments in front of him.

Although he still refused to believe the fact that he had transmigrated at this moment.

But looking at the bullet comments, the surrounding environment, and then at his own body, the giant Stone Men, nearly ten meters tall, suddenly sat down with a thud.

Accompanied by a loud bang, a large pit was smashed into the ground by him.

He had truly transmigrated!

At this moment, his mind was blank.

He was unwilling to believe the reality before his eyes.

He only thought he was still dreaming.

After all, he was someone protected by the God of Creation!

Even if anyone transmigrated, it couldn't possibly be him!

Why was it him who transmigrated!!!

Faced with such a result, he refused to believe it no matter what.

Just as his mind was still blank, the sky suddenly darkened, and then another ball of white light lit up.

A large hand stretched out, and the darkness was immediately seized. Then, accompanied by a scream, the darkness completely receded.

Yu Jiemo stared blankly at the sky, still not having fully recovered.

Looking at the scene in the live stream, the audience completely erupted.

【Damn it, what did I just see!】

【Did you hear the scream!】

【I heard it. What do you think that was?】

【This, this, this, those two are fighting!】

【The Evil God was grabbed by that one with a single hand, like an eagle catching a chick. No, it should be like the Evil God grabbing Yu Jiemo!】

【Damn it, that one actually stood up for Yu Jiemo!】

【Nonsense, Yu Jiemo might not be a true Divine Emissary, but the divine light that just burst from him at least proves that he really had contact with that one.

If it weren't for the God's protection, I suspect the Evil God wouldn't have just made him transmigrate, but would have crushed him to death.】

【Yes, no matter how Yu Jiemo communicated with that one, the most basic point is that he has already been branded with the God of Creation's mark.

As a result, this Evil God still dared to lay hands on him. Isn't this just asking for trouble with the big boss!

Now he's directly being taught a lesson by that one.】

【Just saying, don't you feel something strange? If the God Yu Jiemo worships is really that powerful, how could this Evil God still dare to attack him?】

【Yes, I'm actually wondering about this too. If there was truly an overwhelming power difference, that Evil God should have been killed many times over by now. Why is he still alive?】

【I have an idea, which is that the Evil God might not be as weak as we imagine. Just like Xu Shilun can create so many dark clones, it's very normal for the Evil God to create some clones if he wants to.

Moreover, I suspect that he is actually an existence on the same level as this God of Creation, but the God of Creation is the main body, while the Evil God only appears as clones, which is why he's not the God of Creation's opponent.】

【No, the God of Creation should be even more powerful. Otherwise, the Evil God wouldn't dare not confront him after so many clones were killed.

So, the result should be that the God of Creation can't kill the other party, but the Evil God also can't do anything to the God of Creation, only disgust him.】

【Damn it, that's the truth! Now that you say it, I probably understand. No matter what the God of Creation creates, the Evil God wants to destroy it. Just like Yu Jiemo said he was the God of Creation's Divine Emissary, then it's very normal for the Evil God to come and try to kill him.】

【When two tigers fight, one will be injured?】

【If you're uncultured, read more books. This is called 'when the city gate catches fire, the fish in the moat suffer'.】

【I didn't expect Yu Jiemo to even cause such two existences to fight for him. In this situation, even if he dies, I'd consider it worth it.】

【Uh, you'd consider it worth it if he dies?】

【I know this question. If I could marry Liu Yifei, I'd even be willing to become the world's richest man. That's how it works, right? It's easy to solve problems with a formula like this.】

【...】

All of this was, of course, the doing of our great God of Creation, Su Mo.

As the saying goes, if you're putting on a show, go all the way. If this Evil God captured the God of Creation's subordinate, and the God of Creation did nothing about it.

Then wouldn't the God of Creation lose too much face?

After doing all this, Su Mo glanced at Yu Jiemo and, finding him still in a daze, ignored him.

After all, having just experienced something like transmigration, it's understandable that he needed some time to accept it.

However, it was a good thing he transmigrated as a Stone Men. If he had gotten the Bolo script, in the time he spent in a daze, he would probably have been eaten by Mosquitoes until not a single hair was left.

At this moment, the small group chat that Xiao Pang Shu and others had created earlier was also deadly silent.

Especially those who had been jealous of Yu Jiemo before.

Those who could appear here were generally smart people.

They hadn't thought it through earlier, only being overwhelmed by Yu Jiemo's family background and the fact that he had gained the God's recognition.

After all, being the sole heir to a Fortune 500 company or being recognized by a god, either of these things was astonishing enough.

Not to mention having both concentrated in one person.

Even Wenhe was considering whether to build a relationship with Yu Jiemo and get himself a position in the temple or his group.

Although he had indeed thought of using them for experiments when he said that before.

If it failed or something went wrong, it wouldn't be his problem.

But if it succeeded, he would definitely have a share of the credit.

The subsequent situation, however, made everyone in the group wary of Wenhe.

This guy, he's just not human!!!

He truly only harms Shang Tianhe, but not Wenhe!

...Yu Jiemo ignored the constantly floating bullet comments in front of him, still lying blankly on the ground.

He looked up at the sky, his face filled with despair.

The sky changed from bright to murky, and after an unknown amount of time, Yu Jiemo finally came to his senses.

【Brother, you're finally awake. Since you've transmigrated, you must accept reality. The first thing you should do now is find Da Boluo, then grind Xu Shilun's fragments into powder, otherwise I'm afraid he'll grab you and instantly refine you.】

【Whether it's Da Boluo, or Liu Dajun, or Wang Jianzhi, anyone is fine, just don't trust Xu Shilun, otherwise you'll be sold out and still have to help him count the money.】

【Young man, get moving, otherwise it'll really be too late.】

【Yu Gongzi, by the way, since you've transmigrated, can you give me your inheritance?】

【Yes, while you can still speak, why don't you make a will?】

【By the way, will a will made like this be recognized by our country?】

【That's really a problem, after all, the person has transmigrated to another world, and now he's not even a person anymore...】

Yu Jiemo still did not speak. During his silence, he suddenly felt a little hungry.

Subconsciously, he looked around, wanting to find food.

Not far away, a two-meter-long tiger looked like a small cat in front of him, who was over nine meters tall.

He stood up without a word, and a loud bang accompanied his movement.

The tiger immediately looked up warily, and after realizing it was a Stone Men, it lowered its head again.

It recognized this thing; it was just a rock, with no danger whatsoever.

Even if it pooped on its head, this thing wouldn't even budge.

Just as the tiger was still using its few brain cells to think, Yu Jiemo had already walked towards it.


#132 What to do if the stone man is hungry?

Chapter 132 What to do if the stone man is hungry?

“Little my grandma’s leg, you just don’t have a brain and can’t compare, this is clearly a tiger!”

“How is your family’s tiger so small!”

“Can’t you compare? You look at this thing and it doesn’t seem very big, but you don’t compare how tall Fengchu Brother actually is!”

“Compared to the flowers, plants, and trees on the ground, Fengchu Brother is at least ten meters tall!”

“Holy cow, that big!”

“And Fengchu Brother has a Stone body, I have to say, this race is a bit strong!”

“Silicon-based life forms, ten meters is actually not too big. According to our speculation, if silicon-based life forms exist in the universe, they should be at least thirty meters tall, basically the size of Optimus Prime.”

“Could it be that this Stone Men is still small and will grow bigger when he grows up?”

“Still don’t quite understand, but why isn’t Yu Gongzi speaking?”

“Probably autistic.”

“I’ve wondered about that. After all, one second he was the high and mighty heir of Qiangsheng Group, and even received God’s approval, then the next second he was a Traveler. Who could stand such a contrast?”

“You guys, is there a possibility that this Stone Men can’t speak?”

As soon as these words came out, the live broadcast room instantly fell silent.

They had never thought of this question.

After all, no matter which civilization or which Traveler before, as soon as they became a Traveler, the first thing they did was communicate with everyone.

Speaking seemed to be an instinct.

Now that such a Stone Men appeared, they didn't react for a moment.

“This, Fengchu Brother, you can’t really speak, can you?!”

“Holy cow, this is too tragic. Not only is he a Traveler, but he’s also a Traveler into a Stone Men who can’t speak…”

“No! Yu Gongzi, if you can’t speak, how will you inherit the company to me? Why don’t you write a copy by hand.”

“Yes, Yu Gongzi, please write me a copy of the inheritance will.”

“Get lost, Qiangsheng Group is mine!”

“Damn it, if you have the guts, let’s have a one-on-one fight!!!”

“…”

Every step Yu Jiemo took was accompanied by a thunderous roar, and a large pit would appear under his feet.

Although he was very careful every time he lifted and landed his foot, he still couldn't avoid it.

After all, his weight was there.

The mud on the ground simply couldn't bear his terrifying weight.

It was as if a person stepping in a paddy field, with every step, his legs would sink deep into the mud, and this was simply unavoidable.

The tiger looked up strangely at this Stone Men. Although it didn’t understand what the Stone Men was going to do, its heart still began to be vigilant.

It wasn’t afraid that the Stone Men would actively attack him.

He was afraid that the Stone Men would fall and crush him.

After all, with the Stone Men’s weight, if he fell, he would instantly turn into a meat paste.

Although Stone Men eat dirt and do not actively attack other creatures.

But their massive numbers are there. The number of animals crushed to death by them flipping over, stepped on by them walking, or even falling into the large pits they dug and dying is not small.

They are like elephants in nature, massive in size, with no natural predators.

“What is Fengchu Brother going to do?”

“Stupid, can’t you tell? He wants to catch that tiger and make it dinner.”

“By the way, do Stone Men also need to eat?”

“By the way, if Stone Men need to eat, what is their food?”

“Yeah, I also want to know that question.”

“Uh, I mean, don’t you feel strange? Every step Fengchu Brother takes, the ground shakes like an earthquake, but that tiger doesn’t run away, just watches him. If they feed on this thing, it shouldn’t be like this.”

“That’s right, this situation is very wrong.”

“This, could it be that this tiger is raised by this Stone Men?”

“It’s also possible, otherwise why isn’t the tiger afraid of him?”

Originally, Yu Jiemo was very happy to see that the tiger didn’t run away. At this time, he was already planning to directly slap the tiger to death.

But after seeing this bullet screen, he hesitated again.

This tiger isn’t afraid of him, could it really be raised by his Stone Men form?

This, wouldn’t it be too cruel to kill his own pet for a meal?

But he was really hungry.

He wanted to speak, but what came out of his mouth was a series of clanging sounds of stones colliding.

He wanted to ask the people in the live broadcast room what he should do now.

He couldn't even speak. Among so many Travelers, he was the most useless.

Thinking of this, he almost collapsed again.

“Why did Mo Ge stop moving?”

“I feel like he has something to say.”

“He really can’t speak!”

“Well, executives of Qiangsheng Group, listen up, Yu Jiemo just communicated with me through his eyes and wants to give Qiangsheng Group to me.”

“You’re farting, he clearly gave it to me!”

“Damn it, it was clearly me!”

“Stop arguing, it’s mine!”

Seeing that these people, after confirming that he couldn't speak, not only didn't help him find a solution but instead made sarcastic remarks, he almost fumed with anger.

In the end, he still didn’t kill the tiger.

He felt that this should be his pet, otherwise, how could the other party not be afraid of him?

It was just a pity that he was still hungry now and didn't know where to get some food for his little kitty.

Having been pampered with fine clothes and rich food since childhood, he had never experienced hunger before. Today was the first time in his life, and that feeling was truly unbearable.

He gently extended a huge Stone hand towards the tiger’s face.

The tiger paused, not understanding what the Stone Men was going to do.

Although he was puzzled by the Stone Men’s actions, he was still ready to run away at any time.

But this hand just stopped a little bit in front of him, neither wanting to attack him nor wanting to put him down.

The tiger was puzzled, then thought that this Stone Men was probably going to sleep again, just like before.

After all, Stone Men have all sorts of strange sleeping postures, so even a strange one is very normal.

After thinking for a moment, the tiger lowered its head, and then in the next moment, its back neck was grabbed by a large hand.

It struggled frantically, but it was useless.

His sharp teeth and claws had no effect on the Stone Men.

But this Stone Men didn’t seem to hurt him, he just placed him in front of him and looked at him twice, then extended another Stone hand, gently stroked the fur on his back, and then put him down again.

The moment the tiger landed, it immediately ran over thirty meters away.

Then he roared at Yu Jiemo.

After two roars, the tiger saw that Yu Jiemo didn’t pay attention to him, so he thought for a moment that the Stone Men hadn’t hurt him just now, and then he lay down again.

At this moment, Yu Jiemo was also confused. So, was this tiger his pet or not?

The tiger had been struggling so much just now that he thought he had hurt him, and he carefully checked to make sure he hadn't before putting him down.

Finally, watching the tiger run far away, Yu Jiemo no longer paid attention to him.

It was already deep night, and he was both hungry and sleepy.

But without food and without wilderness survival skills, he truly had no way out.

Although the people in the live broadcast room also taught him how to hunt, the Stone Men’s bulky size ensured that he couldn't move fast.

Not only was his movement slow, but every step he took would create a huge sound.

In this situation, it was impossible for him to succeed in hunting.

If he were to say he succeeded, perhaps, that was the most successful time just now.

But he let that tiger go.

At this moment, he even suspected that he would be the first Traveler to starve to death.

He wanted to find some of his kind, that is, his own people.

Based on the experience of previous Travelers, a Traveler would definitely have a race just like themselves.

But because it was too late, he didn’t know where to look.

Finally, he listened to the audience in the live broadcast room and decided to sleep first.

After all, the people in the live broadcast room said that if he slept, he wouldn’t be hungry.

Although he knew whether this method was reliable or not, in the end, having no other choice, he still decided to do so.

In the Little Universe, Su Mo shook his head slightly, looking at Yu Jiemo’s actions.

“After all, he is a Traveler. He is indeed different from other Stone Men.

He cannot connect to the collective will of the Stone Men, and he still has his own emotions.

However, having emotions in such a world is not a good thing.

Especially since several of your Old fellow are not easy to deal with, especially your master. I just don’t know what you will do next.”

After speaking, Su Mo no longer paid attention to this side, but looked at the other Stone Men.

He had already set the stage.

All that was left was for the Stone Men to perform.

The Stone Men also seemed to have realized that the secret realm was expanding, compressing their living space.

And the Mosquito were getting more numerous, and they were eating more.

But there was still plenty of food now, and they still planned to proceed according to the previous plan.

It was just that their dirt-eating time became longer, and their resting time became shorter.

The Mosquito were desperately eating dirt, and they were also desperately eating dirt.

The current situation was that even the edges were frantically eating dirt.

After an unknown amount of time, Yu Jiemo fell asleep. In the darkness, a colossal creature slowly approached from afar.

The people in the live broadcast room saw this scene and frantically sent bullet comments, then Yu Jiemo woke up.

The ground tremors grew larger and larger.

Finally, Yu Jiemo, who had not been sleeping very soundly, woke up.

As soon as he woke up, he saw a huge monster, completely black, passing by him.

The tiger from before had long since run off to who knew where.

This large monster, over two hundred meters long, didn't pay attention to the Stone Men who suddenly got up. He continued to move forward, even wanting to step over him.

At this moment, Yu Jiemo and the people in the live broadcast room finally discovered that this was an Ant!

An Ant that had already gone extinct in the Da Boluo era!

Their figures hadn’t even appeared in the last era, and now, they appeared once again on this land!

The moment he saw this large Ant, Yu Jiemo didn’t know if he was going crazy or was scared silly, but he raised his hand and struck out at the other party.


#133 I really don’t even want to act anymore!!!

Chapter 133 I really don’t even want to act anymore!!!

The enormous Ant only looked enormous, but its actual weight was less than his own.

For the same weight, one was three hundred meters tall, while the other was less than ten meters tall; the difference in their mass was imaginable.

It was just that the Stone Men didn't like to fight, nor did he bother to fight.

Otherwise, if they truly fought, even purely based on strength, they would dominate this world.

After all, for the same weight, their body sizes differed so much, like a height of ten meters but a weight of one hundred tons.

Then every meter of him would have a weight of ten tons.

And this didn't even account for smaller parts like his feet and head.

In other words, even if he merely lifted his hand, he would have to exert five or six tons of immense force.

With a slight push, it would be the so-called single arm swinging a hundred thousand catties, ah, no, a hundred thousand tons of immense force!

With a loud crack, the giant Ant’s exoskeleton instantly shattered like an egg.

Although his body wasn't sent flying, with just this one punch, his exoskeleton had already largely shattered.

The internal Ant meat, which resembled shrimp, instantly turned into a paste and then flowed out from the shattered parts of the exoskeleton.

The live stream audience was stunned by the scene before them.

This was the Ant that had fought Da Boluo for a lifetime!

Yet, Yu Jiemo killed him!

He was killed with just one punch!

Although the Ant was not dead yet, he had already certainly lost his combat capability.

The Ant’s huge body twitched continuously, and after a moment, it completely ceased to move.

Not to mention the audience in the live stream, even Yu Jiemo, who had just killed the Ant with one punch, was in a state of bewilderment.

Am I really that strong!?

Is this the legendary One-Punch Man!?

Is this, is this the golden finger I gained because of my status as a God’s Emissary!?

At this moment, not only did he think so, but everyone across the entire network thought the same.

Those who were previously gloating and saying that rich people deserved to be Travelers were now envious.

Why is his fate so good!

Why, despite his excellent family background, was he also granted the protection of God?

Wouldn't it have been better if he had just been a Traveler and then been killed by a monster from another world, or slapped to death by Xu Shilun!

Why did he have to be given such powerful strength!

You should know that all the Travelers they had seen before, when they first traveled, were basically starting from scratch.

Let alone facing a big guy like the Ant, even a natural disaster could pose a risk of extinction.

Don't look at how the Ultraman Civilization handled the solar storm with ease last time; that was entirely because Liu Dajun was a veteran player on his second playthrough.

If it were the First Era, let alone a solar storm, they had prepared for seven hundred years for just a great flood.

When Da Boluo faced the Ant back then, if he hadn't been decisive enough, leading a group of people into the Ant's nest with a do-or-die resolve, the Ashura truly could have faced extinction.

But what about this guy!?

Damn it, he just became a Traveler!

It hasn't even been a day, and he's spent most of that time in a daze!

He just went and single-handedly killed an Ant!

Look at the Ant’s size, and then look at yourself!

If he hadn't cheated, they wouldn't believe it even if they died!

If it were someone else, they might still wonder if it was due to a racial talent.

But for this guy, even if he didn't cheat, people would still say he did!

He's the heir to a hundred-billion-dollar fortune, converses with God, was chosen by God, faced an attack by an Evil God, protected by the God of Creation, traveled to another world, and killed the overlord of the previous era with one punch.

Just tell me, if one or two of these things appeared individually, they might feel like a coincidence, but for all of them to happen to one person, isn't that a bit too coincidental!?

Given the current situation, not only are others suspecting Yu Jiemo of cheating.

Even he himself suspects he cheated.

After all, with this situation, he can't explain it unless he cheated!

At this moment, he even began to wonder if he was the reincarnation of some powerful figure in his previous life.

So everyone else was giving him some face.

If that were the case, there would be even more reasons for the Evil God to attack him.

After all, if they had an old feud in a past life and he caused this situation again in this life, he felt that the Evil God didn't kill him only because he was strong enough in his previous life.

Otherwise, this simply couldn't be explained.

After a brief silence in the live stream, a surging wave of bullet comments suddenly erupted.

“Oh my god, big brother, you’re not even pretending anymore!”

“Others at least pretend when they cheat, but you’re not even pretending!”

“Big brother, did you recharge before becoming a Traveler? How much did you recharge? Can you tell me where the recharge portal is? I've been speaking a lot lately, and I want to recharge some too.”

“What nonsense are you talking about? Mo Ge is clearly not an ordinary person; he's not only a God’s Emissary but also blessed by heaven! This is clearly a person of great destiny!”

“Could it be the legendary reincarnation of a powerful figure!”

“Big brother, I beg you, please tell me how to cheat! I also want to cheat and have fun in another world.”

“Alright, Mo Ge, stop pretending. Let out your wings behind you. We all guessed it; you're a God’s Emissary, right? A VIP, right? We understand.”

“What VIP? Oh my god, no way, you’re not saying that his investment of a billion dollars to build the temple was a recharge, are you!?”

“Oh my god, no wonder Da Boluo and the others were so miserable; it turns out they didn’t recharge!”

“No, with that kind of recharge, Da Boluo and the others wouldn't be able to afford it!”

Yu Jiemo did not respond to the discussions in the comment section about whether he had cheated or not.

After all, with the way they were talking, he even wondered if it was true.

Was it because he was the reincarnation of a powerful figure, or because he invested a billion dollars to build the God of Creation Temple that he received such a benefit?

In any case, he didn't know and wouldn't answer.

Even if he wanted to answer, he couldn't speak, because the Stone Men's throat simply didn't support him doing such a thing.

After killing the Ant, Yu Jiemo came to the Ant's corpse, feeling his stomach, which was uncomfortably hungry.

After hesitating for a moment, he pulled off an Ant leg, put it to his mouth, and took a bite.

He didn't smell any blood, nor did he taste anything specific.

It just looked rather disgusting, but he didn't feel anything else.

But then again, no matter what, he was the young master of a hundred-billion-dollar corporation, accustomed to a life of luxury since childhood; the fact that he could eat this raw showed how much resolve he had made.

After eating one leg, Yu Jiemo finally no longer felt so hungry.

It's just that for some reason, after eating this, he felt a bit uncomfortable.

It seemed as if he couldn't digest it.

However, this was his first time eating since becoming a Traveler, and also his first time eating Ant meat, so he didn't know if this situation was normal.

The tiger, which had been scared away by the Ant earlier, smelled the blood and ran back.

It saw a Stone Men eating an Ant and showed a puzzled expression.

It cautiously approached; the Stone Men did not harm it, and the other party seemed to have no ill will towards it either.

Instead, he waved at it, but the tiger didn't understand what this meant.

Then it saw the Stone Men throw a large object towards it.

Without the slightest hesitation, the tiger immediately ran far away.

A moment later, it returned, and upon seeing that it was an Ant leg, it cautiously approached, bit it, and then ran away swiftly.

Yu Jiemo scratched his Stone head in confusion.

The people in the live stream seemed to understand his confusion and immediately sent bullet comments.

“Mo Ge, this thing doesn't seem to be your pet.”

“Yeah, it looks like it’s wild.”

“But it doesn’t matter. Whether it’s wild or not, it poses no threat to you because you’re made of stone, and it can’t bite through you. Even if it really wanted to harm you, you could slap it to death.”

“It’s fine to keep it, but you need to be careful not to scare it. Cats are very sensitive.”

After seeing these bullet comments, Yu Jiemo nodded, then continued to gnaw on an Ant leg before lying down again.

This time, he was truly tired.

At this moment, he truly fell asleep.

And he felt incredibly at ease, even more so than at home.

Because he felt that at this moment, he was an invincible existence.

Since he had received the grace of the God of Creation, perhaps no one in this world, except the Evil God, could be his opponent.

Of course, there was a prerequisite: excluding the other Travelers.

Yu Jiemo finally woke up at noon the next day.

The tiger, which was eating Ant meat, was startled by this scene and was about to run away, but then stopped.

It merely crouched, ready to run at any moment.

Yu Jiemo looked at the tiger, and the tiger also looked at Yu Jiemo; a Stone Men and a tiger just stared at each other.

A moment later, Yu Jiemo cautiously extended his hand, grabbed a piece of Ant meat, and threw it towards the tiger.

The tiger hesitated for a moment, then finally lowered its head and began to eat.

Seeing this, Yu Jiemo slowly reached out and touched the cat’s body.

The tiger paused its movements but did not resist; it just slowed down its eating speed.

After gently stroking the cat, Yu Jiemo threw out another large piece of good Ant meat, and then he also began to eat.

Although he still felt a bit off after eating, he thought it was because he hadn't cooked it properly.

But before, due to the simple conditions and the fact that he had just become a Traveler, he hadn't thought much about it.

Although he now had time, he wasn't planning to make a fire yet.

He intended to find his own kind and see how they lived.

If possible, he planned to learn from Da Boluo’s method of making fire, being elected as a wise man, and then becoming a tribal leader.

Then he carried the large Ant corpse and left.

The tiger behind him initially didn't want to follow, but Yu Jiemo would drop a piece of meat every so often, and the tiger slowly followed along.

Some other animals in the distance looked at this combination of a Stone Men, a tiger, and an Ant corpse, all bewildered, not knowing what was happening.

Initially, Yu Jiemo also didn't understand why those animals were looking at him with strange eyes.

Shouldn't they be afraid of him?

Why did they have such expressions?

But after walking for a while, when Yu Jiemo saw his first kinsman, he fell silent.


#134 A treasure from heaven and earth is born?

Chapter 134: The emergence of natural treasures?

Yu Jiemo also looked at the Stone Men lying splayed out in the large pit.

The Stone Men kept looking up at him, then turned back to eat a mouthful of dirt, and then continued to look back at him.

This Stone Men didn't understand why the guy on the surface was carrying a flesh and blood creature around.

He also didn't understand why this Stone Men wasn't eating, sleeping, and reproducing in his own territory, but instead came to his territory.

What he understood even less was why this Stone Men couldn't communicate.

So he could only look at the other party with such a puzzled gaze.

Boom!

The huge Ant corpse was thrown to the ground by Yu Jiemo, kicking up a cloud of dust.

After being startled, the tiger rushed to the Ant corpse and began to gnaw on it again.

But the Ant's shell was too hard; it couldn't bite through it at all and could only look at the Stone Men.

Yu Jiemo didn't even look at the tiger; he just silently glanced at the dirt at his feet, then at his fellow clansman in the large pit, and then silently picked up a handful of dirt, put it to his mouth, and took a small bite.

It tasted like nothing, just like eating plain white rice, but the indigestion he felt after eating flesh and blood no longer appeared.

At this moment, he understood.

The people in the live stream also understood.

Then, a burst of earth-shattering laughter erupted.

Hahaha~ I'm dying of laughter, I'm dying of laughter!!!!

I can't take it anymore, I really can't, hahaha...

Everyone, come and see, the heir of the multi-billion dollar Qiangsheng Group is eating dirt in another world!

I never thought this race would actually eat dirt!

True dirt-eating!

Thinking about it, there's nothing wrong with it. After all, Mo Ge is a Stone Men. If a Stone Men doesn't eat dirt or minerals, is he supposed to eat meat?

Oh my god, we were prejudiced. It really is like that.

Mo Ge, give me ten million, and I promise not to let anyone know you eat dirt.

Me too, me too.

Yu Jiemo fell silent after taking a bite of dirt, or rather, he had always been silent, because he couldn't speak at all.

Just then, the tiger came to his side, looked at him, then went to the Ant, indicating to Yu Jiemo that it couldn't bite through the shell.

Yu Jiemo silently broke off a part of the Ant's shell, then sat down next to it.

He dug up dirt with his hand and started eating.

The tiger finally felt that the Stone Men had become normal again when it saw Yu Jiemo resuming his dirt-eating.

After eating his fill, Yu Jiemo hoisted the Ant corpse onto his shoulder again and began to wander around, eating wherever he went.

After all, his food was right under his feet, food was everywhere.

And that tiger, after following him and eating Ant meat for a month, completely chose to follow him.

Eating Ant meat for a month didn't mean the Ant meat could only be eaten for a month. After all, a three-hundred-meter Ant, with only one tiger eating it, couldn't possibly be finished in a month.

The main reason was that after a month, the Ant meat had already gone bad.

Although Yu Jiemo had no nose and couldn't smell it, he understood the situation after seeing the tiger refuse to eat and the explanations from the live stream's bullet comments.

After that, he killed another Ant and began to wander with the tiger.

Half a year passed in a flash. At this time, although Yu Jiemo and the live stream audience hadn't fully understood this world yet.

But they roughly understood the situation of this race.

All Stone Men were the same; except for moving around for a period when reproducing, they basically stayed in their pits eating dirt.

Some Stone Men wouldn't even come out to reproduce if they could find so many stones in their own dirt pits.

Only Yu Jiemo was an exception, carrying an Ant or some other animal corpse on his shoulder, with a sleek, shiny tiger following him.

Before, they had suspected that Yu Jiemo was cheating, but after seeing his comparison with the surrounding trees, they understood.

It turns out it wasn't that Yu Jiemo was cheating, but that this race was indeed extraordinary.

What they had previously thought was a cat, someone now confirmed was a tiger.

Although it now looked no different from a cat.

For example, when Yu Jiemo slept, this tiger liked to lie on his chest and sleep.

Sometimes, when it didn't want to walk, it would directly climb up his leg, all the way to his head, and then lie down on his head.

When hungry, it could even directly bite into the food next to it a couple of times. Life couldn't be more comfortable.

Stone Men have extraordinary physiques; besides having to eat dirt when hungry, even carrying an Ant and a tiger for a day and a night wouldn't tire them.

Of course, sometimes they would get sleepy. The people in the live stream analyzed that perhaps this was just how Stone Men were.

After all, many of the Stone Men they saw were either eating dirt or sleeping.

So they felt that it was fine for Stone Men to be sleepy.

There was also another possibility: that Yu Jiemo's human instincts hadn't been fully released, because whether it was three meals a day, or waking up in the morning and sleeping at night, these were all human habits.

Logically speaking, Stone Men shouldn't have such habits.

But none of this was important.

The former Yu Jiemo was the heir of a multi-billion dollar listed group, living an incomparably carefree life.

Everyone originally thought that after he transmigrated, he would suffer, but they didn't expect him to still be carefree.

Currently, whether it's Mosquitoes, or Ants, or some small animals with extraordinary power, none of them are a match for him.

Stone Men are an invincible existence in this world.

Moreover, their food is dirt and minerals, so they basically don't have disputes with other life forms. The only one that competes with them is the Mosquito.

But how can you fight over land? Everyone eats their own, there's no impact at all.

It can be said that they have no natural enemies at all.

Every day, he would take a tiger and some dry food for the tiger, travel around and enjoy the scenery of this other world.

From time to time, he would also use a pen made from a tree branch to write and communicate with his companions in the live stream.

He was like a Traveler in another world, constantly recording everything here and sharing it with his companions on Earth.

No pressure, no worries.

It could be said that life was boundless joy!

...

In a blink of an eye, another three years passed, and the number of Stone Men doubled once again. At this time, the number of Stone Men had already reached eighty million.

One must know that it hasn't even been twenty years since Su Mo's creation of the world, and the reproduction of Stone Men has only reached its third cycle.

At the very beginning, when Su Mo created the world, he released ten million Stone Men. The first reproduction was five years later, at which point the number reached twenty million!

The second reproduction was in the tenth year, with a number of forty million!

Now, in the fifteenth year, the number has doubled again!

Eighty million!

Such a reproduction speed can simply be described as outrageous!

They are different from other flesh and blood creatures; as long as their own mass reaches a certain standard, and they find a stone, they can reproduce through asexual reproduction.

Each time, it's a doubling!

Eating continuously every day, they can reproduce once every five years.

No fighting, no killing, and no natural enemies!

They just quietly hide in a corner, constantly devouring the matter of this world and transforming it into their own kind.

Their numbers are constantly increasing, and the matter on the continent is constantly decreasing.

If this continues, they will be able to devour this entire continent in less than a hundred years.

They truly have no natural enemies, but Su Mo has already created natural enemies for them.

Mosquitoes, and the previous veteran Travelers.

The desire to devour matter will inevitably collide with the Travelers' secret realms.

That is to say, none of those guys from before have revived yet, otherwise they would be the first to confront these Stone Men.

Because, except for Xu Shilun, everyone else will definitely revive their clansmen after resurrection.

If there are human clansmen, then land will be needed...

Yu Jiemo has also been in this world for five years.

During this time, he witnessed many things.

He saw Xu Shilun's clone, Dark Tiga.

The moment he saw that thing, he didn't hesitate for a second and turned to run.

Later, after long-term observation, he found that these Dark Tiga had not revived and seemed to only perform instinctive self-protection.

He also saw the scriptures written by Xu Shilun and exposed them in the live stream.

The people in the live stream almost burst out laughing when they saw these scriptures.

Then countless people cursed his shamelessness.

Not only did he glorify himself, slander Ashura, blame the Soul Clan, but he also tried to trick others into reviving him.

Especially when they saw that 'Luminous Shilen was tainted by ominousness due to suppressing the darkness, leading him into eternal darkness, his whole body pitch black, and only by liberating Shilen's true soul could he reappear in the light,' countless people almost spat out their drinks.

This guy truly stopped at nothing to be revived.

You could understand him glorifying himself like this to get others to revive him.

But slandering Ashura was something others couldn't tolerate.

Xu Shilun, in order to prevent others from reviving Ashura or Liu Dajun first, also went to great lengths in slandering.

In short, it was one sentence: revive the God of Light and receive the protection of the God of Light.

Revive the Evil God, and the world will be destroyed!

In this world, apart from the God of Luminous Shilen, all others are evil gods!

All the suffering you endure is because you have not rescued the God of Light.

At first, everyone was still cursing Xu Shilun, but they kept feeling that this thing looked a bit familiar.

Then, after a bit more research, they found that other religions seemed to follow the exact same pattern...

Four Travelers and eighty secret realms spread across this world, which is thirty times the size of Earth, is not particularly many.

It's even very possible that one might not see a single fragmented secret realm for millions of square kilometers.

On this day, Yu Jiemo arrived in a special forest.

The trees in the forest were generally over thirty meters tall, and around them, strange-looking animals, noticeably one or two times larger than animals outside, each occupied a designated area.

While they were wary of other animals around them, they were also paying attention to the animals outside.

If any other animal tried to enter, they would jointly kill it, but if one of the animals inside was injured, the other animals would also gang up on it.

Because they were also enemies!

Upon seeing such a scene, an idea immediately popped into Yu Jiemo's mind.

When a heavenly treasure appears, there must be a strange beast guarding it!


#135 Big Pineapple or Xu Shilun?

If he’s not the son of Heaven, I don’t believe it!”

Following this, the live stream chat was filled with envious comments.

Yu Jiemo didn’t continue watching.

Sure enough, these netizens thought the same as him.

Could it be that this is a cultivation world?

No, if it were a cultivation world, it shouldn’t be like this.

So, what exactly is going on?

Just as he was thinking this, a gigantic python, two meters thick, suddenly stood upright at a position closest to the center. Its upright body was even half a head taller than Yu Jiemo.

Yu Jiemo paused. He had been in this world for a long time.

He had encountered many strange and bizarre things.

He had seen snakes before.

But even a regular python, straightened out, wouldn’t be as tall as him.

He could easily stomp such a creature to death.

But the one in front of him was completely different.

Most of its body was still coiled on the ground, yet just by raising its head, it was already ten meters tall.

Looking at the snake’s cold vertical pupils, he suspected that this thing, straightened out, would be at least fifty meters long!

Fifty meters, to be honest, wasn’t particularly huge in this world, after all, both Ants and Mosquitoes were massive creatures that had surpassed a hundred meters.

Yu Jiemo could punch such things to death, and even if one punch didn’t kill them, two punches would be enough.

So, he wasn’t particularly scared, just found it interesting.

Because in these five years, he had already discovered something.

His identity as a Stone Men was simply a panacea; almost no animals were afraid of or hostile towards him.

Even when he got close, it was the same. At most, they would just be wary of not being stepped on.

But that chance was as low as a rock suddenly rolling down a mountain and crushing them to death.

It was only now that he was carrying Mimi; otherwise, many animals wouldn’t be afraid of him.

This was the first time an animal had shown hostility towards him.

“Mo Ge, why don’t you go in and take a look? With your strength, you can just punch them all to death, one by one. Whatever good stuff is in there, get it first.”

“I think it’s best not to rush. Look at these animals, each one is several times larger than those outside. Perhaps they all possess some extraordinary power.”

“Uh, Mo Ge hasn’t seen extraordinary power before? Water-spraying little rabbits, leaf-controlling little bugs, rock-shattering big bears, fire-breathing wild oxen, what hasn’t he seen? What if they have extraordinary power? Mo Ge can still punch them one by one.”

“I know, but I feel like these might be stronger.”

“The Chosen One is the strongest. No matter how strong they are, can they be stronger than Mo Ge? Mo Ge, go!”

“Yes, Mo Ge, charge in and let us broaden our horizons, see what Heaven and Earth Treasures look like.”

“Or perhaps not. I see Mimi is bristling.”

“Indeed, Mimi seems very afraid of them. Why don’t you find a place to put Mimi down first?”

Yu Jiemo thought for a moment, then protected Mimi, who was perched on his shoulder, with one hand and walked inside.

He was a Stone Men, not afraid of being attacked.

Moreover, he was truly thick-skinned and strong, anyone who dared to fight him would be sent to their demise with one punch.

Actually, if it were just him, he wouldn’t care about such things.

At most, he would just look out of curiosity.

It was still for the previous reason, because he was a Stone Men, eating plants would make him feel uncomfortable.

But Mimi was different; he was truly getting old.

If possible, he hoped to find something to extend Mimi’s lifespan.

Thinking this, he lifted his leg and walked inside.

Accompanied by a series of rumbling sounds, with every step Yu Jiemo took, a huge deep pit would appear on the ground.

The commotion immediately attracted the attention of countless animals.

Some animals, seeing that it was a Stone Men, immediately moved aside, making way for him.

After all, this thing didn’t like to fight and ate dirt.

It had no competition with them.

As long as they made way and ensured they weren’t stepped on, it was fine.

Only the dozen or so massive creatures in the very center all glared at Yu Jiemo with hostility.

The giant python flicked its tongue, emitting hissing sounds.

A giant bear, nearly twenty meters tall, suddenly stood up.

A giant elephant, over forty meters tall, raised its trunk and let out a roar.

Yu Jiemo ignored them directly and continued forward.

All those big guys stared intently at Yu Jiemo, but upon seeing him truly walking towards them, none of them attacked.

Because it was a Stone Men, they didn’t know where to start.

Moreover, a Stone Men was just lazy and didn’t like to move, which didn’t mean he was truly weak.

It couldn’t be seen from the outside, but upon reaching the innermost part, he discovered a transparent barrier in front.

At the very front, there was a large hole, from which wisps of black gas continuously emerged.

But looking through the barrier, the inside was no different from the outside: the same lush green forest, the same bright sunshine.

It was just that there weren’t as many animals inside.

As soon as the black gas emerged, Yu Jiemo instinctively took a step back, but the other giant beasts directly used various methods to disperse the black gas.

After absorbing the black gas, their bodies visibly grew a little larger.

Seeing this, a question popped into his mind.

They became this big because they absorbed this black gas!

But this black gas didn’t seem like any kind of Heaven and Earth Treasure.

#136 Crouching Dragon and Phoenix travel through time together!

But he turned out to be all talk.

He had many ideas and a broad mind.

One to strategize, and one to implement.

This was just like the ancient Demon Court; they also had two kings!

And Mosquitoes, there were simply too many of them.

But, who should he choose?

Su Mo thought, conjuring a small notebook out of thin air, filled with many usernames.

“Here they come, here they come, volunteering to leave their names!”


#139 Difficult communication!

Second, directly enter the dungeon and gamble. If they won, they would ascend to heaven; if they lost, they would ascend directly to heaven!

He indeed couldn't control his own power, nor could he use his consciousness to control Dark Tiga.

In fact, as long as one entered the instance, those Dark Tiga would attack everyone indiscriminately.

So he thought about going out first to see, and then carefully made his way out from the central area.

It took him more than five years just to get from the inside to the outside.

Actually, the reason he could escape from inside was because he didn't even know how many times he had done this.

Whenever an accident occurred and his will perished, he would reawaken when another anomaly appeared.

But after reawakening, he had no memory of this place at all.

Their memories still remained at the last moment of self-destruction.

But those paths eventually ended.

That's how he knew the previous methods had failed.

After enduring countless hardships to get outside, he found that he still couldn't leave this place.

To leave, he had to bring his fragments with him.

#142 Only in, not out!

In other words, he could swallow infinitely!

Before the Fourth Tier, he was constantly absorbing power, from Soul Liquid to darkness, and then to Otto energy.

After the Fourth Tier, his body autonomously absorbed energy from the universe, which is why he could maintain the Fourth Tier.

Anyway, once they all broke through to the fifth rank, and his own strength allowed him to leave this Little Universe, he would immediately destroy this entire universe!

It was because these guys had snuck in that he was also forced to stay in this Little Universe with them.

Otherwise, if he could just play on his phone outside, watch short videos, sleep, wake up, and then periodically come in to harvest a wave, wouldn_t that be better than anything else?

Why stay here with these guys for so many years!

Fifteen years!

If he were outside, it would be over in the time it took him to eat a meal.

But now, he was stuck here for fifteen years!

Thinking of this, Su Mo would definitely be lying if he said he wasn_t angry!

After all, even a clay figure has a temper, let alone a god!

Therefore, what he said, no, what the Evil God said, would absolutely not be fulfilled!!!

Think about it, if Da Boluo knew that completing the task could resurrect his wife, son, and former clansmen.

Then, wouldn_t this be the legendary super workhorse who only needed one drawn-up plan per hundred kilometers!

Moreover, it was just a drawn-up plan, not actually giving anything; if he didn_t complete it, there was no need to give.

If he completed it, he would just turn around and destroy the universe, no, perhaps he could change it.

The great God of Creation fulfilled his request, resurrecting his wife, child, and all his clansmen.

Then, just as they were about to reunite, he would bring about the end of the world!

Yes, that_s how it should be; only then could he vent the evil in his heart!

If Da Boluo could be handled this way, could Liu Dajun also be given a similar treatment?

Tell him that Otto V, Zoffy, and the original Ultraman could be resurrected; wouldn_t that be another nuclear-powered donkey!

One must know, this was no longer their era; even if they were resurrected, whether they would help Wolong Wenhe, or how they would help them, was still a question.

But now with the plan he had drawn up, they would surely be loyal and without complaint!

After all, from what Su Mo saw, with their current strength, it was truly not easy to raise their power to the fifth rank before the Stone Men destroyed the world.

It could even be said that the possibility was minuscule, almost impossible!

The Stone Men would devour the continent in one hundred and fifty years, and that was counting the dungeon_s consumption speed; if it was included, one hundred years was the limit.

In one hundred years, the Stone Men breaking through to the fifth rank was a certainty.

But for the demon race, it was truly hard to say.

After all, as the later dungeons and the Stone Men devoured more and more land, their living space would become smaller and smaller.

Moreover, with less land, various natural disasters would continuously occur.

They not only had to face the powerful Stone Men, but also various natural disasters, food shortages, scarce resources, and all sorts of other problems.

And they still had to break through to the fifth rank within a limited time; this was truly not easy.

Therefore, to achieve this, they must have enough strength in the early stages to suppress the Stone Men_s development.

Otherwise, the Stone Men would later be like a snowball rolling down a mountain, not only getting bigger but also becoming increasingly difficult to stop!

So, having these Travelers help was indispensable.

Da Boluo could be handled with Yu Ji and his child.

Liu Dajun was Otto V, Zoffy, and the original Ultraman.

Wang Jianzhi was even simpler; it was just Ashura.

Finally, back to Xu Shilun.

This guy was the hardest to deal with; he had no attachments in this world, which meant he was invulnerable!

Just as he thought of this, Su Mo suddenly shook his head; he had thought wrong.

Others could be given a drawn-up plan because they would fall for it.

As for Xu Shilun, it was even simpler; if he didn_t fall for the plan, then he would get the whip!

It was like an employer drawing up a plan for you, and if you didn_t take it, then the employer could only give you a good beating from society.

Moreover, as the mastermind behind the stowaway plan in the previous epoch, this guy wasn_t even qualified to fall for the plan!

He only deserved the whip!

While Zhang Jiao was helping him wield the whip, he could also give him a promise similar to Da Boluo_s, helping him resurrect his clansmen.

After all, the original Zhang Jiao rose to power by leading his clansmen in resistance.

The third-rank Zhang Jiao continuously moved through Xu Shilun_s dungeons, absorbing his power, then beating the Xu Shilun within them until he cried for his parents, killing him again and again, making him feel true despair!

This was the blessing that someone who dared to scheme against me, the God of Creation, deserved!!!

And given what Xu Shilun had done, even if other Travelers saw his miserable state, no one would ever offer help.

Not only would they not help, they would even applaud!

Su Mo mentally simulated the upcoming plot development and felt like laughing a little.

He had originally found this Little Universe boring and uninteresting, but if the plot really unfolded as he envisioned, perhaps it would truly be very interesting!

Thinking this, he looked back into Da Boluo_s dungeon.

At this moment, the Ant Queen was still communicating with Da Boluo.

"I don_t know why I resurrected. My last memory was of the moment of death, and the next moment I was already in this world.

After arriving in this world, I didn_t think much and began to reproduce worker ants to build nests and soldier ants to hunt. Five years passed like this.

Actually, during that time, I had already discovered your existence, but I wasn_t particularly sure.

After all, I only sensed your aura, and in my memory, you should have already died.

Most importantly, your aura had also changed somewhat from before, so I couldn_t be certain.

Later, I discovered many stone tablets in some ruins, and the Mosquito written on them was the same one you taught me before.

I found many records that mentioned your existence, although many parts were inconsistent, and many things were not real, but I felt that the person described in the records should be you."

Da Boluo asked in confusion, "What ancient texts?"

To be honest, Da Boluo was quite curious about this stuff.

For a moment, he even forgot about resurrecting Yu Ji.

He really wanted to know how he was described by later generations.

And who was the person who recorded this.

Could it be his admirer?

The Ant Queen feeling it wasn_t real should be because his admirer described him too well.

And it had a grudge against him, so it felt it wasn_t real, right?

Thinking this, Da Boluo instantly understood.

His nature is warlike, evil, depraved, resentful, feeding on the flesh and blood of all races, fond of tearing souls and rending spirits, the king of evil Ashura..."

Da Boluo listened to this description, utterly bewildered. Was this talking about him???

Was this how he appeared in others_ eyes!!!

Just listening to this description, if it weren_t for the fact that it could be linked to his experiences, he would have suspected it was describing some heinous, peerless demon!

Ashura might not be good-looking, but for you to say fierce and ugly, isn_t that a bit much!!!

He did indeed have four arms, but when did he ever like flying naked? Uh, wait, when he was showing off back then, it seemed... but no one should know about that, right?

No, he absolutely couldn_t admit to this; he would never admit to liking to fly naked.

Ashura is warlike, his power comes from resentment, and it_s fine to say he_s evil, eating the flesh and blood of all races is just eating meat, and that part about liking to tear souls and rend spirits, damn it, you even care about me tearing my own soul!

And who taught you to write like this!!!

No, who the hell wrote this!!!

Thinking of this, Da Boluo looked at the Ant Queen with suspicious eyes. Could these things have been written by the Ant Queen herself?

Just as he thought this, he heard the Ant Queen about to continue reciting, and he instantly interrupted, "Wait, don_t read anymore. This is absolutely slander. It must be my enemy who specifically wrote this to slander me."

The Ant Queen nodded upon hearing this. "I think so too. After all, even though you and we are enemies, it_s just a difference in position, and in my understanding, you_re not that kind of person either."

"Uh..."

She had resurrected!

Da Boluo, after learning that the Ant Queen didn_t know how she resurrected, was silent for a moment before speaking, "My soul is somewhat special. After our epoch, the Ashura Civilization reappeared, and the Ultraman Civilization that existed before us also appeared. Then I resurrected. There are many things involved, and I_ll tell you about them slowly when the time comes..."

Da Boluo had just spoken halfway when his vision was suddenly pulled up, appearing in the universe. Looking up, there was an infinitely vast and terrifying existence. He merely stood there, as if he were the center of the universe.

The moment he saw the other party, the words 【God of Creation】 instantly appeared in his mind!


#149 Big Pineapple talks to the God of Creation!

Chapter 149: Big Pineapple talks to the God of Creation!

Su Mo quietly looked at Da Boluo, seemingly discerning his thoughts, and conveyed a message.

"I am the Creator of the Universe, the God of Creation of this realm. I have witnessed your experiences, and now I hope you can help me fight against the Evil God."

Su Mo's words were like the sound of the Great Dao, and the entire universe resonated.

"Fight against the Evil God? You, the Creator of this Universe, cannot fight against the Evil God, how can you ask me to help you!"

"And as the Creator of this world, why do you watch the Evil God destroy everything in this world!"

"The Evil God is a great demon from outside the heavens, waging cosmic wars, farming civilizations, and growing by devouring the destinies of living beings.

Therefore, I continuously create worlds, develop civilizations, and give them a trial every thousand years. Those who pass the trial can develop rapidly in this universe.

Because only strong enough races can survive in this dangerous world.

If they are not strong enough, after a thousand years, when their civilization is unsealed, even if I don't act, their ultimate fate will only be to be treated as slaves or made into food.

Rather than continuing to live such a sorrowful existence, it is better to be destroyed in the trial.

Even if they don't pass the trial, I have already given them life and a thousand years of time."

Su Mo spoke silently, like an emotionless machine.

His words were like a heavenly sound, making the entire universe tremble.

He was the ruler of this universe!

The reason he said all this was mainly because the bug of the Evil God needed a patch.

Otherwise, the God of Creation creates while the Evil God constantly destroys; isn't that slapping the God of Creation's face?

Otherwise, the Evil God's actions would be truly inexplicable.

Even if he kept acting, it wouldn't help.

Otherwise, once they had their own ideas, it would be very difficult to change their entrenched thinking if he then tried to explain these settings.

Da Boluo said nothing about the God of Creation's statement of a trial every thousand years.

After all, he had already discovered this from the previous stone tablets.

Whether it was the destruction of the seven previous civilizations or other events, it seemed to be the case.

If it was truly as the God of Creation said, that after a thousand years, when civilizations were unsealed, those weaker civilizations would be treated as slaves and food by other civilizations, then it truly would be better to die directly in the trial.

This way, at least they wouldn't suffer too much pain.

Su Mo had already prepared for this question.

If an outsider like you breaks through to the fifth-tier, it cannot be considered a civilization breakthrough.

Do you have any more questions now?"

Let alone discussing any conditions with him.

Before the other party even spoke, Da Boluo would have exploded with him!

No, that's wrong. He wouldn't even need the other party to appear before him. Even if a figure appeared as before, he would directly commit suicide!

He, Da Boluo, would never grovel before his enemy!

Even if it meant death, it would be so!

Because he was the Ashura King!

It was also because Su Mo was currently too bored being trapped in the Little Universe.

Otherwise, he wouldn't have bothered to talk so much with Da Boluo.

He would have just given a task and been done with it.

Giving a task was at most like what he did to Xu Shilun and the others before, just for fun.

But now he couldn't, he didn't have that much time.

Moreover, being alone in the Little Universe was boring, otherwise, he wouldn't have said so much.

They don't need to break through to the fifth-tier, or rather, they don't need to win; they just need not to lose.

Directly destroying the world means no one wins, it's a draw, and then the Evil God can still extend his hand!

Indeed, this is truly worthy of the Evil God's handiwork!

Truly disgusting!
