Chapter 151

Suddenly, Yu Jiemo's movements froze, his eyes staring straight at those two unusual mosquitoes.

These mosquitoes were at least twice the size of other mosquitoes.

Just looking at the patterns on their bodies made Yu Jiemo feel itchy all over.

If these mosquitoes bit someone in the real world, they would definitely leave a huge bump.

The lethality of these things was almost on par with bees.

A bee sting's swelling would go down in a day, but a bite from this large patterned Mosquito wouldn't subside for three to five days.

Yu Jiemo had seen other mosquitoes before.

But he had never observed them closely; after all, no one on Earth liked these things.

Only the Ashura of this world had a special fondness for them.

Looking at these two giant mosquitoes now, Yu Jiemo began to observe them carefully for the first time.

Because he knew these two were different from ordinary mosquitoes.

It wasn't just their larger size; it was because these two shells contained souls that came from the same place as him.

Before this, they had even served under the same person.

Although back then, they only treated it as a joke, something done for fun.

But the moment they traveled to this world, everything changed.

“Phoenix Fledgling, come out and chat.”

A mental intent was sent out, and Yu Jiemo received it in the next instant.

Yu Jiemo fell silent for a moment and nodded slightly: “Fine.”

As soon as he spoke, Wolong Wenhe, the Two Mosquitoes, were stunned.

“You can speak?”

“No, you can also communicate mentally!”

Yu Jiemo slowly stood up: “If you both can speak, why can't I?”

As he spoke, he slowly walked outside.

Seeing this, Wolong Wenhe immediately took flight, heading toward the sky.

Just as they took off, Yu Jiemo suddenly grabbed a giant wild boar over thirty meters long beside him. With a surge of strength in his hands, he ripped it in half, blood gushing out instantly.

In the next moment, he used his strength to throw the halves directly in the direction of Wolong Wenhe.

The two sections of the giant boar's carcass flew out in straight lines like flesh-and-blood cannonballs, reaching Wolong Wenhe's side in an instant.

The extreme speed erupted in a terrifying sonic boom, distorting the air around them.

Wolong Wenhe immediately turned around; one Mosquito grabbed with six arms while the other hugged tight with three, using a 360-degree mid-air rotation to bleed off the momentum of the boar-stone.

At the same time, their dark mouthparts pierced into the boar-stone and sucked; a piece of boar skin as thin as a cicada's wing slowly fell from them.

Wolong Wenhe, the Two Mosquitoes, flew in mid-air, looking down at Yu Jiemo, who was covered in blood from tearing the boar apart:

“We've worked together for so many years, after all. It's not very nice to give us such a greeting right off the bat.”

Yu Jiemo sneered: “Isn't this a meeting gift for you? I know you like eating this stuff. See how good I am to you.”

“Haha, then I really must thank Fengchu Brother. I ate very well this meal. So, can we have a proper chat now?”

“Chat? Chat about what? About how you betrayed me in front of Xu Shilun?

Or about how you want to stab me in the back?

I might not know what you've been up to lately, but all the previous news has already reached me. Do you think I don't know what you want to do or what you're planning!”

Wolong Wenhe went silent for a moment before speaking: “You know me. Given the situation then, I had no choice.

Besides, this time only the three of us can live-stream. We are natural allies; we should be cooperating with each other.

As for what was said to Xu Shilun, that was purely a matter of expediency.”

Wenhe: “Exactly. Although our current positions and factions are different, we all came from Earth!

We are natural allies. Furthermore, by traveling to this world and putting in some effort, everyone can have a lifespan of ten thousand years. Don't you want to live longer?”

Yu Jiemo: “Keep talking, see if you can move me.

You think I don't know? Your mission is to eliminate me and the Stone Men.

Under these circumstances, I'd like to see how you plan to convince me.”

Wenhe: “I've thought about it carefully. The reason so many tragic things happened to Xu Shilun and the others in the last era was mainly because the three Travelers and Da Boluo didn't communicate well in the early stages.

But we are different. We are all on the same planet and come from the same place. We can communicate and negotiate properly.

Any problem can be discussed; there's absolutely no need for a life-and-death struggle.

Even if we really have to fight to the death, we can try to drag it out as long as possible so that everyone can live longer, right?”

Phoenix Fledgling sneered: “So many things happened...”

Originally, he wanted to say that Xu Shilun did so many inhumane things precisely because you two were giving him advice.

But he swallowed the words back.

It seemed he had also given advice to Xu Shilun back then; if he cursed them, he'd be including himself.

After a long silence, Phoenix Fledgling spoke again: “Tell me your plan.”

“The three of us join forces to kill Xu Shilun first. We can deal with what comes after that later.”

“Kill Xu Shilun? How? Give me a method.”

“The current Xu Shilun is just a fragment. Even if he has power, he definitely won't be too strong. Moreover, you Stone Men have a collective consciousness and mental protection, so you aren't afraid of Xu Shilun's attacks at all.

The three of us will kill the Xu Shilun in a dungeon together, then divide his power. How about it?”

Yu Jiemo was stunned when he heard this.

Do Stone Men have a collective consciousness?

And mental protection too?

Why didn't I know?

Over the years, Yu Jiemo had tried to communicate with more than one Stone Man he found, but it never worked.

Every time, those Stone Men would look at him with a look like they were looking at an idiot.

There was even a hint of pity in their eyes, as if saying, 'How did this kid turn stupid at such a young age?'

He could clearly feel that Stone Men possessed intelligence, but no matter how he tried to communicate, he returned empty-handed.

But now Wolong said that Stone Men had a collective consciousness. This was truly the first time he had heard of it.

However, if Stone Men had a collective consciousness, why hadn't he felt it?

He felt nothing at all.

Over these years, if it hadn't been for Mimi and the friends in the live-stream room accompanying him, he would have gone crazy from loneliness long ago.

Phoenix Fledgling was a very smart person himself. After hearing Wolong's claim, he only felt puzzled for a moment before figuring out the reason.

The reason he couldn't connect to the Stone Men's consciousness was likely because he was a Traveler.

His soul was different from other Stone Men, so he was being isolated!

And if what they said was true, although his physical body was invincible, his soul remained his weakness.

If they used soul attacks, his soul wouldn't be able to resist at all.

Especially when facing that guy Xu Shilun—his source of strength was the soul, even though he had become a consciousness entity when he joined Otto.

But later, he somehow found a way to re-condense his soul body.

If this guy used a soul attack on him, he might have his soul annihilated in a single encounter!

Therefore, he absolutely could not let them know that he was isolated and rejected by the Stone Men, lacked a collective consciousness, and didn't have the ability for soul protection.

Thinking of this, he kept his face expressionless.

Mainly because Stone Men couldn't make many complicated expressions anyway.

After thinking everything through, Yu Jiemo sneered once more:

“Heh, you've really got it all figured out, haven't you? Your calculations are so loud I can hear them from here.

Telling me to go to Xu Shilun's dungeon—don't think I don't know you guys were tricked by him and have self-destruct systems installed in you.

The task he gave you was to bring a Stone Man to find him.

Since you couldn't fool the other Stone Men, you've come to try and fool me!

Nice plan. You say we'll join forces against Xu Shilun, but once we enter the dungeon, it's hard to say if it'll be the three of us against him or the three of you against me!”

“Mo Ge, what will it take for you to believe us? We really came with sincerity!”

“Believe you? How can I believe you?

Is it your Mosquito bodies, your mission to destroy the Stone Men, or the demands of that old fox Xu Shilun?

Given all that, what part of you is worth my trust!

Now, immediately, right now, get lost. I've claimed this spot. Next time we meet, don't blame me for showing no mercy.”

After saying that, Yu Jiemo ignored the Two Mosquitoes, turned around, and returned to Mimi's side, protectively placing her behind him.

Wolong and Wenhe left. In fact, before coming here, they had mostly guessed that the plan might not succeed, but they still wanted to try, unwilling to give up.

They had actually thought it through before coming.

If Yu Jiemo really agreed to go see Xu Shilun with them...

When the fight really started, they would just slack off on the sidelines and help whichever side gained the upper hand.

As for whether to help Xu Shilun deal with Yu Jiemo or help Yu Jiemo deal with Xu Shilun... since the talk failed, they decided to start their second plan.

Recruiting troops and buying horses!

And acting separately!

Wolong would head overseas to the Mosquito headquarters, the female Mosquito breeding base!

They wanted to obtain a large number of Mosquito soldiers there.

Although they couldn't possess them, they could still communicate using mental links if the mosquitoes lacked intelligence.

Even simple communication would be much better than their current state.

Wenhe would head to various dungeons to trick animals that had just gained intelligence—but weren't very smart yet—into joining the Demon Race.

At this time, not long after creating Zhang Jiao, Su Mo suddenly discovered a problem.

His disguise seemed about to be exposed!!!


#152 Su Mo: I am simply a genius! (Evil God)

Chapter 152 Su Mo: I am simply a genius! (Evil God)

First, he, who was trapped in this Little Universe, had been taking action too frequently recently.

Previously, whether it was the Evil God or the God of Creation, they basically wouldn't check the situation inside for decades or even centuries.

But this time, after being trapped here, although he had been constantly watching, the number of his interventions had clearly increased.

One must know that the Stone Men had appeared for less than twenty years, and Su Mo had already taken action so many times.

The more action he took, the more likely something would go wrong.

Second, he was still too careless.

His mindset had not yet shifted.

Before, he could indeed sit in his courtyard, browse videos, look at beautiful women, and let the people in the Little Universe fight to the death.

He only needed to harvest at fixed times.

So his mindset was still that he was invincible in his own Little Universe, that he was the God of Creation.

Therefore, he acted without scruples.

But it could also be said that his mindset had already changed.

Because he was anxious!

Precisely because he was anxious, he did more things!

After Wolong Wenhe traveled through, it was a mission given to them by the Evil God.

The mission was to destroy the Stone Men.

When he communicated with Da Boluo before, the God of Creation indeed requested the destruction of the Stone Men.

This was a huge bug!

If this problem was not solved, as soon as they communicated, it would surely be exposed.

So, this problem must be solved.

But how was it to be solved?

For a moment, Su Mo fell silent.

The simplest way to solve a problem was to eliminate the person who discovered the problem.

Directly destroying the universe was the simplest.

However, not to mention that there wasn't enough time now, even if there was ample time, facing such a civilization that would definitely break through to the fifth stage in less than a day, Su Mo was reluctant to destroy it now.

The Stone Men was different from other species.

The previous civilizations, not to mention the Da Boluo period, even in the last period, so many people broke through to the fourth stage.

But if they lost pressure and motivation, even if they were given a day, or even a week, they might not be able to break through to the fifth stage.

Not to mention the previous civilizations.

At that time, Ashura developed steadily, Xu Shilun became the King of the Three Races, and Liu Dajun hid in the Land of Light.

In that situation, only the Evil God could give them pressure.

Moreover, in that situation, even if the Evil God gave pressure, breaking through to the fifth stage was not so simple.

There was only one with the most hope, and that was Wang Jianzhi.

Da Boluo could only rank behind him.

As for Liu Dajun, breaking through to the fourth stage was already very difficult.

And Xu Shilun, if he wasn't ruthless enough, he wouldn't even be able to break through the third stage!

He shook his head, throwing all these messy thoughts out of his mind.

He began to think again about how to solve the current problem.

Currently, only Da Boluo and the Ant Queen knew about this matter.

The reason it hadn't been exposed was because they hadn't communicated with the current Travelers yet.

Otherwise, as soon as they communicated, it would definitely be exposed.

Should he just kill this Da Boluo and let him revive once?

No, he had already obtained his fragments and could store his memories.

The next revived Da Boluo would still have this memory.

The Ant Queen wouldn't work either; Da Boluo had memories, but for the Ant Queen to be suddenly revived by someone, that was clearly not normal either.

Delete memories?

This was possible, but there would be some blank periods.

Moreover, if he did this, then everything he had done before would basically be wasted.

So, how was this problem to be solved?

He had to let them continue along this trajectory.

His alternate identity could not be exposed.

Block their communication?

This seemed impossible... Wait a minute, it seems possible!

First step, set all words related to himself, the Evil God, and the God of Creation, whether descriptions or records, as taboos!

Although they would surely find a way to communicate later.

But whether it was the Evil God or the God of Creation, they had already become taboos.

They could only express themselves in vague ways.

This way, it could confuse the public; after all, others wouldn't know which god the other person was talking about.

Wolong said it was a mission given by the Evil God.

When spoken, it would be a mission given by ***.

Da Boluo said he knew information from the God of Creation.

When spoken, it would become information known from ***.

This was not enough; he also had to block outside information.

The reason his alternate identity was exposed was because people outside knew about him.

They knew that the God of Creation and the Evil God were the same person.

The one who constantly annihilated worlds and pulled people into the Little Universe was the same person.

Then those Travelers would absolutely not want to work for him and become stronger.

Because they knew that no matter what they did, they would die.

So instead of that, it was better to lie flat.

Yu Jiemo and Wolong were already starting to have this problem.

But it wasn't particularly obvious yet; using death as a threat could still make them obedient.

But if this continued, it would absolutely not work.

Therefore, whenever they had information related to gods in their minds or were about to speak it, the live broadcast would be directly cut off.

Thinking of this, Su Mo waved his hand and set this rule.

After doing so, he thought again, although it was absolutely prevented for them to know that the God of Creation and the Evil God were the same person outside.

But the guys inside would still try to communicate and find out.

Whether it was hand gestures, secret codes, or even various methods, they would definitely figure something out.

Because face-to-face communication was absolutely unstoppable.

Unless he kept watching them whenever they talked.

However, after thinking about it, Su Mo felt relieved again.

If only these guys inside knew that the God of Creation and the Evil God were the same person.

It was just to make them constantly stronger and fight among themselves.

Actually, the problem wasn't big.

After all, this Little Universe was his territory.

No matter what you do, as long as you are in this universe, you have to obey me.

If they could become stronger because of this, that would be even better!

Imagine, when they finally completed their mission after much hardship, all broke through to the fifth stage, and were waiting for the God of Creation to complete their mission, the Evil God appeared.

And stated that all the bad things were done by him.

The reason they were like this was also secretly designed by him.

He wondered if they would directly break through another major level due to anger.

No, no, I am a God of Creation, how can I have such evil thoughts!

No, let's change it again.

In fact, the God of Creation has always chosen the Stone Men faction; only the Evil God chose other Travelers!

The reason the Stone Men wanted to destroy this world was because he wanted to destroy everything related to the Evil God.

Who said that the Evil God was only Su Mo?

One must know that in the last epoch, it was Da Boluo and his group who destroyed the world and the universe!

They were also Evil Gods!

And those animals infected by their power and possessing extraordinary power, weren't they the Evil God's pawns!

The reason the Evil God appeared before Da Boluo in the guise of the God of Creation was for deception!

It's normal for the Evil God to deceive people, isn't it?

Also, Yu Jiemo had spoken with the God of Creation and wanted to build a temple for him.

Although he eventually betrayed the Traveler due to the Evil God, the God of Creation helped him, making him a Traveler on his side.

Not only powerful, but also without a mission!

Thinking of this, Su Mo suddenly felt that all the threads had been sorted out by him.

The God of Creation created the Stone Men to destroy the Evil God.

And to avoid being destroyed, the Evil God constantly sought out Travelers to help him fight against the God of Creation!

I'm practically a genius!!!

Although, I still need to block outside information about myself.

Ever since Yu Jiemo said he contacted him on Earth, many people now constantly pray to him in their hearts every day.

Although he didn't manage it, this matter also needed to be dealt with.

It was just that he was currently trapped in the Little Universe; after this time ended and he got out, he would definitely resolve all these issues.

Although Su Mo had already explained it to himself.

It wouldn't cause the God of Creation to lose his alternate identity.

But Su Mo also had no intention of abandoning another plan.

If those guys could really break through one more time at the last moment, it would be a huge profit; if they didn't break through, it didn't matter.

It was just a try anyway.

As for later, when they found out that the God of Creation was the Evil God.

This time, he would directly destroy the universe.

What if they knew?

Who else could they tell!

First, they couldn't possibly survive!

Secondly, even if they really survived, Su Mo would restrict them!

Before, they still belonged to Earth.

But after being in the Little Universe for so long, they were now bound to this universe!

They were now lives of this universe!

As long as he wanted, he could knead them into any shape!

Finally, add a double layer of insurance by clearing this memory.

In this situation, even if they had the ability to reach the heavens, they would absolutely not be able to tell anyone.

Subsequently, Su Mo thought again that the reason such a thing happened was because he had taken too much action.

Now everything was ready, and the stage had been set for them.

Next, he just had to watch.

Unless absolutely necessary, he would definitely not take action next.

Of course, this was said in situations where it wasn't necessary.

If it was necessary, he wouldn't mind...

...

Near a dungeon, Wenhe stepped on a wild boar more than twenty meters long: "Now, do you want to become as strong and awesome as me!

Follow me, and I'll take you to enjoy the good life!"

Wild boar: "Why do I have to eat fragrant and pull?"

Wenhe: "Emmm, don't worry about that, do you want to be as strong as me!"

In the time that followed, Wenhe relied on this method to gather a large team and occupy two dungeons.

Three years passed in a flash, and Su Mo just watched quietly; he truly did not interfere with the Little Universe's affairs.

And at this time, Wolong had already gathered an army of thirty thousand Mosquitoes!

Although they still had no intelligence, they had learned to accept his commands.

Looking at the team behind him, Wolong laughed heartily: "It's time to conquer the world and raise the banner of the Demon Race!!!"


#153 Emperor Xu Tian died, and all the people mourned!

Chapter 153 Emperor Xu Tian dies, all the people sigh!

Su Mo, who had originally decided not to act rashly again, suddenly turned cold.

At this moment, in Xu Shilun's dungeon, Xu Shilun, who had been tormented for three consecutive years, was already on his last breath.

His misery was beyond description.

He wasn't afraid of death, because he knew he could revive after dying.

But being tormented like this, he truly couldn't bear it.

Xu Shilun looked at Zhang Jiao, who was using soul power to heal him, and weakly said, "Zhang Jiao, I'll tell you a secret. Please give me a quick end, alright?"

"A secret? No, I don't need such things. I just want to torment you, that's all!"

Xu Shilun's face was extremely grim: "This secret concerns the God of Creation who resurrected you. Do you really not want to know?"

"No."

Zhang Jiao shook his head as he spoke, and at the same time, he cut off another portion of soul power from Xu Shilun's body.

Xu Shilun's face turned pale once more. At this point, he was so weak that he didn't even have the strength to scream.

Seeing that Zhang Jiao remained unmoved, his expression hardened, and he used the last ounce of strength in his body to shout, "The God of Creation and the Evil God are the same person!"

When he shouted this, time itself seemed to freeze at that moment.

Zhang Jiao's face was filled with horror.

And at this moment, Su Mo's face instantly turned as black as a pot bottom.

This sentence had two meanings.

One was that the God of Creation and the Evil God were the same entity!

The other meaning was that the God of Creation and the Evil God were the same "person"!

Regardless of whether he meant that the God of Creation and the Evil God were the same, or that the God of Creation and the Evil God were one, a person.

This matter had already touched Su Mo's bottom line!

Because this was something that absolutely could not be said!!!

He had thought that he indeed had many loopholes, and some clever people might have even guessed it.

Although he had also been constantly trying to remedy the situation, continuously patching up his identity.

Making everyone's expressions look better.

Not to mention that he still had a disguise now, and it was a good one.

Even if his true body was full of loopholes, with only a fig leaf to cover his shame, this matter was still not allowed to be spoken of by anyone.

You could think it in your heart, but you couldn't say it.

Even talking to himself was forbidden, let alone shouting it out so brazenly!

Therefore, no matter how clever Xu Shilun was.

And no matter if Xu Shilun would be useful to him in the future.

He was destined to die!

And not the kind of death where he could revive.

Instead, it was the kind where he would never be able to revive; he would eternally annihilate Xu Shilun!

In Xu Shilun's dungeon, Zhang Jiao's face was horrified: "You're crazy! Is this something that can be directly said!!!"

Xu Shilun's mouth twitched, and he sneered: "What, you're scared too? Now that you know about this, we will all die. The Evil God will definitely not resurrect you if you die, but I'm different. I'm still useful to the Evil God, so I can still rev..."

Before he could finish his sentence, a massive and terrifying dark presence had already appeared before him. This person was none other than Su Mo.

Not only did he appear, but he directly interrupted Xu Shilun's words: "No, you're wrong. You can't revive either."

There was no emotion in Su Mo's words, like a cold machine announcing a direct fact.

Because, this, was a fact!

This, was also a God's judgment!

Just this one sentence, however, made Xu Shilun, who had just been triumphant, feel as if he had fallen into an ice cave!

He didn't even have time to speak before he saw Zhang Jiao turn into a substance like wind-blown sand before him, slowly dissipating.

Zhang Jiao, who had only revived to torment Xu Shilun for three years and had done nothing else, died silently once again!

He didn't even have time to let out a scream.

Xu Shilun only saw horror written in Zhang Jiao's eyes.

It was only at this moment that Xu Shilun finally came to his senses.

And at this moment, he also seemed to realize what a foolish thing he had just done.

"No, no, no, Evil God, no, God of Creation, please listen to my explanation..."

Su Mo looked down at the tiny existence before him and nodded: "Hmm, speak, I'm listening. I also want to hear how you discovered it."

Xu Shilun, who had thought he was doomed, felt a little more at ease when he saw that the Evil God was actually willing to communicate with him.

If the Evil God had immediately killed him without a word, just like he killed Zhang Jiao, then no matter how eloquent he was, it would have been useless.

But now that he saw he could still communicate, he felt relieved.

As long as he could communicate, it meant there was still a chance.

If he seized the opportunity, he might not die.

Not only would he not die, but he would also receive assistance from the Evil God.

Thinking of this, Xu Shilun felt much calmer, then weakly said, "Great God of Creation, I am too weak right now. May I rest for a while?"

With a thought from Su Mo, Xu Shilun's injuries instantly vanished. Not only that, but even his strength returned at that moment.

It wasn't that Su Mo was merciful, but in his eyes, Xu Shilun was already a dead man!

Talking to him now was merely to find out where he had been exposed.

As for restoring his strength, it was simply because he directly reset Xu Shilun to his peak period, as that was the simplest way.

Xu Shilun felt the powerful force within his body, which made his heart incredibly excited.

The feeling of having power was truly exhilarating!

In the previous epoch, he had just broken through to the Fourth Order when it reached its final stage.

So he had never truly experienced much of the Fourth Order's power.

At this moment, he could finally truly experience it!

If he had known earlier that speaking of this matter would give him a chance to converse with the Evil God, and even receive his help.

Then he should have done so long ago.

Su Mo looked at the excited Xu Shilun expressionlessly and said faintly, "Speak. How did you find out?"

Xu Shilun, who had just been immersed in his own powerful strength, snapped back to reality upon hearing this.

His excited mood vanished instantly.

His own strength was already so immense, so how powerful must the Evil God be, who could restore him with just a thought!

Thinking of this, he quickly composed himself, showing a humble expression, and said:

"God of Creation, actually, you did very well. There were basically no loopholes, and you didn't reveal any flaws.

The reason I discovered all of this was simply my deduction..."

Xu Shilun kept staring at Su Mo as he spoke, and when he saw that his expression remained unchanged, he suddenly choked. He immediately knew that this wasn't what the Evil God wanted to hear.

So, he swallowed his previous flattering remarks and began again: "Actually, as early as Da Boluo's live broadcast, when I was still on Earth, I already had this idea.

But I wasn't sure yet. Later, when the Evil God tried to escape to Earth and was caught back, I became suspicious again.

It wasn't until I transmigrated and received the energy of Dark Tiga, in which I saw your destruction of the Ultraman Civilization.

At that moment, I was certain that both the God of Creation and the Evil God were you."

Upon hearing this, Su Mo thought to himself, so it was because of that Dark Tiga.

At the time of the Ultraman Civilization's destruction, he indeed hadn't created any so-called Evil God identity.

Now it seemed that perhaps not many people knew about this matter.

Even if others were suspicious, it was only suspicion.

Thinking of this, Su Mo continued, "Anything else?"

His tone remained as cold as ever, like a machine without emotion.

At this moment, Xu Shilun also seemed to feel that something was amiss, but facing Su Mo's question, he still bit the bullet and said:

"The rest are my deductions, which have no concrete evidence.

The destruction of the Ultraman Civilization combined with the destruction of Ashura, and the situation of Mosquitoes and Ants as mentioned by Liu Dajun.

Originally, these things actually had no connection, and if they were considered as two separate Gods, it could actually be understood.

But there were too many coincidences.

So..."

Xu Shilun spoke for more than half an hour, and Su Mo watched him expressionlessly the entire time.

It wasn't until he completely stopped that Su Mo finally spoke: "Are you done?"

Xu Shilun nodded: "Great God of Creation, I am done."

Su Mo also nodded slightly: "Hmm, since you're done, then go die."

Having said that, Su Mo was about to act.

Xu Shilun's face changed drastically upon hearing this, and he suddenly exclaimed, "No, you can't kill me! I'm still useful! I can still work for you! I can be your dog!"

His words were urgent and rapid, a long string of words without any pause, spoken in one breath.

However, upon hearing Xu Shilun's statement about wanting to be his dog, Su Mo shook his head: "I don't want a dog that bares its fangs at its master."

With that, Su Mo slapped down.

Xu Shilun, who had already planned to escape, saw this scene and, without the slightest hesitation, turned and ran.

The power of the Fourth Order had already touched space.

His figure flashed and he had already appeared outside the dungeon.

This was the first time in his life he had used spatial abilities.

And it was also the fastest he had ever run in his life!

This time, he was truly fighting for his life.

But no matter how fast he was, in front of Su Mo, it was all in vain.

He had just run out of the dungeon for less than a second, and before he could even speak, he appeared in front of Su Mo again.

Subsequently, his body, like Zhang Jiao's before him, began to wither and dissipate.

His face filled with terror, he wanted to beg for mercy but found that he couldn't speak at all.

He could only watch helplessly as his body weathered and disappeared.

At the same time, all of Xu Shilun's fragments in the twenty dungeons also turned into powder and dissipated.

It wasn't until all of Xu Shilun's fragments disappeared that the dungeons also vanished.

At the same time, myriad strange phenomena emerged, and the sound of the heavens weeping spread throughout the universe, as a torrential blood rain, accompanied by the wailing of the Great Dao, fell.

In an instant, everyone in the dungeons, whether awake or not, awoke at that moment.


#154 When Shi Lun dies, disaster arises!

Chapter 154: Shi Lun dies and disaster arises!

The power contained within a Fourth-Tier powerhouse, when fully unleashed, can destroy half a galaxy in an instant.

Endless dark energy surged from the suddenly vanishing dungeons.

In just an instant, it enveloped the entire continent.

When a whale falls, all things are born!

Countless animals that had not yet gained sentience, after being baptized by this dark energy, all began to awaken their intelligence.

Living beings that had already gained sentience were promoted one after another.

Many animals that were stuck at the peak of the extraordinary realm, relying on this energy, truly stepped into the First-Tier of the extraordinary realm.

At this moment, countless Great Daos manifested, and although it was only for a moment, it allowed countless animals to comprehend their own Dao and embark on their own paths.

The Dark Great Dao remained illuminated, seemingly mourning Xu Shilun, and also seemingly searching for a new host.

However, all things embrace the yin and bear the yang; if there is a good side, there is naturally a bad side.

Although the dark energy enlightened countless animals and advanced many, darkness is darkness after all.

Animals that absorbed the dark energy began to become irritable and gloomy, and countless negative emotions continuously arose.

They wanted to destroy everything in front of them, to kill their companions, and massacres constantly occurred on the continent.

Even the Stone Men, who had always disliked movement, began to attack other animals under the influence of this energy.

Of course, they were still as lazy as ever, still as unwilling to move.

Therefore, they would only attack animals that entered their attack range.

They would no longer be like before, where they were too lazy to even turn over or look up when an Ant or other animals stepped on them.

In the world, blood rained down, and the wailing of the Great Dao still echoed.

Whether it was the animals already fighting in a chaotic mass or the Stone Men lying on the ground unwilling to move, they could all feel the sorrow of heaven and earth.

Da Boluo looked at the blood rain falling in the dungeon, then looked up at the pitch-black sky and remained silent for a long time: "Xu Shilun is dead!"

After a long period of silence, he continued: "Originally, I thought I would be happy to see you die, or even if not happy, at least calm, but for some reason, I still feel a bit sad.

Actually, to be honest, although I don't agree with many of the things you did, if I stood in your shoes, you weren't wrong.

It's just that your methods were too extreme. If your methods weren't so extreme, perhaps you really would have been a very formidable person."

After Da Boluo finished speaking, he silently lowered his head, not knowing what he was thinking.

He stood there in the blood rain, letting his soul body be soaked and stained red by the blood rain, yet he remained unmoved.

The Land of Light dungeon, among all dungeons, appeared to be the most righteous, the most radiant, and the most magnificent.

Liu Dajun looked at the black mist and blood rain in the sky, speechless for a long time.

As a fellow Fourth-Tier, he naturally knew what this situation was.

Although he, like everyone else, had lost all his power.

But ultimately, he was still a Fourth-Tier, and he still had his Great Dao.

It's just that he had no power.

Third-Tier, God of Civilization.

Fourth-Tier, can be a True God, enjoying a lifespan of thirty thousand years.

This was the fall of a True God.

And, he knew this God.

No, it should be said that he would recognize him even if he turned into ash.

Their hatred, even if written with the water of three rivers, could not be washed away.

In fact, he felt that Xu Shilun had always been targeting him.

It had been like this since the moment he transmigrated.

Originally, he thought he would be very happy to see this scene, but for some reason, he was so calm.

There was even a slight sense of loss in his heart.

Perhaps it was the regret of not being able to kill him with his own hands.

After speaking, he cupped some blood rain in his hand, then turned and found a place to sit down, and then began to restore his power.

In a world full of flames, although it was already enveloped in darkness, everything around it was still illuminated by the light of the flames.

The blood rain was even burned dry and evaporated by the flames before it even hit the ground.

A muscular man with a rough appearance and four arms, looking at the celestial phenomenon and the wailing of the Great Dao, began to laugh maniacally.

"Hahahahaha, hahahahaha, Xu Shilun, Xu Shilun, Xu Shilun, you dog, you're finally dead!!!

Hahaha, retribution, it's truly retribution, you dog, you're finally getting your just deserts. Hahahahaha..."

The rough man ran wildly in the flames, his roars mixed with bursts of laughter that made people's scalps tingle.

After laughing for several tens of minutes, he suddenly pointed at the sky and cursed: "What are you crying about! Did your dad die or your mom die? Shouldn't you be happy that Xu Shilun is dead! Laugh for me!"

【Boom~】

A thunderclap sounded in the world of flames, and just now, the four-armed man was instantly blasted to a crisp.

And such a scene did not happen in just one dungeon, but in all twenty dungeons.

Su Mo was stunned when he saw this scene.

This was the Great Dao wailing.

This was truly a Fourth-Tier rule!

This, this, well, this was an idiot*, daring to curse in such a situation, one can only say he deserved it.

One can only say that Wang Jianzhi's hatred for Xu Shilun was truly immense.

Otherwise, it wouldn't be like this.

Originally, with Xu Shilun dead, everyone awakened, which was a joyous occasion for all.

But Wang Jianzhi had to be different and pull off a bizarre stunt.

Then, the newly revived Wang Jianzhi once again returned to the fountain to wait for the resurrection cooldown.

Dark energy enveloped the continent, and countless animals began to become gloomy and bloodthirsty.

Combined with the current drama of Wolong Wenhe raising the banner of the Demon Clan and the Stone Men of the Witch Clan, this dark energy became very obvious; this was called Calamity Qi!

The Calamity Qi of the Great Witch-Demon Calamity.

Xu Shilun truly did not die in vain; not only did he enlighten countless animals, but he also caused a large number of animals to enter the calamity.

And this Calamity Qi afterward was even more astonishing!

Just the death of one Fourth-Tier brought so many benefits. If...

Su Mo stroked his chin, thinking to himself.

Ultimately, he still didn't plan to make a move.

One at a time was enough.

The current situation was very good.

Keeping them might be useful later.

After all, they were reusable resources; using them as single-use items would indeed be a bit wasteful.

Of course, if there was a need, Su Mo wouldn't mind making a move.

On this side, Su Mo was secretly pondering how to kill the other few people to maximize his benefits.

Meanwhile, on the other side, Wolong Wenhe and Two Mosquitoes, who had just raised the banner of the Demon Clan, looked at the celestial phenomenon and at first didn't realize what was happening.

#155 This is original sin!

Chapter 155 This is original sin!

There were many reasons for him to do all of this.

First, he had, after all, called him Master for so many years.

Secondly, the dark power after Xu Shilun's death also strengthened his soul once, although he still hadn't broken through the first stage due to his late cultivation.

But this was also a favor.

Thirdly, they were all Old fellows from Earth, and although they had never met in reality, they were still acquainted.

Combining this with the favor mentioned above, erecting a monument for him was the only thing he could do.

As for why Xu Shilun died, and why he died so suddenly, these matters...

It wasn't that he didn't want to know, but that he didn't dare to think about it.

There were many intelligent people in this world; for example, several current Travelers had served as Xu Shilun's strategists.

None of them were foolish.

Xu Shilun's strength had already reached the fourth stage; in this current Little Universe, these newly born beings absolutely could not kill Xu Shilun.

So, who exactly killed Xu Shilun?

This was a question.

A very serious question, one that knowing the answer to might also lead to death.

Therefore, he absolutely could not investigate; let alone investigate, he couldn't even think about it!

At the same time, in Yu Jiemo's live stream, a large number of bullet comments popped up.

As the first person to become a Traveler, his live stream had the most viewers.

So, in his live stream, there were all kinds of bullet comments.

“Why did Xu Shilun, that guy, die so suddenly?”

“Yeah, he died too suddenly!”

“His death caught me off guard.”

“It's good that he died. A bad seed like him, alive, not only wastes air but also kills more innocent lives.”

“Xu Shilun, sigh, what a pity.”

“You heretic, you actually pity Xu Shilun.”

“Isn't Xu Shilun worthy of pity? In the end, it's just a difference in stance.”

“Yes, Xu Shilun wanted to live so badly. To live, he resorted to all means, regardless of anything, and ultimately even used the insane method of smuggling.

Although cruel, he truly just wanted to live.

Moreover, since Da Boluo and the others were willing to agree to his idea, then Ashura and Otto would definitely be resurrected.”

“A person who always wanted to live, using every means just to live, ultimately met such an end. He didn't even know how he died. Sigh~”

“Think about how miserable Xu Shilun was back then. Whether it was Ashura or Otto, they all had their own paths. Only he was left with a Soul Clan whose development was uncertain, and his mission was to unify the three clans.

Although that mission was a joke, he really kept fighting desperately.”

“Yes, holding such a hand of cards, yet ultimately sitting on the throne of the King of the Three Clans. Such perseverance, such wisdom, and ruthlessness are simply beyond what ordinary people can compare to.”

At the same time, in the real world, some people fell silent upon witnessing Xu Shilun's death.

Becoming a Traveler to another world, living a second life, and being able to control powerful forces.

Even becoming a person above others.

And even able to be resurrected after death.

Although there would be some hardships in the early stages, wasn't it also a new beginning?

Da Boluo had been suffering before, so no one wanted to become a Traveler.

But after seeing Xu Shilun's actions, and the possibility of resurrection, would some wealthy individuals whose lives were already nearing their end, or some young people who were not doing well in the real world, truly have no thoughts?

This was impossible.

They also wanted to become Travelers.

Not only did they want to become Travelers, but they were also preventing others from becoming Travelers.

Otherwise, why do you think someone would send a bullet comment saying that those who speak more will become Travelers?

It was because they didn't want others to send bullet comments.

It was because they didn't want others to snatch their Traveler slots.

After all, with so many people in the live stream, who could become a Traveler, and whether anyone would become a Traveler next time, were uncertain matters.

After all, the Evil God had never said that someone would definitely become a Traveler.

Nor had he ever said that those who send more bullet comments could become Travelers.

These were just conclusions they had drawn based on patterns.

As for the specifics, no one dared to guarantee it.

Well, there was one, but that person was currently in the Little Universe.

Based on those superficial pieces of information, they had already started to act.

The rich recruited think tanks to offer advice and make their bullet comments more impressive.

The poor racked their brains to find ways to say more outrageous things, hoping to catch the attention of the Evil God.

But the moment Xu Shilun died, they suddenly fell silent.

For the poor, although the real world was hard, surviving was not an issue.

And they also had family and friends; becoming a Traveler meant completely abandoning them.

If they did well over there, that would be fine; those who genuinely cared for them would be happy for them, and their enemies would be jealous.

But what if they didn't do well?

Starting out like Wang Jianzhi, working as a laborer every day, living hand-to-mouth, or even facing constant life-threatening danger.

Moreover, not everyone could be like Xu Shilun.

One wrong move, and they could be beaten to death right after becoming a Traveler.

Rather than that, it was better to continue living a mediocre life in the real world.

If it were before, they would have thought that Travelers had divine favor and basically wouldn't die.

Look at Da Boluo, look at Liu Dajun, and Xu Shilun; they were all living well.

And they also possessed powerful strength.

It was precisely for this reason that they even thought that this was why the Evil God chose people from their world.

After becoming a Traveler, although they would experience some hardships, they would definitely rise to prominence later and wouldn't die. Who wouldn't want such a thing?

But the moment Xu Shilun died, everything changed.

The same was true for the wealthy big shots; unless it truly reached a point where if they didn't become a Traveler today, they would die tomorrow, otherwise, their lives in the real world, apart from not having supernatural powers, had truly enjoyed everything they could enjoy.

However, even so, there were still some who didn't give up. Those were desperadoes, with no relatives at home, living hand-to-mouth, with their lives constantly on the line.

Because their lives in the real world were similar to those in another world. Rather than rot in this world, it was better to take a chance in another world.

What if they became invincible? What if… what if!

At the same time, more organizations were pondering Xu Shilun's death.

Why did he die?

And how did he die?

Did he know something, or was it because he did something?

Although these things were always said to be taboo, that curiosity would kill them.

But there were always some people who weren't afraid of death.

And there were always some obsessive people.

But all of this was done in private and not brought into the open.

After all, this could truly lead to deaths.

And many deaths.

Even if things went wrong, Earth could be destroyed.

Because everyone had a consensus in their minds: only the Evil God could kill Xu Shilun.

And what they were discussing, talking about, and thinking about was: why did the Evil God kill Xu Shilun?!

Did he know something?

Or did he do something?

If it was about what he did, Xu Shilun indeed did a lot.

He stole the Evil God's peach, causing the previous epoch to be destroyed prematurely, making the Evil God suffer a setback.

He led others to smuggle... These were all reasons for certain death.

But if it truly was for this reason, then why didn't the Evil God kill Xu Shilun before?

They didn't believe the Evil God couldn't do it.

After all, if he could kill him now, he definitely could have killed him before.

Aside from anything else, just when the Evil God captured people to be Travelers, he definitely had the ability and opportunity to kill Xu Shilun.

But the Evil God didn't do that.

Therefore, the situation was very clear: at that time, the Evil God had not yet thought of killing Xu Shilun.

Although Xu Shilun stole the Evil God's peach, Xu Shilun must have still been useful to the Evil God at that time.

But then, why exactly did it happen later?

They desperately listed many possibilities.

There was Xu Shilun provoking the Evil God.

Or Xu Shilun no longer wanting to live.

And so on and so forth.

Ultimately, there was only one, and the most probable option: Xu Shilun knew the Evil God's secret.

But what kind of secret could Xu Shilun have known?

And what kind of secret could make the Evil God disregard his dignity, even destroying the secret realm he had created with Xu Shilun before, and disrupting years of planning just to kill him?

Thinking of this, some of them felt their hair stand on end, while others felt excited and thrilled.

Because they were about to uncover the secret of a god!

This was a true god, a great existence capable of transcending worlds and dimensions.

This was the first case in history.

If they were right, they might even go down in history.

Of course, they also knew that their actions could potentially doom the entire Earth, but they didn't care.

Because they were all true madmen!

...

Su Mo stood in the void, sensing the thoughts and calls of the people outside, and snorted coldly in his heart.

Since he was currently trapped in the Little Universe and couldn't leave, he would let them live a little longer.

Once he got out, every single one of them would have to...

Then he looked at the remaining sixty dungeons on the continent and muttered, “How tragic.”

Xu Shilun, who single-mindedly wanted to live, was the first to die.

Those guys who, from beginning to end, only wanted to protect their clan, even preferring to die themselves rather than let their loved ones and clansmen die.

Yet they repeatedly clung to their clansmen, relying on their flesh and blood to survive again and again.

Again and again, they cried out “for the clan,” yet again and again, they tearfully ate buns stained with their blood, allowing their strength to grow stronger and stronger.

Only Xu Shilun, that guy, was alone from beginning to end.

Perhaps, the reason for your death was also because you were alone.

After all, this is also considered a sin in my eyes.

So, why didn't you have a clan?


#156 Bring millions of demons to have a good talk with Feng Chu!

Chapter 156: Bring millions of demons to have a good talk with Feng Chu!

Those without emotions also developed faint emotions due to this power.

It was precisely because of this power that the Stone Men would attack other animals.

In eighty years, the Stone Men would have almost eaten up the continent.

The great tribulation would also be almost over.

Most of the monster beasts around the dungeons had already been subdued by them.

Near one of Wang Jianzhi's dungeons, there was a second-order, six peak first-order, more than fifty mid-first-order, and tens of thousands of first-order monster beasts entrenched.

And there was another, also one of Wang Jianzhi's dungeons.

At this moment, he seemed to see Mimi again, that small Mimi, who still needed his protection, waving at him.

It was also as if Mimi wanted to climb up his leg to rest on his head.

At this moment, it was as if everything in the world had nothing to do with him.

Because he felt that Mimi had never left him.

And it was precisely because of this that he could not be sad, could not be angry.

At this moment, he just wanted to die like this, to go to the world where Mimi existed.

His consciousness rapidly dispersed and flew away, and a huge consciousness body pulled him into it.

#164 Twelve prototypes!

Chapter 164 Twelve Prototypes!

Wenhe sent people down to check the situation.

Even after confirming that Yu Jiemo had completely turned into a pile of Stone Men, he still didn't relax his vigilance in the slightest.

"Stone Men won't be protected by mental links after they're broken. To make sure this guy is really dead, I have to check it myself!"

The next moment, a mental attack was launched.

Boom!

As soon as he touched the world of consciousness, Yu Jiemo felt his spirit being violently attacked.

Then, his consciousness dissipated...

"Sure enough, Old fellows are hard to kill. You almost got away."

Even so, Wenhe still launched several attacks at the pile of Stone Men, ensuring that his attacks flowed like mud into the sea, without hitting anything, before he finally gave up.

"Alright, now that this place has been recovered, there are only two places left!"

After Wenhe finished speaking, he swept the ground again, making sure nothing was left behind, and then prepared to lead his troops away.

As for Yu Jiemo's body.

They didn't let it go either, calling a bunch of unconscious divine Mosquitoes to eat it.

Actually, Yu Jiemo and the others had wanted to eat it themselves before.

After all, Stone Men are so strong, if they could devour a Stone Men, wouldn't they also gain a powerful physical body?

But after they killed a Stone Men for the first time and tried to devour its body, only to find they couldn't digest it, they gave up on that idea.

Then, they discovered that if a Stone Men devoured the body of another Stone Men, its strength would become even greater.

So they didn't dare to leave the Stone Men's bodies behind either.

Even if a Stone Men was killed, their bodies had to be taken away.

Otherwise, it would only become nourishment for other Stone Men to grow stronger.

During that period, they killed no less than three hundred Stone Men for experiments.

After confirming that they truly couldn't do anything with the Stone Men's bodies, they could only pile them up.

It's not that they gained nothing, though; they did get some information from those Stone Men bodies.

For example, the weight of Stone Men is not the same.

The heavier the Stone Men, the stronger his strength.

And based on this, they also knew how Stone Men grew stronger.

Their volume remained unchanged, but their weight kept increasing, and their mass kept growing.

It was hard for them not to covet such creatures.

And these guys even grew exponentially; just thinking about it felt terrifying!

This was the information they obtained from the Stone Men bodies.

But that was all.

After all, besides this, there really wasn't much else to study about Stone Men.

Wenhe and Wolong were not scientists, and it was even less possible for them to figure out how they managed to keep their volume unchanged while their mass kept increasing.

As for the other demon races, how many years have they had intelligence now?

Although they weren't pure brain-dead like Ashura, if you were to say they were smart, they really weren't that smart.

At least, when it came to things like scientific research and studies, they completely didn't understand.

A large pile of Stone Men bodies accumulated, and after a long time, they discovered that some Mosquitoes could actually digest Stone Men bodies.

Although it took a long time, they could indeed eat the Stone Men bodies.

This greatly surprised Wolong Wenhe.

Later, they discovered that these Mosquitoes were precisely those Mosquitoes that couldn't communicate, couldn't gain intelligence, and couldn't grow stronger.

These Mosquitoes had a very big characteristic: they ate an awful lot.

The amount of food they needed to consume in a day was ten times that of normal Mosquitoes.

Previously, Wenhe had suggested that these Mosquitoes not only couldn't communicate and wouldn't obey orders, but they also couldn't grow stronger and ate a lot.

Moreover, they ate dirt, and although they would excrete, their appetite was simply too large.

So his idea was, instead of letting them live and waste air and consume land, it would be better to kill them and use them as military rations for other demon races.

At that time, Wenhe was already preparing to do this.

But then he suddenly discovered that these Mosquitoes had this function.

They only needed to eat a tiny bit of Stone Men fragments, which was equivalent to eating several days' worth of dirt.

Of course, the exact number of days depended on how much mass the Stone Men had.

Then they would digest those stones and excrete them.

Just a tiny bit of powder, and they could excrete nearly a thousand pounds of giant feces.

Mosquito feces, in large lumps, whether used as fertilizer for plants, or thrown into a pit for land use, or even fed to some omnivorous animals, were an excellent choice.

At this moment, they suddenly knew how to solve the problem of the land eaten by the Stone Men.

As long as all Stone Men were killed, and then these Mosquitoes ate them, digested them, and excreted them, the land could be restored!

Their living problem was naturally solved as well.

Wenhe, who had originally planned to kill these Mosquitoes for military rations, immediately regarded them as protected animals.

After all, in the entire world, currently only this type of Mosquito can digest this stuff.

Su Mo hadn't thought of this before.

Previously, he only wanted to put pressure on the Stone Men, so he expanded the number of Mosquitoes tenfold.

So, among the previous Mosquitoes, nine out of ten were created by God.

Moreover, these Mosquitoes' appetite was ten times that of ordinary Mosquitoes.

However, since their appetite was tenfold, their digestive ability was also tenfold or more!

He just thought of doing this at the time, so he acted.

Anyway, it was just a thought, and it was very simple to do.

But what he completely didn't expect was that Mosquitoes were indeed large in size.

And they did eat dirt.

But!

Mosquitoes don't just eat without excreting!

They eat dirt only to absorb the nutrients their bodies need for growth.

The excess Slag will still be excreted by them.

And the feces of these Mosquitoes were indeed much less compared to the previous dirt, even reaching one-third.

But, their feces are good stuff for plants, and even some omnivorous animals.

Unfortunately, Wenhe discovered this too late.

But this type of Mosquito had already been mostly killed off during this long period.

You know, before they transmigrated, Mosquitoes, while not at the very bottom of the food chain, weren't much higher either.

Basically, anyone with a bit of extraordinary power liked to kill Mosquitoes and Ants for food.

Just because they were large, had a lot of meat, and had no extraordinary power, and were weak.

So over the years, almost half of those Mosquitoes had already died.

And the most important problem was that these Mosquitoes, one less when one died, because they were not born through normal reproduction.

Except for divine creation, there was no other way to produce them.

Watching the Mosquitoes devour Yu Jiemo's stone, Wenhe nodded in satisfaction.

He decided to stay here and watch Yu Jiemo's body be completely devoured before leaving.

Anyway, he wasn't short on that much time.

At this moment, in the collective consciousness, countless fragmented thoughts were still being devoured and merged by other thoughts.

Finally, twelve new consciousnesses were born.


#165 The highest will, God's conjecture!

#166 The supreme will of terror!

#181 Fellow countrymen meet fellow countrymen!

Chapter 181: Fellow villagers meet!

Wolong's behavior was unusually agitated, at least on the surface.

However, this did not mean that these guys were fools.

No matter how honest or harmless they looked, he would never believe that they were truly as they appeared on the surface.

An Old Coin is an Old Coin; once an Old Coin, always an Old Coin!

Even if the Old Coin farted, Wolong would have to suspect that the fart had an ulterior motive, let alone something he specifically created.

Regardless of their thoughts on their current submission, the goal had been achieved.

Just as Wolong thought, Dark Tiga was not truly submissive.

There was a reason why they did this.

The deeds of the Stone Men were not difficult to investigate; before, Wolong didn't say anything, and they didn't care.

But now that Wolong had given a speech in front of them, a little investigation would reveal everything.

Moreover, with their Three-Order Middle Stage strength, it should be known that in the last epoch, they were also masters who roamed the universe.

It was only in this epoch, due to changes in specifications, that they could not go into space.

However, neither their speed nor their strength had decreased at all.

Therefore, with their speed, within heaven and earth, it truly wouldn't take much time to fly wherever they wanted.

It's just that some places couldn't be flown into recklessly.

For example, certain dungeons, and the Ant Clan Queen's nest...

In that short period of time, they had already collected countless pieces of information.

After compilation and calculation, if the Stone Men continued to develop in this way, it would inevitably destroy the entire world in the future.

In outer space, the Spider Sky Emperor suppressed heaven and earth, and no one could escape.

On the ground, the Stone Men devoured the world, and there were also terrifying mighty figures from the last epoch who destroyed worlds.

In such a dangerous world, if they didn't band together, they would eventually be eliminated by history.

Although they currently possessed the strength to suppress most of the demon race.

However, they had just discovered something.

They could no longer grow stronger.

Or rather, their method of growing stronger had completely changed.

Before, they were machines created by Xu Shilun, his clones.

They were Old Coins thrown haphazardly across the universe by Xu Shilun.

But now they had become demon race members.

They could no longer upgrade through technology, through replacing materials and components.

At this moment, their components, parts, and various programs had completely merged with them the moment they gained sentience and became demons.

In other words, now, if their hand was cut off, they truly lost a hand.

Even if parts were replaced, it would no longer be as flexible as before.

It was like the so-called prosthetics installed in the human world.

To regrow a true arm of their own, they could only rely on cultivation to break through to the third tier.

Although they were already at the third tier themselves.

But this was the third tier of technology from the last epoch, which was not the same path as transcendence; one could even say they weren't even on the same track.

Now, if they wanted to become stronger, they could only cultivate like other demon race members!

And they had to start from scratch, step by step, from transcendence to first tier, and then slowly reach the third tier.

This process, who knows how long it would take, so this was also the biggest reason for them to choose to join Wolong and the demon race team.

【My goodness, this is unscientific!!!】

【What the hell is this? Why? Just saying these few words, just giving a speech like that, tens of thousands of Three-Order Middle Stage chose to submit. This is a script, isn't it?!】

【Wolong, tell me the truth, are you a failed art student*? Otherwise, I suspect you're cheating!】

【Authentication complete, this is definitely a shady deal with Xu Shilun, that Old Coin.】

【I feel something is not right.】

【Nonsense, if this were right, there would be ghosts.】

【Other things aside, no matter what these Dark Tiga are thinking, as long as they are truly obedient now and willing to help you, the Stone Men won't be difficult.】

【Not only will the Stone Men not be difficult, I think it wouldn't be hard for you to go hang Da Boluo up and beat him now.】

With his final strength, I suspect he wasn't far from that one guy who raised civilizations.】

【Six six six!】

【One speech, thirty thousand Three-Order Middle Stage. With this strength, you are already invincible!】

Wolong ignored the barrage of comments in front of him and instead pondered what to do next.

Before, he had thought that if things didn't work out here, he would just go to another place and try again.

His current strength, while not enough to crush Da Boluo, was certainly enough to face the Ant Clan Queen.

At the same time, Yu Jiemo's twelve avatars still hadn't realized the seriousness of the situation.

#182 Big Pineapple: You are Wenhe!

#183 Eliminate the tyranny of stone!

Ten thousand ordinary extraordinary people are not as good as one first-tier.

#184 When you reach middle age, you have no choice but to soak wolfberries in pineapples!

Because this was related to whether Yu Ji and the others could be resurrected.

Because, even if he himself held onto that non-existent glimmer of hope, his rationality was telling him that it was impossible!

He also bore the responsibility for this generation of Ashura...

#185 Prove the truth with strength!

Chapter 185 Prove the truth with strength!

Wenhe led 300,000 Three-Order Middle Stage and a small number of Tier 2 Demon Commanders, along with ten million Ant Clan, to implement a scorched-earth policy.

All Stone Men they saw were to be destroyed!

The Ant Queen led ten million Ant Clan to travel in reverse along the path of an Ancestral Witch, clearing out all Stone Men along the way, while also being responsible for killing an Ancestral Witch.

Da Boluo, as the strongest single combatant, had the task of solo-killing an Ancestral Witch, and then providing support to other Demon Clan at any time.

If they saw a Stone Men, they would kill it.

If they could win, they would fight; if they couldn't, they would gather a large force and continue fighting.

Of course, their plan couldn't be said to be completely useless.

At least this way, they could effectively assign tasks and plan routes.

It prevented those Demon Commanders and Da Boluo from running around like headless chickens.

Under circumstances of absolute power suppression, military deployment was such a simple matter.

Because they didn't need any schemes or tricks.

They could win simply by overwhelming with their superior numbers and strength.

It was only when their strength was weak and they were at a disadvantage that they would resort to schemes and tricks.

After the discussion, Wolong Wenhe, the Ant Queen, and Da Boluo immediately began to march according to the set routes.

In the starry sky, Su Mo looked at the situation below and secretly compared the strengths of both sides.

Currently, the Demon Clan had dispatched twelve million troops, with a minimum strength of Tier 1.

If the ten million Mosquito Clan providing aerial support were included, the total number of troops would reach twenty-two million.

In addition, with the Ant Clan joining, twenty million Ant Clan had reached ordinary Transcendent, and there was also a Three-Order Middle Stage Ant Queen.

Furthermore, there was the strongest group of combatants: over twenty-three thousand Three-Order Middle Stage Dark Tiga, and a Da Boluo!

This was the full strength the Demon Clan had deployed now.

To say this strength was strong, it actually wasn't too strong.

Even during the late period of Da Boluo's era, the Ashura Clan already had 100,000 Tier 3s.

The number of Tier 2 existences reached a staggering million.

After a thousand years of development, the minimum strength of the Ashura was Tier 2.

It was only because of their difficulty in reproduction that their numbers were not large; otherwise, their strength would only be greater.

Now, the birth of life in this world had not even exceeded thirty years, yet it already possessed power equivalent to the Ashura after a thousand years of development back then.

Although it was also partly due to receiving many legacies from the previous incomplete world destruction.

But the strength was still real.

Thinking of this, Su Mo wanted to drag Xu Shilun, that scoundrel, out for another whipping.

Originally, he had wanted to directly destroy the entire universe last time and create a new one.

But he was tricked by him.

So, it was not unfair at all that he died.

But don't forget, they also had a Three-Order Middle Stage Ant Queen!

It was precisely for this reason that their army could be assembled and launched so quickly.

The Ant Queen also saw this, so her Ant soldiers had always been gathered around her.

Five days, neither long nor short.

For the Demon Clan, in five days, they had just finished organizing their army and were ready to depart.

However, on Yu Jiemo's side, his strength had already increased fivefold again.

If multiplied by twelve again, his strength would have directly increased sixtyfold!

Adding the previous thirtyfold, at this moment, his strength had surpassed his power before his last death by nearly a hundredfold!

Even when dispersed, any one of his clones was almost eight times stronger than before.

Yu Jiemo, who was eight times stronger than before, wasn't actually that strong.

His power could at most kill a Tier 2 Peak with one punch.

He still had no way against Tier 3.

As for defense, it had already reached a terrifying Three-Order Middle Stage.

At this point, they still needed another month to fully converge.

If they were truly allowed to continue like this until the end, and then merge together.

Their defense could directly reach Tier 4!

Even with weaker power, it would definitely reach Three-Order Middle Stage!

This was also because there weren't many Stone Men in the early stages.

If it truly reached the point where Stone Men were everywhere.

Their strength after devouring them all would be unimaginable.

Devouring a hundred million Stone Men, their mass could reach the level of a neutron star, which was Tier 4!

If the Stone Men were to gather and devour all Stone Men now, a Tier 4 could appear instantly.

And if they wanted to break through to Tier 5, the number would have to reach at least eighty billion!

This was the minimum standard.

Because their breakthrough method was different from other Transcendent paths or technological paths.

Transcendent ones needed to comprehend a Great Dao and then follow it all the way to Tier 4.

The technological path was even more troublesome.

They were exactly opposite to Transcendent.

Transcendent comprehended the Great Dao and then used the Great Dao to touch space.

The technological path, however, needed to touch space first, and then rely on space to create their own Tier 4 technology, reaching Tier 4.

Civilizations that had touched space but still didn't have their own Tier 4 technological products were called pseudo-Tier 4.

Theoretically, they were still Tier 3, because they didn't have Tier 4 technology or weapons.

Against a true Tier 4, they would surely die.

But, if compared to Tier 3, they could easily dominate Tier 3 civilizations, making them cry for their mothers.

A technological civilization that had touched space, fighting against someone who still relied on their physical body for flight, could just sit at home and fire two shots into the sky to take the opponent out directly.

Another reason for the existence of the pseudo-Tier 4 level was that many civilizations lacked potential, and Tier 3 Peak was already their limit.

But they stubbornly ground it out over time, touching some rules of space, and then became pseudo-Tier 4.

However, Tier 4 technology was an unattainable existence for them.

And the Stone Men, however, took another path.

Their method of breakthrough, described in four simple words, was: Prove the Dao with Strength!


#186 The war begins!

Chapter 186 The war begins!

They don't need to comprehend any rules, nor do they need to research any technology.

Or, one could say, they themselves are a rule, and they themselves are a technology!

They only need to continuously eat and accumulate mass, and once their mass reaches that of a neutron star, they can become Fourth Order.

Then, they continue to accumulate mass and grow stronger, until the mass of the neutron star explodes, transforming into a black hole!

At this moment, they will become Fifth Order!

The power span of the Third Order is very large.

Early Third Order can only travel through the universe.

By mid-stage, they can already perform a'skin-changing' operation on a planet's surface.

With a little more time, they can even destroy a star.

By late-stage, they can directly destroy a star in one go!

At peak power, like Da Boluo, they can even extinguish a star!

He can explode a star, but he is helpless against a neutron star, which is formed from a star collapsing and compressing.

It's like an original house being compressed into something the size of a marble.

You can smash and destroy the house, but against this marble, you are completely helpless.

This is a neutron star.

It has no rules, no technology; it only possesses extreme mass and weight.

Destroying a neutron star, even for a Fourth Order being, is not that simple.

The more compressed it is, and the higher its mass, the more difficult it is to destroy.

It is true that both Transcendent and Technological civilizations have mastered rules by the Fourth Order.

And it is true that rules are very powerful.

However, the current situation is like this: rules are a weapon, a knife, making it as easy to cut people as to cut melons and vegetables.

And then you have a diamond placed in front of you.

This is the path for the Stone Men to grow stronger.

The number of Stone Men has now reached 160 million.

If he truly wanted to, he could indeed create a Fourth Order being!

However, the Stone Men are scattered far and wide, making it impossible to gather them in a short period of time.

Forget about gathering all the Stone Men together.

Right now, it's already difficult for Yu Jiemo to survive and merge before Da Boluo and the others find him.

Currently, on the surface, the Demon Race holds all the advantages.

But to say that the Stone Men are at a disadvantage is not entirely true, because the collective consciousness of the Stone Men possesses peak Third Order strength.

This peak Third Order is not an ordinary peak Third Order, but one similar to Da Boluo's peak Third Order back then.

One could even say it's a pseudo-Fourth Order without exaggeration.

It's just that their mass hasn't truly reached Fourth Order; as long as the mass of one Stone Men reaches Fourth Order, he can directly transform into a Fourth Order Will.

If a fight truly breaks out, it's hard to say who would win or lose.

Su Mo originally intended to stop them.

But after thinking about it, Su Mo decided to let them fight first.

Because this battle is bound to happen.

Only after they have fought and felt the pressure from the opponent will they have enough motivation.

As long as he makes his move at the end, stopping them before too much loss is incurred, it will be fine.

This way, it can both stimulate their motivation to grow stronger quickly.

And it can also kick some inferior Demon Race members out of the team, reducing resource consumption.

Only by experiencing the flames of war will they grow and strengthen rapidly.

Just like a man, only by experiencing hardships will he become more resolute; of course, it's also possible he might give up or compromise.

Therefore, Su Mo does not intend to intervene for now.

In fact, what Su Mo doesn't know is that the Highest Will of the Stone Men thinks more than Su Mo imagines.

And he understands the Stone Men better than Su Mo.

He has always known that as long as they keep devouring, they can keep getting stronger and breaking through.

If they eventually devour this entire universe, they might even be able to break through this universe.

But he doesn't dare!

Yes, he just doesn't dare!

He is a will without emotion, the Highest Will of the Stone Men.

Everything the Stone Men experience and see, he knows.

All the ores, plants, animals, Demon Race, everything, he knows!

He even knows the ancient knowledge, texts, and languages.

He knows that many of those messages are false.

But one thing, however, deeply horrified him.

Breaking through to Fourth Order means certain death!

It is precisely because of this reason that he has never dared to break through.

Even his mental will has always been suppressed to the peak Third Order level.

He dared not even use the Stone Men fusion.

If it weren't for Yu Jiemo's persuasion at the end, informing him that ordinary Stone Men simply couldn't face those Demon Race members.

And that continuing like this would even lead to the risk of clan extinction, he wouldn't have used this method.

Otherwise, he could have gathered all the Stone Men together much earlier, then collectively devoured a place, constantly reacting.

When their number surpassed 160 million, they would all fuse, and he would directly break through to Fourth Order on the spot.

He would immediately become the most powerful existence on this planet.

At that time, he could eat however he wanted, and no one would be his opponent at all.

Wouldn't this be much better than the current situation?

With a Fourth Order Stone Men present, there would be no more worries afterwards.

However, that curse of death upon breakthrough prevented him from doing so.

He wasn't afraid, but rather, it was for the development of his race.

Or, after his calculations, the best choice was to continue developing like this, devouring this world, until the very last moment, then collectively fuse and try to resist, or rather, survive.

The thoughts of a god are unknown to anyone.

At this moment, a large number of Demon Race members had already begun to encircle and suppress the Stone Men.

The Wild Boar King, a thousand meters long, led eight million wild boars in a massive formation stretching tens of thousands of kilometers.

Every step taken caused the earth to tremble violently.

Dust billowed, and the terrifying dust was so thick that even three wild boars standing apart couldn't see each other.

They just trampled forward.

The weakest of these wild boars were First Order.

As they ran forward, any ordinary Stone Men they encountered on the way didn't even require them to stop and attack; after one round of trampling, the Stone Men had already turned into a pile of stone powder.

If they weren't dead, larger elephants behind them, or Demon Race members skilled in energy attacks, would deliver another blow to kill them.

If they still weren't dead, there were also a large number of Mosquito in the sky...

They just kept running forward like that.

Everything in their path would be trampled into dust before them.

The Wild Boar King, who had recently been soundly beaten by Wenhe, was now seething with resentment.

He wanted to be the first to find those so-called Ancestral Witches, and then be the first to kill him!

He firmly believed that eight million wild boar warriors were invincible!

Because they possessed tough skin, sharp tusks, terrifying weight, and powerful stamina!

They were the strongest warriors; no matter what, it would be trampled into powder by their hooves!

Finally, after five days of rushing and trampling countless Stone Men to death, he encountered six strange Stone Men.


#187 Rivers of blood!

Just the large swarm of Mosquitoes flying in the sky had already obscured it.

This commotion was as if they were afraid others wouldn't know what was happening here.

With this phenomenon, with this commotion, if Yu Jiemo still didn't know what was happening, he wouldn't need to live anymore.

Recently, a large number of Stone Men had died in various directions.

#188 One man can fight against millions of troops!

Chapter 188 One man alone against a million troops!

With his current strength, these demon race members couldn't cause him any harm at all.

His immense mass granted him terrifying power.

A five-hundred-meter peak-first-tier Wild Boar was lifted with one hand by Yu Jiemo, who was only ten meters tall, looking as if an Ant had lifted an elephant.

It was even less strenuous than when he had just thrown those Stone Men.

Of course, calling it cotton might be an exaggeration, after all, no matter what, this was a peak-first-tier Great Demon.
