Facing ordinary weeds, some could be instantly killed, as for using rattan to hit a large tree, if the force is small, the tree will be wounded.

If the force is large, the tree will still be wounded, and more severely, but the rattan will also break.

A peak-first-tier Wild Boar was reduced to a pulp after being swung around just once by him.

Although many attacks also landed on him.

However, physical attacks were completely ineffective; not only ineffective, but the reflected damage could even numb the opponent's limbs for a period of time.

Those who hit with great force often suffered fractures and breaks.

His body was as if fixed to the earth; those attacks couldn't even make him budge!

Among them, a few energy attacks also struck him.

But, because there were too many demon beasts around him, there weren't many such attacks.

Yet, even those energy attacks that landed on him were like this.

Terrifying energy blasts struck his body, instantly exploding like splashes of water, not only causing no harm to Yu Jiemo, but the exploding energy instead injured the surrounding demon race members.

At this moment, he was an invincible presence on this battlefield.

Even if he faced a million demon race members alone, it would still be the same!

Truly, one man holding back a million troops!

However, a million demon race members, the number was truly too vast.

Those on this side hadn't even been fully killed yet, and the demon race members from behind had already charged forward again.

The demon race was like a tide, and Yu Jiemo was like an isolated island in the sea.

Seemingly isolated and helpless, about to be submerged by the seawater at any moment.

But, he remained steadfast, because no one could shake him!

At this moment, he vividly portrayed a word: invincible!

Or rather, he was invincibility itself!

On the ground, there was nothing but the pulped flesh of trampled demon race corpses.

But, he was still continuously swinging the demon race corpse in his hand.

During this time, the five Stone Men returned once and were launched out again by him as cannonballs.

At this moment, the bloody mud had completely submerged Yu Jiemo.

Revealing once more that Stone Men who struck fear into countless demon race members!

His stone body was now dark red!

He stood in the center of this large pit!

#189 The king descended from the sky, furious and ferocious!

Chapter 189 The king descends from the sky, angry and ferocious!

He endured the pain and anger of a broken leg, as well as the killing desire infected by the Calamity Qi, feeling utterly horrified.

It was like the difference between a badminton racket hitting a shuttlecock and hitting a bowling ball.

#193 Why did the Demon King rebel?

Chapter 193 Why did the Demon King rebel?

Therefore, the best method now was to take advantage of their current superiority and eliminate all the Stone Men in one fell swoop.

The Stone Men was like a rabbit.

Normally, it looked docile and gentle, but when provoked, it would bite.

Just like the current situation.

Although they currently held the advantage and truly had a chance to completely destroy the Stone Men.

When the birds are gone, the good bow is put away; when the cunning rabbit dies, the hounds are cooked...

Raising enemies to maintain one's own importance!

The first option was to always keep the Stone Men under a harmless number.

To fill it with a large number of demon races, constantly creating the appearance of a stalemate.

Integrating all spirits and creating the Demon Race Heavenly Court; isn't the Stone Men also one of the myriad spirits?

So, if he could persuade the Stone Men to also become a demon.

Thinking of this, Wolong fell silent for a moment.

If the Stone Men were integrated into the demon race, would that count as completing the mission?

Would this lead to the outcome of the cunning rabbit dying?

For a moment, his heart was filled with mixed emotions.

The path was ahead, and there were many of them.

But, at the end of each path, what he saw were abysses.

Moreover, some paths were long, some were short, but undoubtedly, the outcome was the same.

Just as the end of life is death.

However, a Three-Tier has an eight-thousand-year lifespan, and from Da Boluo, he learned that a Four-Tier can live for thirty thousand years.

These thirty thousand years are only the limit of the soul, not the limit of the physical body.

Of course, if one mainly cultivates the soul, one can live even longer.

As for Wang Jianzhi, who cultivates both body and soul, even Da Boluo doesn't know how long he can live.

If he were truly just an ordinary person, without this opportunity, he might not strive for it.

But now, five million is right in front of you; can you really resist not picking it up?

No, I misspoke. Five million is not even worth mentioning compared to ascending the throne and living for ten thousand years.

How about I also learn from Xu Shilun and try to sneak through?

As this thought just burst forth, he felt a terrifying chill shoot straight from the soles of his feet to the top of his head.

Dozens of meters of cold sweat densely covered his entire body.

"Wolong, Wolong, what's wrong with you?"

Wenhe looked at Wolong and asked cautiously.

Wolong shook his head, thinking to himself that he might have raised the demon race's banner a bit too early.

If the Stone Men was not stopped, then the strength of their twelve, no, it should be eleven Divine Ancestors after fusion, would be unrivaled.

He wanted to discuss this matter with Wenhe, Da Boluo, and the others.

If there are two, it might reach Three-Order Middle Stage.

Three, no...

At most, only two Stone Men can fuse.

The strength of two fused Stone Men is still within a controllable range.

As long as we keep suppressing the opponent, or maintain equal strength on the surface, that is the best.

At that time, we won't be able to kill this big Stone Men, but we can eliminate other small Stone Men and control their numbers.

This way, it won't be able to rapidly grow stronger, only steadily improve, and we can continue to suppress it with our numerical advantage.

This is the best choice!

It is also the way to live the longest!

Moreover, if that person is Phoenix Fledgling, or a smart person, he believes that the other party will guess his approach.

Although this still carried some risks, it was the only option.

Thinking of this, he secretly made up his mind, intending to let two Divine Ancestors fuse.

#195 The strength of a third-level stone man!

Chapter 195 The strength of the third-level stone man!

Who could have done such a thing? This is a question worth pondering.

According to the information he and his livestreaming companions received, only those at the Fourth Tier could access space.

Ren Chaofan, who had just broken through to the Fourth Tier, was not particularly proficient in controlling space.

Essentially, he could only traverse space himself, achieving the effect of spatial teleportation.

It was like a technological civilization that had just reached the Fourth Tier and developed the ability for spatial teleportation, but the early experiments carried significant risks.

They needed to gradually debug it, make the machine safe and harmless, then military-grade, and finally civilian-grade.

Therefore, this process, even at its fastest, would take hundreds of years and tens, or even millions, of experiments.

The same applied to the Transcendent, perhaps a bit faster, but they had limitations.

Once a Fourth Tier technology successfully developed something, it could be mass-produced on an assembly line.

However, a Fourth Tier Transcendent was an individual, and their individual identity was their greatest limitation.

Are there Fourth Tiers in this world?

Yes, there definitely are, and there are four, no, now it should be three.

Three Fourth Tiers, but all of them are incomplete and absolutely cannot recover their peak strength in a short time.

According to what was said before, even if they recovered their peak strength, how long had it been since they broke through to the Fourth Tier?

It was already quite good that he could spatially teleport himself.

Wanting to enable others to spatially teleport was not a simple matter.

And then, he suddenly, inexplicably, spatially teleported and converged with them.

Who did this?

A terrifying thought suddenly popped into his mind.

Then he shook his head vigorously; some things were better left unsaid, as speaking them could very likely lead to death.

But what was the other party's purpose in doing this?

Yu Jiemo pondered endlessly.

At the same time, his companions in the livestream were doing the same.

Given the current situation, let alone intelligent people, even a fool would have roughly guessed what had happened.

Of course, fools wouldn't come to watch this livestream; they would probably find it more interesting to lie on the ground and tease Ants, or simply stare blankly.

In the livestream, a long silence fell due to this miracle.

After all, most of them came to the livestream to watch the excitement, experience the scenery and time of another world, and earn money.

Speaking carelessly in the current situation, what would happen next was a somewhat horrifying question.

Yu Jiemo silently watched his eleven clones.

A moment later, they began to devour each other.

No matter what the purpose of that individual was, since he brought these clones together, his goal must have been to make them devour each other and become stronger.

Although his original goal was precisely this, it was originally necessary.

But for some reason, after these clones suddenly converged, he felt a sense of resistance in his heart.

However, inner resistance is sometimes the most useless thing.

It's like working overtime; some things, you can't just not do them because you don't want to.

Especially in the current situation, where one's life and death could be controlled by a single thought from someone else.

He had no doubt that the one who could bring his clones together could also easily swat him to death.

If he didn't obey...

In a trance, he saw a path appear beneath his feet.

The end of the path was a stretch of darkness; he could not see the future, and it seemed like a gaping maw of an abyss, where he would surely die if he walked forward.

But if he didn't walk, a giant abyss maw was slowly forming beneath his feet, and if he remained, he would be swallowed by this maw at any moment.

Now, he could choose: to walk or not to walk.

Walking would prolong his life, but for how long, he did not know.

But not walking meant immediate death.

He silently shook his head. Since the path was already laid out, now, whether he wanted to or not, he had to walk.

Because there was only this one path before him.

Although Yu Jiemo had always desired freedom and a carefree life.

He had indeed lived that way before.

But that didn't mean he wasn't afraid of death.

If he weren't afraid of death, he wouldn't have run when he first discovered Xu Shilun's dungeon.

When Wolong Wenhe began cultivating and growing stronger, and he felt a crisis, he wouldn't have run to Wang Jianzhi's dungeon to cultivate either.

As a Stone Men's body was devoured, his strength rapidly increased.

Finally, three hours later, he had devoured all of his clones.

The moment the eleven clones completely converged, his soul also completely merged at that instant.

He had returned to his peak state.

Or rather, he had long surpassed his peak by an unknown number of times.

At this moment, merely by standing there, the surrounding ground began to crack and collapse continuously.

Even the surrounding air was slightly distorted by the terrifying mass around him.

This power, this phenomenon.

At this moment, Yu Jiemo felt as if he could punch Da Boluo to death!

With a thought, a repulsive force emanated from his body, repelling and pulling against the gravitational force of the world. Finally, his terrifying body, weighing millions of tons, wobbled into the air.

And his wobbling ascent and movements caused the land within a radius of thousands of miles to instantly collapse and sink dozens of meters.

Because he had been standing in one spot before, the force was concentrated, so even if it collapsed, it was only in one place, at most spreading a few kilometers.

And it appeared in the form of a crater.

But when he used repulsion to lift his body, his mass was directly spread over a range of thousands of miles around him.

Only such a large range of gravity and mass could make his body fly.

And with just one movement of his ascent, the surrounding animals, plants, and demonic beasts were all crushed into a pulp under this terrifying pressure.

Therefore, saying that the Stone Men is the destroyer of this world is absolutely true.

Before, when they ate, they were devouring the world.

But now, at Yu Jiemo's level, merely taking a step would cause massive disappointment to the flora and fauna of this world.

If all Stone Men reached this level of strength, then they would only need to fly around the world once, and life on the surface would be extinct.

And if they reached the Fourth Tier, it would be even more terrifying.

They wouldn't even need to hide; their inherent terrifying mass would pull everything around them.

Although they hadn't transformed into black holes, they still possessed their own gravity, and their gravity would definitely be stronger than the normal gravity in this world.

At that time, as long as they stood still, everything around them would be rapidly devoured and integrated into their bodies, until this entire world was completely consumed.

This is the Stone Men!

#196 A truly old coin!

Chapter 196 Real old coins!

Then, it will proceed to other universes to continue devouring!

Infinite devouring!

The best and simplest way to deal with the Stone Men is to eliminate it the moment it first appears!

Because this is a race more terrifying than the Zerg!

Actually, that’s not quite right, because from what Su Mo saw, once the Stone Men transforms into a black hole, it has reached its peak.

After that, if it wants to become stronger, it must change, from devouring matter to devouring rules.

This is an invincible race that can go straight to the Sixth Tier.

Even Su Mo, watching this scene, felt a bit of pity for them.

Born at the wrong time!

If it weren't for the fact that his current epoch required him to destroy the world...

He really didn't want to destroy the Stone Men.

Because this is the best and strongest race he has encountered since he began creating.

Their potential goes without saying.

Even if they didn't change in the later stages and still chose to devour matter, they would eventually be able to devour entire universes in one gulp.

If such things were thrown into other universes, they would be a world-destroying level of danger.

But it's a pity. For races or civilizations not created by him, or unrelated to him, even if they are destroyed, he will not gain Authority.

He can only gain Authority from life in the Little Universe, and from his own race, the Earthlings!

Otherwise, if he threw these Stone Men out, he could just sit at home and continuously gain Authority.

Of course, if he changed his approach, it wouldn't be impossible. For example, creating a parasitic civilization, throwing them into other universes, parasitizing and corrupting them all, and then destroying them, could also grant Authority.

But what he lacks least right now is Authority.

His current Authority is already somewhat excessive.

If he continues to increase his Authority, it would be tantamount to seeking death.

The previous host only allowed the God of Creation to increase strength, not Authority.

But he is now somewhat the opposite.

To become stronger, one must walk on two legs.

Otherwise, one will stumble sooner or later.

Thinking of this, he felt a bit envious of the existence in that high-dimensional world.

It's true that some people are born to toil, while others are born in Rome.

The Authority he painstakingly gained by cultivating civilizations and destroying worlds, others gain directly at the Ninth Tier just by being born in a high dimension.

Doesn't that make you angry?

Although his Authority increase didn't seem to take much time or be particularly difficult.

Before, he just lay on a recliner every day, basking in the sun, playing with his phone, watching young ladies dance, and then conveniently destroying a world, and that was it.

But that was just him.

Others have to ascend repeatedly to increase their Authority. After ascending, if their Authority isn't enough, they make up for it with strength.

Ascending tier by tier, cutting off their cultivation, and re-cultivating. The lucky ones might have something like an ascension platform to cleanse their bodies, which actually converts their strength into Authority.

Normally, a peak powerhouse at the Ninth Tier, ascending a small dimension, for example from 3.1 to 3.2, would have to cut off all their cultivation. But if they use something like an Ascension Platform, they would only lose a large portion.

As for not cutting off cultivation, that's also possible.

But in a high dimension, his rules would be unusable.

Unable to use rules, his entire being would only be at the peak of the Third Tier.

This is only a difference of 0.1 dimensions. If the difference were greater, his physical body, strength, and everything else would be weakened. After a few more dimensions, he really wouldn't even be able to defeat an ordinary person.

This is the difference in dimensions.

But under normal circumstances, high-dimensional beings do not descend, just as people from the Immortal Realm do not casually descend to the mortal world.

Because the rules below would affect them.

Even if someone did descend, the Highest Will, the dimension manager, would send someone down to deal with it.

Just like Su Mo's situation, if he wasn't discovered or reported, nothing would happen.

But if he was reported, then when someone from above came, he would definitely not be able to escape.

Su Mo glanced at Yu Jiemo again. He was also an unlucky fellow. Don't look at how powerful he is now; in reality, he has always been under the Highest Will's calculations.

According to normal circumstances, when Wenhe shattered his soul, he should have died.

But the Highest Will used its power to save Yu Jiemo.

Afterward, Yu Jiemo always thought he was planning for himself and seeking benefits for himself, but in fact, he was just a shield pushed out by the Highest Will, or rather, a stepping stone it threw out.

The will of the Stone Men has never been numerous; they have always had only one.

Every time the Stone Men reproduces, what increases is not only their number but also the quality of the Highest Will.

The more Stone Men there are, the stronger the Highest Will becomes.

It is actually directly proportional to the quality and quantity of the Stone Men.

Moreover, when the Stone Men dies, its quality does not decrease.

After that, it continues to grow stronger until the Fourth Tier appears, and the Fifth Tier appears!

Devouring universes, becoming Sixth Tier.

Its will can also become Sixth Tier!

As mentioned before, the reason it was stuck at the peak of the Third Tier was not because it couldn't break through, but because it didn't dare to break through.

It can be said that the Stone Men's Highest Will is only one, and in the later stage, after the Stone Men merges, the physical body is also only one.

From the very beginning, it had already made preparations.

The reason Yu Jiemo cannot integrate into the Stone Men's collective consciousness is also very simple: because he does not belong to the Stone Men, nor is he an individual split from the Highest Will.

But as for why he was later accepted by the Stone Men's Highest Will, the specific reason is a bit difficult to say.

In any case, in the end, the Stone Men's will will definitely be only one, and what will become of Yu Jiemo, this anomaly, or what his end will be, is hard to say.

As he thought this through, Su Mo suddenly found it interesting again.

Before, he had always focused on those Travelers, considering them Old Coins with a lot of bad ideas.

Because the previous few epochs were basically like this, with one Traveler after another dominating, leading civilizations and ethnic groups to develop and grow.

Of course, there were also powerful natives, such as Otto V and Zhang Jiao, who counted.

But compared to the Highest Will, they were far inferior.

This is an Old Coin even more reclusive than Xu Shilun.

Being reclusive indicates intelligence, and intelligence leads to rapid development and strength.

Moreover, intelligent people often don't live long.

With so many advantages gathered in one being, how could Su Mo not like it?

The Highest Will did not know that it, which had been hiding, had already been targeted by the great God of Creation.

Meanwhile, Yu Jiemo, guided by the live stream audience and the Highest Will's mini-map, had already headed to the nearest Liu Dajun dungeon.


#197 God's grace, the Great Flood!

Chapter 197 God’s Grace, the Great Flood!

Soon, he reacted.

Previously, after his creations, he would let them develop freely and generally wouldn't interfere with them.

Of course, this 'generally' referred to situations where no problems occurred and his own interests were not affected.

For example, Liu Dajun's previous plan to smuggle himself out, and the impact Da Boluo had on him after his breakthrough, were such cases.

The only time a problem occurred was with Xu Shilun; that guy, without any prior warning, suddenly caused an apocalypse while he just went out for a cigarette.

These were things that had already happened, and Su Mo didn't want to bring them up again, otherwise, every time he thought about it, he'd want to resurrect that dog Xu Shilun and kill him again.

What he wanted now was for them to grow rapidly, become stronger, nurture them, nurture this world, and these civilizations; this was what a God of Creation should do.

Logically speaking, this was his main duty!

Otherwise, he would have the title of God of Creation, but besides creation, everything he did was what an evil god would do, which was somewhat unreasonable.

As for why he hadn't thought of it before, it was because he had been an evil god for too long, and his mind couldn't react immediately.

He had always thought that only by giving them pressure would they have motivation.

But, this was not what a God of Creation should do.

As for how a God of Creation should act…

Su Mo took out his previous host's notebook and quickly flipped to the first page.

"The Duty of a God of Creation."

"God of Creation Growth Path."

"First Creation."

"Recording Civilization and the Diversity of Life."

"…"

"Cultivation of Civilization."

Finally, after continuously looking through two pages of the table of contents, he found what he was looking for.

Immediately, he turned to the page he wanted to read.

"A God of Creation possesses the great power of creation; what it should do is not merely create life and bestow wisdom, but like a mother, accompany them, nurture them, and allow them to grow robustly."

"Civilization initially needs wisdom; it needs to cultivate their wisdom and guide them to use tools…"

Su Mo skipped this page directly; the Travelers had already done these things for him.

After flipping through several pages, he finally found what he was looking for.

"After civilization develops to a certain point, it will enter a period of stagnation; at this stage, it can be given some appropriate pressure, or some enemies can be created."

Su Mo nodded in agreement upon seeing this record.

As expected of an existence that had elevated the God of Creation level to the ninth rank, it certainly had some insights into cultivating civilization.

However, if that's the case, then what I've done seems to be the same as him, although the process is a bit off, the purpose is a bit off, and the result is also off, but that doesn't hinder me from being a good God of Creation.

"Food is the most basic guarantee for primitive life; if famine occurs, it is very likely to lead to the destruction of civilization, so this must be dealt with promptly."

"Companions are also an important factor in the development of civilization; not only can they…"

Su Mo silently closed the record when he read this.

At this moment, he already had an idea in his mind.

So, food, companions, pressure, enemies—these things could make civilization develop rapidly.

This reminded him of the only civilization, or rather, the only life he had ever nurtured: Da Boluo!

He gave them pressure, the Mosquitoes.

Then came the enemies, the Ants.

Finally, the Ants and they made peace and became companions.

And he, as the God of Creation, to signify their reconciliation, bestowed upon them countless Mosquitoes as food.

Then their civilization developed rapidly!

So, this was the cultivation of civilization!

He had unknowingly reached this point.

Could it be that he was the legendary sweet potato!!!

He looked down at the situation below and Su Mo began to think about what he needed to do.

The Stone Men needed food, which was simple; just throw in a batch of meteor showers every now and then.

Wait, this, why does this scene feel a bit familiar?

Did I do something like this before?

Su Mo was a little confused and couldn't recall for a moment, so he stopped thinking about it.

As for the Demon Race, they needed not only food but also companions.

Water is the source of life.

At that time, he would directly draw a wave of water from the real world.

There were also so many microorganisms in it; he was afraid that this world would be infected by demonic energy, and over time, they would also become Demon Race.

First stones, then water; this scene was becoming more and more familiar.

Su Mo shook his head, shaking off these chaotic images, and began to plan how to operate.

If the Stone Men doubled every five years, then he would feed them once every ten years.

As for the Demon Race, he would stagger it with them, otherwise, if it was at the same time, it would feel a bit off.

So, he would stagger it by five years; he would start with a wave for the Demon Race now.

Thinking of this, Su Mo looked out through the small world.

He casually found a small ditch near his home and started drawing water.

The reason he chose a small ditch was because he wanted more microorganisms.

Microorganisms were microorganisms outside, but inside, they were nearly ten meters in size.

After transforming into demons, their size could expand infinitely.

At this time, the various armies of the Demon Race were still rapidly killing Stone Men.

They also did not know the favor bestowed upon them by the great God of Creation.

Otherwise, their expressions might not have been so pleasant.

As Su Mo's mind moved, a terrifying great flood poured down.

If it weren't for Spider Sky Emperor's web, which covered the heavens and earth and bore the first and strongest impact, who knows how many lives would have perished from this flood.

Their deaths wouldn't be from drowning, but from being crushed!

That's right, crushed by water!

In the sky, a rumbling sound came, and Da Boluo immediately looked up.

The dark tribulation energy could not block his sight.

In just a moment, Da Boluo's expression changed drastically.

Even though the terrifying flood had been largely blocked by the spider web, it was still enormous!

In the sky, dark clouds and tribulation energy dispersed under this terrifying impact.

Microorganisms, each dozens of meters in size, floated in the flood.

Several small fish, hundreds of meters long, were also tossed about by this flood, and after landing, they nearly died.

Among them, a small Loach, which no one in the real world would even bother to look at, transformed directly into a terrifying giant beast tens of thousands of meters long in this world.

Spider Sky Emperor originally wanted to catch this Loach, but it was too slippery, and furthermore, the webs had lost their stickiness under the impact of the flood, so ultimately, it allowed this large creature to escape downwards.

After all, a Third-Tier is a God of Civilization; he can already solve and create all natural disasters in this universe.

For example, this one is unnatural.

But even so, it's not that they have no way to solve it.

The demon beast army was scattered under this terrifying flood.

At this moment, there was no safe place in this world.

Four Dark Tiga created a protective shield, encompassing Da Boluo and the others.

The rest of the Dark Tiga also flew into the high sky to disperse the water columns; otherwise, with the terrifying impact of the water columns, ordinary demon beasts simply could not withstand it.

In just twenty minutes, the rainwater in some low-lying areas had already reached over three hundred meters.

However, it was also fortunate that the demon beasts in this world, once they became extraordinary, were all incredibly massive.

Even if they hadn't reached First-Tier, they could reach seventy to eighty meters.

After breaking through to First-Tier, one or two hundred meters was considered relatively small.

And demon beasts also have intelligence; they are not fools, and they will run home when it rains.

At this moment, inside the giant protective shield, Wolong, Wenhe, Da Boluo, Two Mosquitoes, and Ashura were all silent.

They looked up at the sky, their eyes somewhat lost, their hearts filled with shock, fear, dread, and various emotions mixed together.


#199 Aquarium vs. Bacteria!

Chapter 199 Aquatic creatures vs. bacteria!

Even the fastest flood formed by torrential rain would take at least three days to become a truly destructive and lethal flood.

Even in such a situation, if precautions were taken in advance, the most that would be lost would be some property, as people can flee.

In the small world, even the smallest demon race had a massive body size of at least twenty meters.

Ordinary small floods would not harm them.

But this was no ordinary flood.

Nor was it accumulated from normal rainfall.

Instead, it was a world-ending great flood.

The mere impact of the rainwater falling from the sky was enough to instantly kill a small demon.

Terrifying floods gathered, sweeping away rocks and vegetation, forming terrifying mudslides, and even the large-bodied demon race swayed precariously under this terrifying impact.

In the sky, the torrential rain, or rather, water columns, continued to fall as if the sky itself was collapsing.

Wolong Wenhe finally calmed his inner emotions at this time.

They clearly knew that this was no ordinary heavy rain.

They also clearly understood that this was a divine punishment, a heavenly punishment!

But now was not the time to consider these things.

Because, no matter what was in the sky.

What they needed to do now was to save the other weaker demon races.

They were Demon Emperors; saving the demon race, leading them to strength, and killing the Stone Men were their duties.

Perhaps they didn't care about these duties, but if they allowed a large number of demon races to die like this, then afterward, they would have no demons to use.

As people who had transmigrated from modern times, they clearly understood a principle: water can float a boat or overturn it.

Saving the demon race could not only minimize their losses but also win them more public support.

The current thirty thousand Tiga could not possibly protect the billions of demon races in the world.

Given this situation, if they didn't deal with it, the demon race would soon die, and even if they didn't die, he, as an inactive Demon Emperor, would definitely lose public support.

If they were to still deal with the Stone Men now, he didn't even know what the consequences would be.

And the most important point was that the flood posed a great threat to the demon race but not to the Stone Men.

In such a situation, they were at a disadvantage.

Instead of continuing to kill the Stone Men now, it was better to first deal with the demon race's affairs.

As the saying goes, to pacify the external, one must first pacify the internal.

As for the Stone Men, Wolong and his group didn't ignore it; instead, they sent the strongest Bolo to find the other eleven Ancestral Shamans and then kill them one by one.

Due to the sudden appearance of the world-ending great flood in the sky, the demon race's widespread scouts across the land were also swept away by this flood.

So they still didn't know at this time that the Eleven Ancestral Shamans had converged and gained the ability to fly.

The flood lasted for two days before ending.

Or rather, the giant water column in the sky disappeared, but the torrential rain still hadn't stopped.

The torrential rain, if placed before, would have been considered the maximum rainfall, but compared to the terrifying scene of the sky collapsing earlier, it could practically be said to be a minor issue in comparison.

Although the heavy rain had stopped, the flood was at its peak at this time, or rather, with the torrential rain in the sky, the flood was still getting larger.

In this natural disaster, countless demon races died; even with the shelters built by Dark Tiga and the collective help of countless large-bodied great demons, the number of deceased demon races still reached hundreds of millions.

This number is not exaggerated; first, under this flood, the fastest to die were the mammalian demon races that had just emerged, followed by the immobile plant race, and then the inherently weak lower-tier demon races.

For example, rabbits, mice, and similar creatures, which are large in number and like to live in burrows, died the fastest and were the weakest.

At this time, large areas of the world had been swept away by the flood, and the entire world seemed to be enveloped in a vast ocean.

Or rather, this was a world of water!

On one mountain peak after another, groups of demon races stood, crying and calling for help, hoping that the Demon Emperor would send people to save them.

The mountain peaks, which were already barely above the water, continuously shrunk under the impact of the flood, with massive rocks and soil being mercilessly swept away by the floodwaters.

In the water, a large group of demon races were fighting desperately.

They were all demon races who had previously submitted to the Demon Emperor and recognized him.

The aquatic demon races were the last to gain sentience because there were no dungeons in the water.

They were only able to gain sentience after Xu Shilun's death, finally gaining wisdom and absorbing the calamity qi from the sky to begin cultivating.

Therefore, the strength of the aquatic race was the weakest.

So weak that Wenhe didn't even want to subdue them back then.

The main reason was that their battles were primarily on land or in the sky; who would fight in the water?

Moreover, the strength of these aquatic races was truly pitifully weak.

Other races on land, while not necessarily strong, at least had strong and weak individuals; even the weakest rabbit race, due to their vast numbers, had some who had already gained sentience before.

Even now, their strength is not weak at all, even reaching the peak of the first stage, ready to break through to the second stage at any time.

As for the aquatic race, they gained sentience when Xu Shilun died, and after gaining sentience, they only began cultivating after the demon race was established.

To date, it has been less than five years in total, and the strongest among them haven't even reached the first-stage transcendent level.

They have only just stepped into the door of transcendence, no, they can't even be said to have stepped into the door, but rather touched the threshold of transcendence.

This kind of thing, it was only because Wenhe thought their task was to unite all spirits, and since the aquatic race had already gained sentience, he included them incidentally.

As soon as the flood appeared, Wenhe had already ordered the aquatic race to save the demons.

At first, they were doing well.

Many demon beast cubs swept away by the flood were rescued by them.

But then, some other life forms appeared in the water.

They were not large, only a little over ten meters, looking about the same size as some demon beast cubs.

Their bodies were like spheres, covered in tentacles, and the tentacles were covered in thorns and various sticky fluids…

From the moment they appeared, they had been devouring and parasitizing everything around them.

Moreover, their bodies also carried potent venom.

Demon beast cubs, once entangled by them, would die in a short moment.


#200 Chapter 200

Chapter 200 Kill or keep?

While the aquatic race was rescuing the demons, they also had to fight against bacteria.

And the number of bacteria was incredibly vast.

As the saying goes, the Buddha said a scoop of water contains ten billion eight thousand insects.

Although this flood was not as much as a scoop of water.

However, the number of bacteria was still considerable.

Weak demon beasts and cubs died rapidly in the flood and under the bacteria's siege, their flesh and blood consumed.

It's a good thing that the demon race in this world is already transcendent; otherwise, if bacteria parasitized them, who knows how many demons would die.

At this moment, Marshal Tianpeng, with one leg broken, unleashed his thousand-zhang Dharma form, standing in the flood, firmly blocking a protective shield behind him that had already developed some cracks.

Because there were simply too many demons, the mere thirty thousand Dark Tiga couldn't cope.

After establishing one shelter, they would rush to the next location.

And this shelter was about to collapse.

The Wild Boar King stood in the flood, countless bacteria constantly devouring his flesh and blood at the site of his broken leg.

Tens of thousands of aquatic creatures guarded him, but facing the bacteria that swarmed in, attracted by the smell of blood, their numbers were still too few.

Marshal Tianpeng, the Wild Boar King, still stood in the flood, his body parting the waters.

At this moment, even his thousand-zhang Dharma form had half its body submerged.

Because all the surrounding soil and sand had been washed away by the flood.

His body was constantly sinking.

At this moment, he dared not move, because any slight movement would cause the flood to crash against the already precarious protective shield behind him.

【Thump~】

A colossal creature, also ten thousand zhang long, completely black, with four whiskers at its mouth, suddenly leaped out of the water.

Just a single leap caused water splashes to soar over the Wild Boar King's massive body and directly hit the dilapidated protective shield behind him.

【Boom!】

The water splashes collided with the protective shield, accompanied by a jarring crackling sound, and a large amount of yellow floodwater began to pour into the protective shield.

Marshal Tianpeng, the Wild Boar King, turned his head sharply, seeing faces filled with despair and eyes pleading.

Turning around, he wanted to save these demons.

But in the next moment, the previous colossal creature violently crashed into him.

【Boom~】

The Wild Boar King's massive body flew backward, crashing into the crumbling protective shield.

And this protective shield finally shattered completely with this impact.

Instantly, the flood swept away tens of thousands of demons, among them countless newly born demon beasts, their eyes still innocent, once again losing the life they had just gained…

"Howl~"

The Wild Boar King looked at the scene before him, his eyes bloodshot, and with a roar, he directly engaged in battle with the colossal creature.

The Wild Boar King, with an early second-stage cultivation and the divine ability of Heaven and Earth Dharma, possessed the cultivation of a mid-second-stage cultivator.

And his opponent was the 'King of Slime,' a common dish in the real world, capable of filling a bucket in two hours using lightning power during spring and summer!

Loach!

The Wild Boar King's entire body was covered in tough hide, capable of splitting mountains and rocks, and his sharp tusks could break through anything.

As for Loach, he was slippery, with abundant muscles, and supported by his massive size, his strength was no less than that of the Wild Boar King.

This was the advantage of size.

The Wild Boar King had this size because he used a divine ability, expanding his body tenfold to achieve this effect.

But Loach was naturally this big!

In the real world, Mosquitoes, spiders, Ants—among these creatures, only spiders are slightly larger, but there's still a gap compared to others.

As for the others, they aren't even as big as a tail.

So, it was normal for Loach to retain his size upon arriving in this world.

Moreover, this was a small Loach.

So small that it was probably just recently hatched.

【Boom!】

With a fierce collision, the Wild Boar King and Loach were both sent flying tens of thousands of meters.

Their strength was surprisingly evenly matched.

In fact, the Wild Boar King flew a little further because he had a broken leg.

At the wound on this leg, if one looked closely, countless bacteria could still be seen clinging to it, devouring and absorbing his flesh and blood.

Although some bacteria fell off during the recent collision, many more still remained attached.

Seeing that he couldn't defeat this big guy, Loach didn't hesitate, directly burrowing into the mud and fleeing.

The Wild Boar King's expression was incredibly grim; he could tell that this terrifying beast before him had not cultivated at all.

He might not even have awakened his intelligence.

Yet, such an unintelligent, uncultivated animal was fighting him to a standstill!

And this was with him in full power, even having activated his trump card, Heaven and Earth Dharma, but even so, it was still like this.

At this moment, he thought of those Stone Men who never cultivated.

He thought of a sentence from Wenhe Demon Emperor.

【Sometimes this world is just unfair; the finish line we strive for is merely someone else's starting point...】

He looked back at the protective shield behind him, which was completely submerged by the flood, then let out another roar, and then, using his remaining three legs, he stomped through the flood, leaping wildly forward.

The Demon Emperor was still commanding the overall situation, and those black guys were also busy; if this fellow continued to cause trouble like this, who knows how many demons would die.

Therefore, he had to kill him!

Locking onto the opponent's aura with his soul, no matter which direction the opponent ran, the Wild Boar King pursued relentlessly.

Whenever the distance closed, a collision would erupt between them.

Loach was thus constantly chased, with no opportunity to cause trouble.

A month later, the flood finally subsided somewhat.

And the other Demon Commanders, along with Wolong Wenhe and others, finally found some free time.

At this moment, due to bacterial infection, his remaining half-leg had completely turned black.

Coupled with the continuous pursuit of this Loach, the Wild Boar King had reached his limit.

Marshal Tianpeng, the Wild Boar King's eyes were bloodshot, and he was breathing heavily through his nose.

Ahead, a completely black head with four whiskers emerged, smiling mockingly at the Wild Boar King.

Yes, he smiled.

He had only been in this world for a month and a half, and he had already gained intelligence.

Although he hadn't yet transcended or learned to speak, he already possessed his own wisdom.

The Wild Boar King charged again, and a huge tail directly sent him flying.

His thousand-zhang body instantly shrunk to a thousand meters; in mid-air, he spat out a mouthful of blood, truly having reached his limit at this moment.

In the next moment, a gentle force caught him.

Accompanied by a series of rumbling sounds, the Dark Tiga sealed off the surroundings.

Wenhe stood in mid-air, looking at the large Loach and asking Wolong beside him:

"He has gained intelligence; kill him, or spare him?"
